    status: String(r[RAW_IDX.status] || ""), note: String(r[RAW_IDX.note] || ""), loggedAt: r[RAW_IDX.logged_at],
    checkpoints: {
      5:  { evalDate: r[RAW_IDX.eval_date_5bd],  evalClose: toFiniteNumberOrNaN_(r[RAW_IDX.eval_close_5bd]),  perf: parsePerfValue_(r[RAW_IDX.perf_5bd]),  winFlag: r[RAW_IDX.win_flag_5bd]  === true, reported: r[RAW_IDX.reported_5bd]  === true || String(r[RAW_IDX.reported_5bd]  || "").toUpperCase() === "TRUE" },
      10: { evalDate: r[RAW_IDX.eval_date_10bd], evalClose: toFiniteNumberOrNaN_(r[RAW_IDX.eval_close_10bd]), perf: parsePerfValue_(r[RAW_IDX.perf_10bd]), winFlag: r[RAW_IDX.win_flag_10bd] === true, reported: r[RAW_IDX.reported_10bd] === true || String(r[RAW_IDX.reported_10bd] || "").toUpperCase() === "TRUE" },
      20: { evalDate: r[RAW_IDX.eval_date_20bd], evalClose: toFiniteNumberOrNaN_(r[RAW_IDX.eval_close_20bd]), perf: parsePerfValue_(r[RAW_IDX.perf_20bd]), winFlag: r[RAW_IDX.win_flag_20bd] === true, reported: r[RAW_IDX.reported_20bd] === true || String(r[RAW_IDX.reported_20bd] || "").toUpperCase() === "TRUE" },
      40: { evalDate: r[RAW_IDX.eval_date_40bd], evalClose: toFiniteNumberOrNaN_(r[RAW_IDX.eval_close_40bd]), perf: parsePerfValue_(r[RAW_IDX.perf_40bd]), winFlag: r[RAW_IDX.win_flag_40bd] === true, reported: r[RAW_IDX.reported_40bd] === true || String(r[RAW_IDX.reported_40bd] || "").toUpperCase() === "TRUE" }
    }
  };
}

// =====================================
// 書式
// =====================================
function applyRawFormats_(sh, startRow, numRows) {
  if (numRows <= 0) return;
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.received_at + 1, numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd hh:mm:ss");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.signal_date + 1, numRows, 2).setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.entry_price + 1, numRows, 2).setNumberFormat("#,##0.00");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.eval_date_5bd + 1,  numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.eval_close_5bd + 1, numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("#,##0.00");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.perf_5bd + 1,       numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("0.0%");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.eval_date_10bd + 1,  numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.eval_close_10bd + 1, numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("#,##0.00");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.perf_10bd + 1,       numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("0.0%");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.eval_date_20bd + 1,  numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.eval_close_20bd + 1, numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("#,##0.00");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.perf_20bd + 1,       numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("0.0%");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.eval_date_40bd + 1,  numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.eval_close_40bd + 1, numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("#,##0.00");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.perf_40bd + 1,       numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("0.0%");
  sh.getRange(startRow, RAW_IDX.logged_at + 1, numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd hh:mm:ss");
}
function applyReportFormats_(sh, startRow, numRows) {
  if (numRows <= 0) return;
  sh.getRange(startRow, REPORT_IDX.generated_at + 1, numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd hh:mm:ss");
  sh.getRange(startRow, REPORT_IDX.report_week_start + 1, numRows, 2).setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd");
  sh.getRange(startRow, REPORT_IDX.win_rate + 1, numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("0.0%");
  sh.getRange(startRow, REPORT_IDX.avg_perf + 1, numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("0.0%");
}

// =====================================
// Date helpers
// =====================================
function parseIsoDateOrThrow_(value) { const d = new Date(value); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) throw new Error(`Invalid receivedAt: ${value}`); return d; }
function makeJstMidnightDate_(date) { const ymd = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(date), CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd"); return new Date(`${ymd}T00:00:00+09:00`); }
function addDaysJst_(date, days) { return new Date(date.getTime() + days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); }
function addBusinessDaysJst_(date, businessDays) {
  let d = makeJstMidnightDate_(date);
  let added = 0;
  const holidaySet = getMarketHolidaySet_();
  while (added < businessDays) { d = addDaysJst_(d, 1); if (!isMarketHolidayJst_(d, holidaySet)) added++; }
  return d;
}
function startOfWeekJst_(date) { const d = makeJstMidnightDate_(date); const weekday = Number(Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "u")); return addDaysJst_(d, -(weekday - 1)); }
function isWeekendJst_(date) { const n = Number(Utilities.formatDate(date, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "u")); return n === 6 || n === 7; }
function formatYmd_(date) { return Utilities.formatDate(date, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd"); }
function formatYmdSlash_(date) { return Utilities.formatDate(date, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd"); }
function isDateBetweenInclusive_(date, start, end) { if (!(date instanceof Date) || !(start instanceof Date) || !(end instanceof Date)) return false; const k = formatYmd_(date); return k >= formatYmd_(start) && k <= formatYmd_(end); }
function parseYmdFlexible_(s) {
  const text = String(s || "").trim();
  if (!text) return null;
  const m = text.match(/^(\d{4})[\/\-](\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{1,2})$/);
  if (!m) return null;
  const y = Number(m[1]), mo = Number(m[2]), d = Number(m[3]);
  if (!Number.isFinite(y) || !Number.isFinite(mo) || !Number.isFinite(d)) return null;
  return new Date(`${y}/${pad2_(mo)}/${pad2_(d)} 00:00:00 +0900`);
}
function pad2_(n) { return String(n).padStart(2, "0"); }

// =====================================
// Text / numeric helpers
// =====================================
function toSafeCellString_(value, maxLen) { let s = String(value == null ? "" : value).slice(0, maxLen || CONFIG.MAX_STR_LEN); if (/^[=+\-@]/.test(s)) s = "'" + s; return s; }
function toFiniteNumberOrBlank_(value) { const n = Number(value); return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : ""; }
// perf列専用パーサー
// "-26.2008733624454%" のような文字列も、0.262... のような数値も両方対応
function parsePerfValue_(value) {
  if (value === '' || value == null) return NaN;
  const s = String(value).replace(/%/g, '').trim();
  const n = Number(s);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return NaN;
  // % 記号が含まれていた場合は100で割って小数に変換
  // 含まれていない場合はそのまま（既に小数の場合）
  return String(value).includes('%') ? n / 100 : n;
}
function toFiniteNumberOrNaN_(value) { if (value === "" || value == null) return NaN; const n = Number(value); return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : NaN; }
function looksLikeJpxCode_(symbolCode) { const s = String(symbolCode || "").trim().toUpperCase(); return /^(?:\d{4,6}|\d{3}[A-Z])$/.test(s); }
function appendTag_(base, add) { const b = String(base || "").trim(); if (!b) return add; if (b.indexOf(add) !== -1) return b; return b + "|" + add; }
function formatPct1_(v) { if (!Number.isFinite(v)) return "-"; return Utilities.formatString("%.1f%%", v * 100); }
function formatSignedPct1_(v) { if (!Number.isFinite(v)) return "-"; if (v > 0) return "+" + Utilities.formatString("%.1f%%", v * 100); if (v < 0) return "-" + Utilities.formatString("%.1f%%", Math.abs(v) * 100); return "0.0%"; }
function isTiePerf_(v) { return Number.isFinite(v) && Math.abs(v) < 1e-10; }
function jsonOut_(obj) { return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(obj)).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); }

// =====================================
// debug ログ
// =====================================
function purgeOldDebugLogs_() {
  return { ok: true, deleted: 0, skipped: true, reason: "debug sheet logging disabled" };
}
function writeProcessLog_(section, message, data) {
  const detail = data == null ? "" : " " + JSON.stringify(data);
  const text = `[${section}] ${message}${detail}`;
  try { console.log(text); } catch (e) {}
}
let OHLCV_VERBOSE_FETCH_LOGS_CACHE_ = null;
function isVerboseOhlcvFetchLogEnabled_() {
  if (OHLCV_VERBOSE_FETCH_LOGS_CACHE_ !== null) return OHLCV_VERBOSE_FETCH_LOGS_CACHE_;
  try {
    OHLCV_VERBOSE_FETCH_LOGS_CACHE_ =
      String(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("OHLCV_VERBOSE_FETCH_LOGS") || "").toLowerCase() === "true";
  } catch (e) {
    OHLCV_VERBOSE_FETCH_LOGS_CACHE_ = false;
  }
  return OHLCV_VERBOSE_FETCH_LOGS_CACHE_;
}
function describeDebugStage_(stage, data) {
  const d = data || {};
  if (stage === "daily.start") return d.isResume ? "日次メンテナンスを再開しました。" : "日次メンテナンスを開始しました。";
  if (stage === "daily.result") return "日次メンテナンスの結果です。評価日を迎えた行の更新状況を確認してください。";
  if (stage === "daily.error") return "日次メンテナンスでエラーが発生しました。再開可能な場合はトリガーで再試行します。";
  if (stage === "triggerOptimize.skip") return "GitHub Actionsの起動をスキップしました。GITHUB_PATが未設定です。";
  if (stage === "triggerOptimize.result") return "GitHub Actionsの起動結果です。HTTPステータスを確認してください。";
  if (stage === "triggerOptimize.error") return "GitHub Actionsの起動中にエラーが発生しました。";
  if (stage === "ohlcv.fatal") return "OHLCV取得が想定外のエラーで停止しました。状態を保存して再開トリガーを設定します。";
  if (stage === "ohlcv.resume.entry") return "OHLCV取得の再開トリガーが起動しました。保存済みのフェーズから続行します。";
  if (stage === "ohlcv.resume.error") return "OHLCV取得の再開中にエラーが発生しました。再試行トリガーを設定します。";
  if (stage === "ohlcv.resume.scheduled") return `OHLCV取得の続きとして ${d.handlerName || "再開ハンドラー"} を予約しました。`;
  if (stage === "ohlcv.phase.summary") return describeOhlcvPhaseSummary_(d);
  if (stage === "ohlcv.phase.error") return `${d.phase || "OHLCV"}: 外部取得または処理中にエラーが発生しました。`;
  if (stage === "ohlcv.phase1.batch") return "PHASE1: Yahoo FinanceからのOHLCV取得バッチが終わりました。";
  if (stage === "ohlcv.phase2.chunk") return d.adjustedRows > 0 ? "PHASE2: 株式分割調整を適用しました。対象銘柄はログ詳細を確認してください。" : "PHASE2: alerts_rawの分割調整チェックを1チャンク処理しました。";
  if (stage === "ohlcv.phase4.start") return "PHASE4: OHLCVシートの重複排除と並べ替えを開始します。";
  if (stage === "ohlcv.phase4.sort_before_dedupe.start") return "PHASE4: 重複排除前に、銘柄と日時で並べ替えています。";
  if (stage === "ohlcv.phase4.remove_duplicates.start") return "PHASE4: timestampと銘柄が同じOHLCV行を重複として削除しています。";
  if (stage === "ohlcv.phase4.sort_final.start") return "PHASE4: 最後にtimestamp昇順で並べ替えています。";
  if (stage === "ohlcv.phase4.complete") return "PHASE4: OHLCV取得フローが完了しました。日次メンテナンスを次に予約します。";
  if (stage === "appendRowsToSheet_.skippedInvalidTs") return "OHLCV追記時にtimestampが空・不正・対象外時刻の行をスキップしました。";
  if (String(stage || "").indexOf("fetchOHLCVRecent.") === 0) return "Yahoo FinanceのOHLCVレスポンス確認ログです。";
  return `${stage}: 処理ログ`;
}
function describeOhlcvPhaseSummary_(data) {
  const phase = data.phase || "OHLCV";
  if (data.event === "phase_start") return `${phase}: 処理を開始しました。`;
  if (data.event === "phase_pause") return `${phase}: 実行時間が近づいたため、進捗を保存して再開します。`;
  if (data.event === "phase_complete") return `${phase}: このフェーズが完了しました。`;
  return `${phase}: 進捗を記録しました。`;
}
function describeOhlcvEvent_(event, data) {
  const map = {
    "fetch.entry": "fetchOHLCVForNewAlertsを開始しました。",
    "entry": "OHLCV取得の初期化を始めます。",
    "lock_busy": "他のOHLCV処理が実行中のため、後で再開します。",
    "resume.scheduled": "OHLCV取得の再開トリガーを予約しました。",
    "resume.entry": "OHLCV取得の再開処理を開始しました。",
    "probe.scheduled": "OHLCV再開確認用のテストトリガーを予約しました。",
    "probe.fired": "OHLCV再開確認用のテストトリガーが起動しました。"
  };
  if (map[event]) return map[event];
  if (data && data.phase) return `${data.phase}: ${event}`;
  return `${event}: OHLCV処理ログ`;
}
function debugLogToSheet_(stage, data) {
  try {
    writeProcessLog_("GAS進捗", describeDebugStage_(stage, data), data == null ? {} : data);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`[debugLogToSheet_] console log failed stage=${stage}: ${err && err.message ? err.message : String(err)}`);
  }
}
function logError_(event, err, data) { console.error(`[ERROR] ${event} ${JSON.stringify({ message: err && err.message ? err.message : String(err), stack: err && err.stack ? String(err.stack) : "", extra: data || {} })}`); }

const MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_CHUNK_ROWS = 5000;
const MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_TIME_LIMIT_MS = 4 * 60 * 1000;
const MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_MIN_REMAINING_MS = 45 * 1000;
const MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_RESUME_AFTER_MS = 5 * 1000;
const MIDDAY_DEDUPE_GUARD_MS = 120 * 1000;
const MIDDAY_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY = "OHLCV_MIDDAY_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS";
const MIDDAY_HISTORY_COVERAGE_SCAN_MAX_ROWS = 250000;
const MIDDAY_HISTORY_GRACE_DAYS = 10;
const MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_STATE_KEY = "OHLCV_MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_STATE_V1";
const MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_SYMBOLS_KEY = "OHLCV_MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_SYMBOLS_V1";
const MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_RESUME_HANDLER = "resumeMiddayOhlcvRollback";

function getOhlcvRuntimeState_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  return {
    phase: props.getProperty(PHASE_KEY) || "PHASE1",
    cursor: props.getProperty(STATE_KEY) || "",
    symbolsSaved: !!props.getProperty(SYMBOL_LIST_KEY),
    newAlertCount: props.getProperty(NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY) || "",
    splitIndex: props.getProperty("SPLIT_INDEX") || "",
    hasSplitQueue: !!props.getProperty("SPLIT_QUEUE"),
    manualBusinessDate: props.getProperty(OHLCV_MANUAL_BUSINESS_DATE_KEY) || ""
  };
}

function traceOhlcv_(event, extra) {
  let state = {};
  try { state = getOhlcvRuntimeState_(); } catch (e) { state = { stateError: e && e.message ? e.message : String(e) }; }
  const data = Object.assign({
    event: event,
    at: new Date().toISOString()
  }, state, extra || {});
  const text = "[OHLCV詳細] " + describeOhlcvEvent_(event, data) + " " + JSON.stringify(data);

  try { PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(OHLCV_TRACE_PROP_KEY, JSON.stringify(data).slice(0, 8000)); } catch (e) {}
  try { console.log(text); } catch (e) {}
}

function logOhlcvRuntime_(event, extra) {
  const data = Object.assign({ event: event }, getOhlcvRuntimeState_(), extra || {});
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", describeOhlcvEvent_(event, data), data);
}

function runFetchOHLCVForNewAlertsAsDate_(dateValue) {
  const dateKey = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(dateValue);
  const expiresAt = Date.now() + OHLCV_MANUAL_BUSINESS_TTL_MS;
  resetProcessProperties_();
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperties({
    [OHLCV_MANUAL_BUSINESS_DATE_KEY]: dateKey,
    [OHLCV_MANUAL_BUSINESS_EXPIRES_KEY]: String(expiresAt)
  });
  writeProcessLog_("手動実行", `OHLCV取得の進捗状態をリセットし、今回のOHLCV取得チェーンでは基準日を ${dateKey} として扱います。`, {
    businessDate: dateKey,
    expiresAt: new Date(expiresAt).toISOString()
  });
  return fetchOHLCVForNewAlerts();
}

function clearManualOhlcvBusinessDate() {
  clearManualOhlcvBusinessDate_();
  writeProcessLog_("手動実行", "手動基準日の指定をクリアしました。次回から実行日のカレンダーで判定します。", {});
  return { ok: true, cleared: true };
}

function clearManualOhlcvBusinessDate_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_MANUAL_BUSINESS_DATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_MANUAL_BUSINESS_EXPIRES_KEY);
}

function normalizeBusinessDateKey_(value) {
  const d = value instanceof Date ? value : parseYmdFlexible_(value);
  if (!(d instanceof Date) || isNaN(d.getTime())) {
    throw new Error(`基準日が不正です。yyyy-mm-dd または yyyy/mm/dd で指定してください: ${value}`);
  }
  return formatYmd_(d);
}

function getManualOhlcvBusinessDateKey_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const dateKey = String(props.getProperty(OHLCV_MANUAL_BUSINESS_DATE_KEY) || "").trim();
  if (!dateKey) return "";
  const expiresAt = Number(props.getProperty(OHLCV_MANUAL_BUSINESS_EXPIRES_KEY) || "0");
  if (Number.isFinite(expiresAt) && expiresAt > 0 && Date.now() > expiresAt) {
    clearManualOhlcvBusinessDate_();
    writeProcessLog_("手動実行", "手動基準日の有効期限が切れたためクリアしました。", { businessDate: dateKey });
    return "";
  }
  try {
    return normalizeBusinessDateKey_(dateKey);
  } catch (e) {
    clearManualOhlcvBusinessDate_();
    writeProcessLog_("手動実行", "手動基準日の値が不正だったためクリアしました。実行日のカレンダーで続行します。", { value: dateKey, message: e.message });
    return "";
  }
}

function getOhlcvBusinessDate_() {
  const manualDateKey = getManualOhlcvBusinessDateKey_();
  return manualDateKey ? parseYmdFlexible_(manualDateKey) : new Date();
}

function getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_() {
  return formatYmd_(getOhlcvBusinessDate_());
}

function getOhlcvRunEndMillis_() {
  const manualDateKey = getManualOhlcvBusinessDateKey_();
  if (manualDateKey) return new Date(`${manualDateKey}T23:59:59+09:00`).getTime();
  return Date.now();
}

function buildLastTsMapFromOhlcv_(sheet, symbols, repairSymsSet) {
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  const map = {};
  if (lastRow < 2 || !symbols || symbols.length === 0) return map;

  const target = {};
  symbols.forEach(sym => {
    const s = String(sym || "").trim();
    if (s && !(repairSymsSet && repairSymsSet.has(s))) target[s] = true;
  });

  let found = 0;
  const targetCount = Object.keys(target).length;
  let row = lastRow;
  let chunks = 0;
  let scannedRows = 0;

  while (row >= 2 && found < targetCount && scannedRows < OHLCV_LAST_TS_SCAN_MAX_ROWS) {
    const numRows = Math.min(OHLCV_LAST_TS_SCAN_CHUNK_ROWS, row - 1);
    const startRow = row - numRows + 1;
    const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, 3).getValues();
    chunks++;
    scannedRows += numRows;

    for (let i = values.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(values[i][0]);
      const sym = String(values[i][2] || "").trim();
      if (!target[sym] || map[sym] || !ts) continue;
      map[sym] = ts.getTime();
      found++;
      if (found >= targetCount) break;
    }

    row = startRow - 1;
  }

  debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.lastTsMap.scan", {
    targetSymbols: targetCount,
    foundSymbols: found,
    chunks: chunks,
    scannedRows: scannedRows,
    maxRows: OHLCV_LAST_TS_SCAN_MAX_ROWS
  });
  return map;
}

function applyPhase1ResultRows_(symbol, resultRows, refreshId, lastTsMap) {
  if (!resultRows || resultRows.length === 0) return [];
  let skippedInvalidTs = 0;
  const skippedSamples = [];
  const validRows = [];
  resultRows.forEach((row, index) => {
    const ts = row && row.length ? row[0] : null;
    if (!isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts)) {
      skippedInvalidTs++;
      if (skippedSamples.length < 5) {
        skippedSamples.push({ index: index, timestamp: ts == null ? "" : String(ts), symbol: symbol });
      }
      return;
    }
    row[1] = refreshId;
    validRows.push(row);
  });
  if (skippedInvalidTs > 0) {
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase1.skippedInvalidTimestamp", {
      symbol: symbol,
      skipped: skippedInvalidTs,
      samples: skippedSamples
    });
  }
  if (validRows.length > 0) {
    const lastTimestamp = validRows[validRows.length - 1][0];
    if (lastTimestamp instanceof Date) {
      const currentLast = lastTsMap[symbol];
      if (!currentLast || lastTimestamp.getTime() > currentLast) {
        lastTsMap[symbol] = lastTimestamp.getTime();
      }
    }
  }
  return validRows;
}

function addSplitQueueItem_(props, symbol, ratio, splitDate) {
  let queue = JSON.parse(props.getProperty("SPLIT_QUEUE") || "[]");
  if (!queue.some(q => q.symbol === symbol && q.date === splitDate)) {
    queue.push({ symbol, ratio, date: splitDate });
    props.setProperty("SPLIT_QUEUE", JSON.stringify(queue));
  }
}

function applySplitAdjustToRawRow_(row, splitInfo) {
  if (!splitInfo || !splitInfo.splitRatio || splitInfo.splitRatio === 1 || !splitInfo.splitDate) return false;
  const splitTag = `SPLIT_ADJ_${splitInfo.splitDate}`;
  const note = String(row[RAW_IDX.note] || "");
  if (note.indexOf(splitTag) !== -1) return false;
  const alertDate = row[RAW_IDX.signal_date];
  if (!(alertDate instanceof Date) || (alertDate.getTime() / 1000 >= splitInfo.splitDate)) return false;
  const ratio = splitInfo.splitRatio;
  const priceCols = [RAW_IDX.entry_price, RAW_IDX.eval_close_5bd, RAW_IDX.eval_close_10bd, RAW_IDX.eval_close_20bd, RAW_IDX.eval_close_40bd];
  priceCols.forEach(idx => { if (Number.isFinite(row[idx]) && row[idx] > 0) row[idx] = Math.round((row[idx] / ratio) * 100) / 100; });
  const entryPrice = row[RAW_IDX.entry_price];
  if (Number.isFinite(entryPrice) && entryPrice > 0) {
    const cpCols = [
      { close: RAW_IDX.eval_close_5bd, perf: RAW_IDX.perf_5bd, win: RAW_IDX.win_flag_5bd },
      { close: RAW_IDX.eval_close_10bd, perf: RAW_IDX.perf_10bd, win: RAW_IDX.win_flag_10bd },
      { close: RAW_IDX.eval_close_20bd, perf: RAW_IDX.perf_20bd, win: RAW_IDX.win_flag_20bd },
      { close: RAW_IDX.eval_close_40bd, perf: RAW_IDX.perf_40bd, win: RAW_IDX.win_flag_40bd }
    ];
    const isTop = row[RAW_IDX.signal_type] === "TOP";
    cpCols.forEach(cp => {
      const evalClose = row[cp.close];
      if (Number.isFinite(evalClose) && evalClose > 0) {
        const perf = (evalClose - entryPrice) / entryPrice;
        row[cp.perf] = perf;
        row[cp.win] = isTop ? (perf < 0) : (perf > 0);
      }
    });
  }
  row[RAW_IDX.note] = appendTag_(note, splitTag);
  return true;
}

function fetchOHLCVForNewAlerts() {
  const entryTime = Date.now();
  let lock = null;
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "fetchOHLCVForNewAlertsを開始しました。休場日判定、ロック取得、PHASE進行の順に処理します。", {
    businessDate: getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_(),
    isManual: !!getManualOhlcvBusinessDateKey_()
  });
  traceOhlcv_("fetch.entry", { entryTime: new Date(entryTime).toISOString() });
  try {
    // 入口ログ後の初期処理で強制終了しても、再開だけは残るように先に保険を置く。
    scheduleOhlcvResume_("resumeOHLCVFetch", OHLCV_SAFETY_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
    deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_RESUME_HANDLER);
    logOhlcvRuntime_("entry", { triggeredAt: new Date(entryTime).toISOString() });
    lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
    if (!lock.tryLock(OHLCV_LOCK_WAIT_MS)) {
      logOhlcvRuntime_("lock_busy", { waitMs: OHLCV_LOCK_WAIT_MS });
      setupResumeTrigger_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
      return { ok: false, skipped: true, reason: "lock_busy" };
    }
    finalizeMiddayOhlcvBeforeMainRun_();
    return fetchOHLCVForNewAlertsInternal_(entryTime);
  } catch (e) {
    const errData = {
      message: e && e.message ? e.message : String(e),
      stack: e && e.stack ? String(e.stack) : "",
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - entryTime
    };
    console.error("[OHLCV] fetchOHLCVForNewAlerts fatal " + JSON.stringify(errData));
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.fatal", errData);
    try {
      setupResumeTrigger_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
    } catch (scheduleErr) {
      console.error("[OHLCV] failed to schedule resume after fatal: " + (scheduleErr && scheduleErr.message ? scheduleErr.message : String(scheduleErr)));
    }
    throw e;
  } finally {
    if (lock && lock.hasLock()) lock.releaseLock();
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "fetchOHLCVForNewAlertsの今回実行分を終了しました。未完了の場合は再開トリガーが続きます。", { elapsedMs: Date.now() - entryTime });
  }
}

function fetchOHLCVForNewAlertsMidday() {
  const entryTime = Date.now();
  let lock = null;
  const todayKey = getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_();
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30のOHLCV先行取得を開始しました。AM分だけを取得し、16:00本番チェーンには進みません。", {
    businessDate: todayKey,
    fetchEnd: getMiddayOhlcvFetchEndIso_(todayKey)
  });
  try {
    if (shouldStopMiddayOhlcvResume_()) {
      finalizeMiddayOhlcvBeforeMainRun_();
      writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "16:00本番処理の時間が近いため、13:30先行取得は再開せず終了します。", { businessDate: todayKey });
      return { ok: true, skipped: true, reason: "main_run_window" };
    }
    scheduleMiddayOhlcvResumeIfAllowed_(OHLCV_SAFETY_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
    lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
    if (!lock.tryLock(OHLCV_LOCK_WAIT_MS)) {
      writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "別の処理が実行中のため、13:30先行取得を1分後に再試行します。", { waitMs: OHLCV_LOCK_WAIT_MS });
      scheduleMiddayOhlcvResumeIfAllowed_(60 * 1000);
      return { ok: false, skipped: true, reason: "lock_busy" };
    }
    return fetchOHLCVForNewAlertsMiddayInternal_(entryTime);
  } catch (e) {
    const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    const errData = {
      message: e && e.message ? String(e.message) : String(e),
      stack: e && e.stack ? String(e.stack) : "",
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - entryTime,
      hasMiddayState: !!props.getProperty(MIDDAY_STATE_KEY),
      hasMiddaySymbols: !!props.getProperty(MIDDAY_SYMBOL_LIST_KEY),
      hasPostprocessPending: !!props.getProperty(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_PENDING_KEY)
    };

    console.error("[OHLCV-AM] fetchOHLCVForNewAlertsMidday fatal " + JSON.stringify(errData));
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.midday.fatal", errData);

    // 取得状態が残っている場合だけ、取得再開トリガーを作る。
    // 取得状態が消えているなら、同じ銘柄群を再取得させない。
    if (errData.hasMiddayState || errData.hasMiddaySymbols) {
      scheduleMiddayOhlcvResumeIfAllowed_(60 * 1000);
    } else {
      deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_RESUME_HANDLER);

      // 後処理だけ未完なら、後処理トリガーを再予約する。
      if (errData.hasPostprocessPending) {
        scheduleMiddayOhlcvPostprocess_({ keepState: true });
      }
    }

    throw e;
  } finally {
    if (lock && lock.hasLock()) lock.releaseLock();
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得の今回実行分を終了しました。未完了の場合は専用再開トリガーが続きます。", { elapsedMs: Date.now() - entryTime });
  }
}

function resumeOHLCVFetchMidday() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_RESUME_HANDLER);
  if (shouldStopMiddayOhlcvResume_()) {
    finalizeMiddayOhlcvBeforeMainRun_();
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "16:00本番処理の時間帯に入ったため、13:30先行取得の再開を停止しました。", {
      businessDate: getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_()
    });
    return { ok: true, stopped: true, reason: "main_run_window" };
  }
  return fetchOHLCVForNewAlertsMidday();
}

function fetchOHLCVForNewAlertsMiddayInternal_(outerStartedAt) {
  const today = getOhlcvBusinessDate_();
  const todayKey = formatYmd_(today);

  if (isMarketHolidayJst_(today, getMarketHolidaySet_())) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", `基準日 ${todayKey} は休場日のため、13:30先行取得をスキップします。`, { businessDate: todayKey });
    clearMiddayOhlcvState_();
    return { ok: true, skipped: true, reason: "market_holiday" };
  }

  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const expectedRefreshId = makeMiddayRefreshId_(todayKey);
  const savedRefreshId = props.getProperty(MIDDAY_REFRESH_ID_KEY) || "";

  if (savedRefreshId && savedRefreshId !== expectedRefreshId) {
    clearMiddayOhlcvState_({ keepTrigger: true });
  }

  if (!props.getProperty(MIDDAY_REFRESH_ID_KEY)) {
    props.setProperty(MIDDAY_REFRESH_ID_KEY, expectedRefreshId);
  }

const startTime = outerStartedAt || Date.now();
const LIMIT_MS = 4 * 60 * 1000;
const ss = openAppSpreadsheet_();
const rawSheet = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.RAW_SHEET);
const ohlcvSheet = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
const fetchEndMillis = getMiddayOhlcvFetchEndMillis_(todayKey);
const refreshId = props.getProperty(MIDDAY_REFRESH_ID_KEY) || expectedRefreshId;

// ★追加ここから
const rawAlertVolumeMapData = buildRawAlertVolumeMapForBusinessDate_(rawSheet, todayKey);
const rawAlertVolumeMap = rawAlertVolumeMapData.volumeMap;

writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "alerts_raw のvolumeを13:30先行取得の出来高上書き用に読み込みました。", rawAlertVolumeMapData.stats);
// ★追加ここまで

  let symbols = JSON.parse(props.getProperty(MIDDAY_SYMBOL_LIST_KEY) || "[]");
  let lastTsMap = JSON.parse(props.getProperty(MIDDAY_LAST_TS_MAP_KEY) || "{}");
  let fullBackfillSymbols = JSON.parse(props.getProperty(MIDDAY_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY) || "[]");
  let fullBackfillSet = new Set(fullBackfillSymbols);

  let newAlertCount = parseInt(props.getProperty(MIDDAY_NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY) || "0", 10);
  if (!Number.isFinite(newAlertCount)) newAlertCount = 0;

if (symbols.length === 0) {
  const todaySymbols = collectTodayRawSymbols_(rawSheet, todayKey);

  // alerts_raw に登場した全銘柄を候補にする
  const allRawSymbols = collectAllRawSymbolsFromRaw_(rawSheet)
    .map(s => String(s || "").trim())
    .filter(Boolean);

  const symbolSet = new Set(allRawSymbols);
  symbols = Array.from(symbolSet).sort();

  // alerts_raw に銘柄が1つも無い場合だけスキップ
  if (symbols.length === 0) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得の対象銘柄がないためスキップします。", {
      businessDate: todayKey,
      todaySymbols: todaySymbols.length
    });
    clearMiddayOhlcvState_();
    return { ok: true, skipped: true, reason: "no_target_symbols" };
  }

  lastTsMap = buildLastTsMapFromOhlcv_(ohlcvSheet, symbols, new Set());

  fullBackfillSymbols = symbols.filter(sym => !lastTsMap[sym]);
  fullBackfillSet = new Set(fullBackfillSymbols);
  todaySymbols.forEach(sym => {
    const s = String(sym || "").trim();
    if (!s) return;
    if (!lastTsMap[s]) fullBackfillSet.add(s);
  });
  fullBackfillSymbols = Array.from(fullBackfillSet).sort();

  newAlertCount = todaySymbols.length;

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得の対象銘柄を差分取得型に初期化しました。OHLCV未取得銘柄だけ120日分、既存OHLCV銘柄はlastTs以降を取得します。", {
    totalFetchSymbols: symbols.length,
    trueNewFullBackfillSymbols: fullBackfillSymbols.length,
    existingDeltaSymbols: symbols.length - fullBackfillSymbols.length,
    todaySignalSymbols: todaySymbols.length,
    fetchEnd: getMiddayOhlcvFetchEndIso_(todayKey)
  });

  props.setProperties({
    [MIDDAY_SYMBOL_LIST_KEY]: JSON.stringify(symbols),
    [MIDDAY_LAST_TS_MAP_KEY]: JSON.stringify(lastTsMap),
    [MIDDAY_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY]: JSON.stringify(fullBackfillSymbols),
    [MIDDAY_STATE_KEY]: "0",
    [MIDDAY_NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY]: String(newAlertCount),
    [MIDDAY_REFRESH_ID_KEY]: refreshId
  });
}

  let startIndex = parseInt(props.getProperty(MIDDAY_STATE_KEY) || "0", 10);
  if (!Number.isFinite(startIndex) || startIndex < 0) startIndex = 0;

  let allFetchedData = [];
  let batchNo = 0;

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", `13:30先行取得を ${startIndex}/${symbols.length} 銘柄目から再開します。`, {
    businessDate: todayKey,
    fetchEnd: getMiddayOhlcvFetchEndIso_(todayKey),
    refreshId: refreshId,
    fullBackfillSymbols: fullBackfillSet.size
  });

  if (startIndex === 0) {
    maybePurgeInvalidOhlcvTimestampRows_(
      ohlcvSheet,
      "midday.before_fetch",
      QUICK_REPAIR_ENTRY_CLEANUP_ROWS,
      todayKey
    );
  } else {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得の再開実行では、広範囲の不正timestamp掃除をスキップします。", {
      cursor: startIndex,
      cleanupRows: 0
    });
  }

  for (let i = startIndex; i < symbols.length; i += MIDDAY_OHLCV_BATCH_SIZE) {
    const elapsedBeforeBatch = Date.now() - startTime;

    if (elapsedBeforeBatch > LIMIT_MS || (LIMIT_MS - elapsedBeforeBatch) < MIDDAY_DEDUPE_GUARD_MS) {
      const appended = appendAndNormalizeMiddayOhlcvRows_(ohlcvSheet, allFetchedData, "pause");

      props.setProperty(MIDDAY_STATE_KEY, i.toString());
      props.setProperty(MIDDAY_NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY, newAlertCount.toString());
      props.setProperty(MIDDAY_LAST_TS_MAP_KEY, JSON.stringify(lastTsMap));
      props.setProperty(MIDDAY_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY, JSON.stringify(Array.from(fullBackfillSet)));

      writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", `実行時間が近づいたため、${i}/${symbols.length} 銘柄までの進捗を保存して再開します。`, {
        cursor: i,
        totalSymbols: symbols.length,
        appendedRows: appended,
        elapsedMs: elapsedBeforeBatch
      });

      scheduleMiddayOhlcvResumeIfAllowed_(10 * 1000);
      return { ok: true, incomplete: true, nextIndex: i, appendedRows: appended };
    }

    const batchStart = Date.now();
    const batch = symbols.slice(i, Math.min(i + MIDDAY_OHLCV_BATCH_SIZE, symbols.length));
    const requests = [];
    const reqMeta = [];

    batch.forEach(sym => {
  try {
    const useLastTs = fullBackfillSet.has(sym) ? null : (lastTsMap[sym] || null);
    const pair = buildOhlcvRequestPairForEndMillis_(sym, useLastTs, fetchEndMillis, null, null, { forcePeriod: true });

    const intraIdx = requests.length;
    requests.push({
      url: pair.intraUrl,
      muteHttpExceptions: true,
      headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0" }
    });

    reqMeta.push({
      symbol: sym,
      intraIdx: intraIdx,
      fullBackfill: fullBackfillSet.has(sym)
    });

  } catch (e) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", `${sym} の先行取得URLを作れませんでした。`, {
      symbol: sym,
      message: e.message
    });
  }
});

    batchNo++;

    let responses = null;
    let parseErrCount = 0;
    let fetchedRowsInBatch = 0;
    let fetchedSymbolsInBatch = 0;
    let fullBackfillInBatch = 0;

try {
  responses = requests.length > 0 ? UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(requests) : [];
} catch (e) {
  const message = e && e.message ? String(e.message) : String(e);

  props.setProperty(MIDDAY_STATE_KEY, i.toString());
  props.setProperty(MIDDAY_NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY, newAlertCount.toString());
  props.setProperty(MIDDAY_LAST_TS_MAP_KEY, JSON.stringify(lastTsMap));
  props.setProperty(MIDDAY_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY, JSON.stringify(Array.from(fullBackfillSet)));

const appendedOnError = appendAndNormalizeMiddayOhlcvRows_(ohlcvSheet, allFetchedData, "fetch_error");

if (appendedOnError > 0) {
  scheduleMiddayOhlcvPostprocess_();
}

if (isUrlFetchDailyLimitError_(e)) {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_RESUME_HANDLER);

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "UrlFetch日次上限に到達したため、本日の13:30先行取得の再開を停止しました。", {
    batchNo: batchNo,
    cursor: i,
    totalSymbols: symbols.length,
    appendedRows: appendedOnError,
    message: message
  });

  debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.midday.urlfetch_daily_limit_stop", {
    batchNo: batchNo,
    cursor: i,
    totalSymbols: symbols.length,
    appendedRows: appendedOnError,
    message: message
  });

  return {
    ok: false,
    stopped: true,
    reason: "urlfetch_daily_limit",
    nextIndex: i,
    appendedRows: appendedOnError,
    message: message
  };
}

writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", `バッチ ${batchNo} の先行取得に失敗しました。再開トリガーに回します。`, {
  batchNo: batchNo,
  appendedRows: appendedOnError,
  message: message
});

scheduleMiddayOhlcvResumeIfAllowed_(60 * 1000);

return {
  ok: false,
  incomplete: true,
  reason: "fetch_error",
  appendedRows: appendedOnError,
  message: message
};
}

    reqMeta.forEach(meta => {
  try {
    const intraRes = responses[meta.intraIdx];

const result = parseIntraResponse_(meta.symbol, intraRes, null, null, {
  todayKeyForMaxAmVolume: todayKey
});
const validRows = applyPhase1ResultRows_(meta.symbol, result, refreshId, lastTsMap);
const filteredRows = filterMiddayOhlcvRows_(validRows, todayKey);

// alerts_raw J列 volume で、該当シグナル足のvolumeだけ上書きする
const rawVolumeApplied = applyRawAlertVolumesToOhlcvRows_(filteredRows, rawAlertVolumeMap);
const finalRows = rawVolumeApplied.rows;

if (rawVolumeApplied.overridden > 0) {
  debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.midday.rawVolumeOverride", {
    symbol: meta.symbol,
    overridden: rawVolumeApplied.overridden
  });
}

if (finalRows.length > 0) {
  allFetchedData = allFetchedData.concat(finalRows);
  fetchedRowsInBatch += finalRows.length;
  fetchedSymbolsInBatch++;
  if (meta.fullBackfill) fullBackfillInBatch++;
}
      } catch (e) {
        parseErrCount++;
        writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", `${meta.symbol} の先行取得レスポンスをOHLCV行に変換できませんでした。`, {
          symbol: meta.symbol,
          message: e.message
        });
      }
    });

    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.midday.batch", {
      batchNo: batchNo,
      startIndex: i,
      symbolsInBatch: batch.length,
      requestCount: requests.length,
      fetchedRows: fetchedRowsInBatch,
      symbolsWithRows: fetchedSymbolsInBatch,
      fullBackfillSymbols: fullBackfillInBatch,
      parseErrCount: parseErrCount,
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - batchStart
    });

    Utilities.sleep(MIDDAY_OHLCV_BATCH_SLEEP_MS);
  }

const appended = appendAndNormalizeMiddayOhlcvRows_(ohlcvSheet, allFetchedData, "complete");

// 重要：重い後処理より先に、13:30取得状態と再開トリガーを消す。
// これを先にやらないと、タイムアウト時に同じカーソルから再取得される。
clearMiddayOhlcvState_();

sendCompletionNotice_(String(newAlertCount), "0", "AM");

// 重複排除・ソートは時間余裕があればインラインで実行。
// 足りなければ既存通り別トリガー予約。GAS タイムアウト 360 秒から
// 30 秒の安全バッファを引いた残り時間が 180 秒以上あればインライン化を試みる。
if (appended > 0) {
  const elapsedSoFar = Date.now() - startTime;
  const INLINE_SAFETY_BUFFER_MS = 30 * 1000;
  const INLINE_MIN_AVAILABLE_MS = 180 * 1000;
  const availableForInlineMs = 360 * 1000 - elapsedSoFar - INLINE_SAFETY_BUFFER_MS;

  if (availableForInlineMs >= INLINE_MIN_AVAILABLE_MS) {
    try {
      const state = loadMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_(ohlcvSheet);
      // runMiddayOhlcvPostprocessChunks_ の deadline = startedAt + MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_TIME_LIMIT_MS。
      // deadline = Date.now() + availableForInlineMs にしたいので startedAt を逆算する。
      const spoofedStartedAt = Date.now() + availableForInlineMs - MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_TIME_LIMIT_MS;
      const result = runMiddayOhlcvPostprocessChunks_(ohlcvSheet, state, spoofedStartedAt);
      if (result.complete) {
        clearMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_();
        writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得が完了し、後処理もインラインで完了しました。", {
          fetchedSymbols: newAlertCount,
          appendedRows: appended,
          refreshId: refreshId,
          postprocessTotals: result.state.totals,
          inlineElapsedMs: result.elapsedMs
        });
      } else {
        saveMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_(result.state);
        scheduleMiddayOhlcvPostprocess_({ keepState: true });
        writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得が完了。インライン後処理は途中までで時間切れのため、続きを別トリガーで実行します。", {
          fetchedSymbols: newAlertCount,
          appendedRows: appended,
          refreshId: refreshId,
          remainingRows: result.state.remainingRows,
          inlineElapsedMs: result.elapsedMs
        });
      }
    } catch (postErr) {
      writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "インライン後処理で例外発生。別トリガーで再試行します。", {
        message: postErr && postErr.message ? postErr.message : String(postErr)
      });
      scheduleMiddayOhlcvPostprocess_({ keepState: true });
    }
  } else {
    scheduleMiddayOhlcvPostprocess_();
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得が完了しました。時間余裕がないため後処理は別トリガーで実行します。", {
      fetchedSymbols: newAlertCount,
      appendedRows: appended,
      refreshId: refreshId,
      elapsedMs: elapsedSoFar
    });
  }
} else {
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得が完了しました（追記行なし）。", {
    fetchedSymbols: newAlertCount,
    appendedRows: appended,
    refreshId: refreshId
  });
}

return { ok: true, complete: true, fetchedSymbols: newAlertCount, appendedRows: appended };
}

function collectAllRawSymbolsFromRaw_(rawSheet) {
  const lastRow = rawSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < dataStartRow_()) return [];

  const numRows = lastRow - dataStartRow_() + 1;
  const values = rawSheet.getRange(dataStartRow_(), 1, numRows, RAW_HEADERS.length).getValues();

  const set = new Set();

  values.forEach(r => {
    const signalType = String(r[RAW_IDX.signal_type] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (signalType !== "BOTTOM") return;
    const sym = String(r[RAW_IDX.symbol_code] || "").trim();
    if (sym) set.add(sym);
  });

  return Array.from(set).sort();
}

function filterMiddayOhlcvRows_(rows, todayKey) {
  const out = [];
  let skippedInvalid = 0;
  let skippedTodayPm = 0;
  let skippedFuture = 0;
  const samples = [];

  (rows || []).forEach((row, index) => {
    const normalized = normalizeOhlcvRowForWrite_(row);

    if (!normalized) {
      skippedInvalid++;
      if (samples.length < 5) {
        samples.push({
          index: index,
          timestamp: row && row.length ? String(row[0]) : "",
          symbol: row && row.length > 2 ? String(row[2]) : ""
        });
      }
      return;
    }

    const ts = normalized[0];
    const dateKey = formatYmd_(ts);

    if (dateKey > todayKey) {
      skippedFuture++;
      return;
    }

    if (dateKey === todayKey && getOhlcvSessionName_(ts) !== "AM") {
      skippedTodayPm++;
      return;
    }

    out.push(normalized);
  });

  if (skippedInvalid > 0 || skippedFuture > 0 || isVerboseOhlcvFetchLogEnabled_()) {
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.midday.filter", {
      inputRows: rows ? rows.length : 0,
      outputRows: out.length,
      skippedInvalid: skippedInvalid,
      skippedTodayPm: skippedTodayPm,
      skippedFuture: skippedFuture,
      samples: samples
    });
  }

  return out;
}

function appendAndNormalizeMiddayOhlcvRows_(sheet, rows, context) {
  if (!rows || rows.length === 0) return 0;

  const appended = appendRowsToSheet_(sheet, rows);

  if (appended > 0) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得でOHLCVを追記しました。", {
      context: context || "",
      appendedRows: appended
    });
  }

  return appended;
}

function scheduleMiddayOhlcvPostprocess_(options) {
  const opts = options || {};
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.setProperty(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_PENDING_KEY, new Date().toISOString());
  if (opts.keepState !== true) props.deleteProperty(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_STATE_KEY);

  deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_HANDLER);

  ScriptApp.newTrigger(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_HANDLER)
    .timeBased()
    .after(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_RESUME_AFTER_MS)
    .create();

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得の後処理を別トリガーに分離して予約しました。", {
    handlerName: MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_HANDLER,
    delayMs: MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_RESUME_AFTER_MS
  });
}

function buildMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_(sheet) {
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  const scanRows = lastRow > 1
    ? Math.min(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_SCAN_ROWS, lastRow - 1)
    : 0;

  return {
    version: MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_STATE_VERSION,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    originalLastRow: lastRow,
    nextEndRow: lastRow,
    remainingRows: scanRows,
    scanRows: scanRows,
    chunks: 0,
    totals: {
      scannedRows: 0,
      convertedRows: 0,
      deletedRows: 0,
      repairSymbols: 0
    }
  };
}

function loadMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_(sheet) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  try {
    const raw = props.getProperty(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_STATE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return buildMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_(sheet);

    const state = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!state || Number(state.version) !== MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_STATE_VERSION) {
      return buildMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_(sheet);
    }

    if (!state.totals) state.totals = {};
    state.nextEndRow = Math.max(1, Number(state.nextEndRow) || 1);
    state.remainingRows = Math.max(0, Number(state.remainingRows) || 0);
    state.scanRows = Math.max(0, Number(state.scanRows) || state.remainingRows);
    state.chunks = Math.max(0, Number(state.chunks) || 0);
    state.totals.scannedRows = Math.max(0, Number(state.totals.scannedRows) || 0);
    state.totals.convertedRows = Math.max(0, Number(state.totals.convertedRows) || 0);
    state.totals.deletedRows = Math.max(0, Number(state.totals.deletedRows) || 0);
    state.totals.repairSymbols = Math.max(0, Number(state.totals.repairSymbols) || 0);

    const currentLastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    if (state.nextEndRow > currentLastRow) state.nextEndRow = currentLastRow;

    return state;
  } catch (e) {
    return buildMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_(sheet);
  }
}

function saveMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_(state) {
  state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
}

function clearMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.deleteProperty(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_PENDING_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_STATE_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_HANDLER);
}

function runMiddayOhlcvPostprocessChunks_(sheet, state, startedAt) {
  const deadline = startedAt + MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_TIME_LIMIT_MS;
  const chunkSummaries = [];

  while (state.remainingRows > 0 && (deadline - Date.now()) > MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_MIN_REMAINING_MS) {
    const availableRows = Math.max(0, state.nextEndRow - 1);
    const chunkRows = Math.min(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_CHUNK_ROWS, state.remainingRows, availableRows);
    if (chunkRows <= 0) {
      state.remainingRows = 0;
      break;
    }

    const endRow = state.nextEndRow;
    const startRow = endRow - chunkRows + 1;
    const result = purgeInvalidOhlcvTimestampRowsInRange_(sheet, startRow, chunkRows, "midday.postprocess");

    state.nextEndRow = startRow - 1;
    state.remainingRows = Math.max(0, state.remainingRows - chunkRows);
    state.chunks += 1;
    state.totals.scannedRows += result.scannedRows || chunkRows;
    state.totals.convertedRows += result.convertedRows || 0;
    state.totals.deletedRows += result.deletedRows || 0;
    state.totals.repairSymbols += result.repairSymbols || 0;

    if (chunkSummaries.length < 5) {
      chunkSummaries.push({
        startRow: startRow,
        endRow: endRow,
        scannedRows: result.scannedRows || chunkRows,
        convertedRows: result.convertedRows || 0,
        deletedRows: result.deletedRows || 0
      });
    }
  }

  return {
    complete: state.remainingRows <= 0,
    state: state,
    chunkSummaries: chunkSummaries,
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt
  };
}

function postprocessMiddayOhlcv() {
  let lock = null;
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  try {
    lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

    if (!lock.tryLock(OHLCV_LOCK_WAIT_MS)) {
      writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "後処理中に別処理が実行中のため、1分後に再試行します。", {
        waitMs: OHLCV_LOCK_WAIT_MS
      });
      scheduleMiddayOhlcvPostprocess_({ keepState: true });
      return { ok: false, skipped: true, reason: "lock_busy" };
    }

    deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_POSTPROCESS_HANDLER);

    const ss = openAppSpreadsheet_();
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
    if (!sh) throw new Error("ohlcv_4h シートが見つかりません");

    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得の後処理を開始します。", {});

    const state = loadMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_(sh);
    const result = runMiddayOhlcvPostprocessChunks_(sh, state, startedAt);

    if (!result.complete) {
      saveMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_(result.state);
      scheduleMiddayOhlcvPostprocess_({ keepState: true });
      writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "Midday postprocess paused and scheduled the next chunk.", {
        remainingRows: result.state.remainingRows,
        nextEndRow: result.state.nextEndRow,
        chunks: result.state.chunks,
        totals: result.state.totals,
        elapsedMs: result.elapsedMs,
        chunkSummaries: result.chunkSummaries
      });
      return { ok: true, incomplete: true, reason: "time_guard", remainingRows: result.state.remainingRows };
    }

    clearMiddayOhlcvPostprocessState_();
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "Midday postprocess completed.", {
      scanRows: result.state.scanRows,
      chunks: result.state.chunks,
      totals: result.state.totals,
      elapsedMs: result.elapsedMs
    });

    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得の後処理が完了しました。", {});

    return { ok: true, complete: true, scanRows: result.state.scanRows, totals: result.state.totals };

  } catch (e) {
    const message = e && e.message ? String(e.message) : String(e);

    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM", "13:30先行取得の後処理でエラーが発生しました。再試行します。", {
      message: message
    });

    scheduleMiddayOhlcvPostprocess_({ keepState: true });

    return { ok: false, reason: "postprocess_error", message: message };

  } finally {
    if (lock && lock.hasLock()) lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function scheduleMiddayOhlcvResumeIfAllowed_(delayMs) {
  if (shouldStopMiddayOhlcvResume_()) {
    deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_RESUME_HANDLER);
    return false;
  }
  scheduleOhlcvResume_(MIDDAY_RESUME_HANDLER, delayMs);
  return true;
}

function shouldStopMiddayOhlcvResume_() {
  const dateKey = getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_();
  const stopAt = new Date(`${dateKey}T15:55:00+09:00`).getTime();
  return Date.now() >= stopAt;
}

function getMiddayOhlcvFetchEndMillis_(dateKey) {
  const key = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(dateKey);
  return new Date(`${key}T13:00:59+09:00`).getTime();
}

function getMiddayOhlcvFetchEndIso_(dateKey) {
  return new Date(getMiddayOhlcvFetchEndMillis_(dateKey)).toISOString();
}

function makeMiddayRefreshId_(dateKey) {
  return `MIDDAY_${normalizeBusinessDateKey_(dateKey)}`;
}

function makeProtectedMiddayRefreshId_(dateKey) {
  return `MIDDAY_LOCKED_${normalizeBusinessDateKey_(dateKey)}`;
}

function isProtectedMiddayOhlcvMarker_(marker) {
  return String(marker || "").trim().match(/^MIDDAY_LOCKED_/) !== null;
}

function makePmLockedMarker_(dateKey) {
  return "PM_LOCKED_" + String(dateKey || "");
}

function isPmLockedMarker_(marker) {
  return String(marker || "").trim().match(/^PM_LOCKED_/) !== null;
}

function previewRepairOhlcvAm20260511Only() {
  return repairOhlcvAmForDateOnly_("2026-05-11", true);
}

function repairOhlcvAm20260511Only() {
  return repairOhlcvAmForDateOnly_("2026-05-11", false);
}

function repairOhlcvAmForDateOnly_(dateKey, dryRun) {
  const targetDateKey = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(dateKey);
  const targetTimestamp = new Date(`${targetDateKey}T09:00:00+09:00`);
  const marker = `AM_REPAIR_${targetDateKey}`;
  const BATCH_SIZE = 25;
  const startedAt = Date.now();

  const ss = openAppSpreadsheet_();
  const rawSheet = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.RAW_SHEET);
  const ohlcvSheet = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!rawSheet) throw new Error(`${CONFIG.RAW_SHEET} sheet not found`);
  if (!ohlcvSheet) throw new Error(`${CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET} sheet not found`);

  const existingRowMap = buildOhlcvAmRowMapForDate_(ohlcvSheet, targetDateKey);
  const symbolSet = new Set();
  collectAllRawSymbolsFromRaw_(rawSheet).forEach(sym => {
    const s = String(sym || "").trim();
    if (s) symbolSet.add(s);
  });
  Object.keys(existingRowMap).forEach(sym => {
    const s = String(sym || "").trim();
    if (s) symbolSet.add(s);
  });

  const symbols = Array.from(symbolSet).sort();
  const updates = [];
  const inserts = [];
  const samples = [];
  const noDataSymbols = [];
  const transientSymbols = [];
  let fetchedRows = 0;
  let backfillRows = 0;
  let backfillSymbols = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < symbols.length; i += BATCH_SIZE) {
    const batch = symbols.slice(i, i + BATCH_SIZE);
    const reqMeta = batch.map(sym => {
      const hasTargetRow = !!(existingRowMap[sym] && existingRowMap[sym].length > 0);
      return {
        symbol: sym,
        hasTargetRow: hasTargetRow,
        url: hasTargetRow
          ? buildYahooIntraDateWindowUrl_(sym, targetDateKey)
          : buildYahooIntraLookbackUrlForDate_(sym, targetDateKey, OHLCV_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_DAYS)
      };
    });
    const requests = reqMeta.map(meta => ({
      url: meta.url,
      muteHttpExceptions: true,
      headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0" }
    }));

    let responses = [];
    try {
      responses = requests.length ? UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(requests) : [];
    } catch (e) {
      batch.forEach(sym => transientSymbols.push(sym));
      writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM-REPAIR", "date AM repair batch fetch failed", {
        dateKey: targetDateKey,
        batchStart: i,
        message: e && e.message ? e.message : String(e)
      });
      continue;
    }

    reqMeta.forEach((meta, index) => {
      const sym = meta.symbol;
      const res = responses[index];
      if (isTransientHttpResponse_(res)) {
        transientSymbols.push(sym);
        return;
      }

      if (!meta.hasTargetRow) {
        const rows = buildOhlcvRowsFromYahooChartResponse_(sym, res, targetDateKey, marker);
        if (!rows || rows.length === 0) {
          noDataSymbols.push(sym);
          return;
        }

        fetchedRows++;
        backfillSymbols++;
        backfillRows += rows.length;
        inserts.push.apply(inserts, rows);

        const targetRow = rows.find(row => formatYmd_(row[0]) === targetDateKey && getOhlcvSessionName_(row[0]) === "AM") || rows[rows.length - 1];
        if (samples.length < 20) {
          samples.push({
            symbol: sym,
            action: "backfill_120d",
            rows: [],
            appendedRows: rows.length,
            ohlcv: targetRow ? targetRow.slice(3, 8) : []
          });
        }
        return;
      }

      const parsed = buildAmOhlcvRowFromYahooChartResponse_(sym, res, targetDateKey, marker, targetTimestamp);
      if (!parsed || !parsed.row) {
        noDataSymbols.push(sym);
        return;
      }

      fetchedRows++;
      const row = parsed.row;
      const rowNums = existingRowMap[sym] || [];
      if (rowNums.length > 0) {
        rowNums.forEach(rowNum => {
          updates.push({ row: rowNum, values: row.slice(3, 8), symbol: sym });
        });
      } else {
        inserts.push(row);
      }

      if (samples.length < 20) {
        samples.push({
          symbol: sym,
          action: rowNums.length > 0 ? "update" : "insert",
          rows: rowNums,
          hours: parsed.hours,
          ohlcv: row.slice(3, 8)
        });
      }
    });

    Utilities.sleep(300);
  }

  if (!dryRun) {
    applyOhlcvValueUpdates_(ohlcvSheet, updates);
    if (inserts.length > 0) appendRowsToSheet_(ohlcvSheet, inserts);
    purgeInvalidOhlcvTimestampRows_(ohlcvSheet, "repairLatestAmOhlcvRows.after_write", OHLCV_APPEND_INTEGRITY_SCAN_ROWS);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  }

  const summary = {
    ok: true,
    dryRun: dryRun === true,
    dateKey: targetDateKey,
    timestamp: Utilities.formatDate(targetTimestamp, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm"),
    targetSymbols: symbols.length,
    fetchedRows: fetchedRows,
    updateCellsRows: updates.length,
    insertRows: inserts.length,
    backfillSymbols: backfillSymbols,
    backfillRows: backfillRows,
    noDataSymbols: noDataSymbols.length,
    transientSymbols: transientSymbols.length,
    noDataSamples: noDataSymbols.slice(0, 30),
    transientSamples: transientSymbols.slice(0, 30),
    samples: samples,
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt
  };

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV-AM-REPAIR", "date AM repair completed", summary);
  return summary;
}

function buildOhlcvAmRowMapForDate_(sheet, dateKey) {
  const map = {};
  const DATA_ROW = 2;
  const CHUNK_ROWS = 5000;
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < DATA_ROW) return map;

  for (let start = DATA_ROW; start <= lastRow; start += CHUNK_ROWS) {
    const numRows = Math.min(CHUNK_ROWS, lastRow - start + 1);
    const values = sheet.getRange(start, 1, numRows, 3).getValues();
    values.forEach((row, index) => {
      const ts = row[0];
      const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
      if (!symbol || !(ts instanceof Date) || isNaN(ts.getTime())) return;
      if (formatYmd_(ts) !== dateKey) return;
      if (getOhlcvSessionName_(ts) !== "AM") return;
      if (!map[symbol]) map[symbol] = [];
      map[symbol].push(start + index);
    });
  }

  return map;
}

function buildYahooIntraDateWindowUrl_(symbol, dateKey) {
  const yahooSymbol = convertToYahooSymbol(symbol);
  const p1 = Math.floor(new Date(`${dateKey}T08:00:00+09:00`).getTime() / 1000);
  const p2 = Math.floor(new Date(`${dateKey}T13:00:59+09:00`).getTime() / 1000);
  return `https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/${yahooSymbol}?interval=1h&period1=${p1}&period2=${p2}`;
}

function buildYahooIntraLookbackUrlForDate_(symbol, dateKey, lookbackDays) {
  const yahooSymbol = convertToYahooSymbol(symbol);
  const p2 = Math.floor(new Date(`${dateKey}T13:00:59+09:00`).getTime() / 1000);
  const p1 = p2 - Math.max(1, Number(lookbackDays) || OHLCV_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_DAYS) * 24 * 60 * 60;
  return `https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/${yahooSymbol}?interval=1h&period1=${p1}&period2=${p2}`;
}

function buildOhlcvRowsFromYahooChartResponse_(symbol, res, targetDateKey, marker) {
  try {
    if (!res || res.getResponseCode() !== 200) return [];

    const rows = parseIntraResponse_(symbol, res, null) || [];
    return (rows || [])
      .map(row => {
        if (!row || !row.length) return null;
        const copy = row.slice(0, 8);
        copy[1] = marker;
        return normalizeOhlcvRowForWrite_(copy);
      })
      .filter(row => {
        if (!row) return false;
        const ts = row[0];
        const dateKey = formatYmd_(ts);
        if (dateKey > targetDateKey) return false;
        if (dateKey === targetDateKey && getOhlcvSessionName_(ts) !== "AM") return false;
        return true;
      });
  } catch (e) {
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.amRepair.backfillParseError", {
      symbol: symbol,
      dateKey: targetDateKey,
      message: e && e.message ? e.message : String(e)
    });
    return [];
  }
}

function buildAmOhlcvRowFromYahooChartResponse_(symbol, res, dateKey, marker, targetTimestamp) {
  try {
    if (!res || res.getResponseCode() !== 200) return null;

    const json = JSON.parse(res.getContentText());
    const result = json.chart && json.chart.result && json.chart.result[0];
    if (!result || !result.timestamp) return null;

    const q = result.indicators && result.indicators.quote && result.indicators.quote[0];
    if (!q) return null;

    const hours = [];
    const round = val => Math.round(Number(val) * 100) / 100;
    let candle = null;

    for (let i = 0; i < result.timestamp.length; i++) {
      const ts = result.timestamp[i];
      if (!ts) continue;

      const dateObj = new Date(ts * 1000);
      if (isNaN(dateObj.getTime())) continue;

      const rowDateKey = Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd");
      if (rowDateKey !== dateKey) continue;

      const hour = parseInt(Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "HH"), 10);
      if (hour < 9 || hour > 12) continue;

      const rawOpen = q.open ? q.open[i] : null;
      const rawHigh = q.high ? q.high[i] : null;
      const rawLow = q.low ? q.low[i] : null;
      const rawClose = q.close ? q.close[i] : null;
      const rawVolume = q.volume ? q.volume[i] : null;
      if (rawOpen == null && rawClose == null) continue;

      const close = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawClose);
      const open = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawOpen) !== null ? toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawOpen) : close;
      const high = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawHigh) !== null ? toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawHigh) : Math.max(open, close);
      const low = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawLow) !== null ? toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawLow) : Math.min(open, close);
      const volume = toFiniteNumberOrZero_(rawVolume);
      if (open === null || high === null || low === null || close === null) continue;

      const isFlatZeroVolume =
        volume === 0 &&
        open === high &&
        high === low &&
        low === close;
      if (isFlatZeroVolume) continue;

      hours.push(Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "HH:mm"));

      if (!candle) {
        candle = {
          open: round(open),
          high: round(high),
          low: round(low),
          close: round(close),
          volume: volume
        };
      } else {
        candle.high = round(Math.max(candle.high, high));
        candle.low = round(Math.min(candle.low, low));
        candle.close = round(close);
        candle.volume += volume;
      }
    }

    if (!candle) return null;

    return {
      hours: hours,
      row: [
        new Date(targetTimestamp.getTime()),
        marker,
        String(symbol || "").trim(),
        candle.open,
        candle.high,
        candle.low,
        candle.close,
        Math.round(candle.volume)
      ]
    };
  } catch (e) {
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.amRepair.parseError", {
      symbol: symbol,
      dateKey: dateKey,
      message: e && e.message ? e.message : String(e)
    });
    return null;
  }
}

function applyOhlcvValueUpdates_(sheet, updates) {
  if (!updates || updates.length === 0) return 0;

  const sorted = updates.slice().sort((a, b) => a.row - b.row);
  let applied = 0;
  let runStart = null;
  let runValues = [];
  let expectedRow = null;

  function flushRun_() {
    if (runStart == null || runValues.length === 0) return;
    sheet.getRange(runStart, 4, runValues.length, 5).setValues(runValues);
    applied += runValues.length;
    runStart = null;
    runValues = [];
    expectedRow = null;
  }

  sorted.forEach(update => {
    const rowNum = Number(update.row);
    if (!Number.isFinite(rowNum) || rowNum < 2) return;
    if (runStart == null) {
      runStart = rowNum;
      expectedRow = rowNum;
    }
    if (rowNum !== expectedRow) {
      flushRun_();
      runStart = rowNum;
      expectedRow = rowNum;
    }
    runValues.push(update.values);
    expectedRow++;
  });

  flushRun_();
  return applied;
}

function previewRollbackMiddayOhlcv20260511() {
  return rollbackMiddayOhlcvRun_("2026-05-11", true, { deleteLookbackWindow: true });
}

function rollbackMiddayOhlcv20260511() {
  return rollbackMiddayOhlcvRun_("2026-05-11", false, { deleteLookbackWindow: true });
}

function previewRollbackMiddayOhlcvRun(dateKey) {
  return rollbackMiddayOhlcvRun_(dateKey, true, { deleteLookbackWindow: true });
}

function rollbackMiddayOhlcvRun(dateKey) {
  return rollbackMiddayOhlcvRun_(dateKey, false, { deleteLookbackWindow: true });
}

function rollbackMiddayOhlcvRun_(dateKey, dryRun, options) {
  const DATA_ROW = 2;
  const CHUNK_ROWS = 5000;
  const LIMIT_MS = 4 * 60 * 1000;
  const GUARD_MS = 30 * 1000;
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  const resume = options && options.resume === true;

  let state = resume ? readMiddayRollbackState_() : null;
  if (!state) {
    dryRun = dryRun !== false;
    options = options || {};
    const targetDateKey = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(dateKey);
    const marker = makeMiddayRefreshId_(targetDateKey);
    const lookbackEnd = new Date(`${targetDateKey}T23:59:59+09:00`);
    const lookbackStart = new Date(lookbackEnd.getTime() - OHLCV_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

    clearMiddayRollbackState_();
    state = {
      version: 1,
      phase: "scan_marker",
      dateKey: targetDateKey,
      marker: marker,
      dryRun: dryRun,
      deleteLookbackWindow: options.deleteLookbackWindow !== false,
      lookbackStartMs: lookbackStart.getTime(),
      lookbackEndMs: lookbackEnd.getTime(),
      nextRow: 0,
      lastRow: 0,
      markerCandidates: 0,
      lookbackWindowCandidates: 0,
      deletedRows: 0,
      scannedRows: 0,
      dateCounts: {},
      samples: [],
      startedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
  }

  const ss = openAppSpreadsheet_();
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) throw new Error("ohlcv_4h シートが見つかりません");

  if (!state.nextRow) state.nextRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (!state.lastRow) state.lastRow = sh.getLastRow();

  let symbols = readMiddayRollbackSymbols_();
  const symbolSet = {};
  symbols.forEach(sym => { if (sym) symbolSet[sym] = true; });

  if (state.phase === "scan_marker") {
    while (state.nextRow >= DATA_ROW) {
      if (Date.now() - startedAt > LIMIT_MS - GUARD_MS) {
        symbols = Object.keys(symbolSet).sort();
        writeMiddayRollbackSymbols_(symbols);
        return pauseMiddayRollback_(state, symbols, false);
      }

      const endRow = state.nextRow;
      const startRow = Math.max(DATA_ROW, endRow - CHUNK_ROWS + 1);
      const numRows = endRow - startRow + 1;
      const values = sh.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, 3).getValues();

      values.forEach((row, index) => {
        const alertId = String(row[1] || "").trim();
        if (alertId !== state.marker) return;

        const sheetRow = startRow + index;
        const ts = row[0];
        const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
        state.markerCandidates++;
        if (symbol) symbolSet[symbol] = true;

        if (state.samples.length < 20) {
          state.samples.push({
            row: sheetRow,
            timestamp: ts instanceof Date && !isNaN(ts.getTime())
              ? Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm")
              : String(ts || ""),
            symbol: symbol,
            alertId: alertId,
            reason: "midday_marker"
          });
        }
      });

      state.scannedRows += numRows;
      state.nextRow = startRow - 1;
    }

    symbols = Object.keys(symbolSet).sort();
    writeMiddayRollbackSymbols_(symbols);
    state.phase = "delete_rows";
    state.nextRow = sh.getLastRow();
    state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    writeMiddayRollbackState_(state);
  }

  if (state.phase === "delete_rows") {
    while (state.nextRow >= DATA_ROW) {
      if (Date.now() - startedAt > LIMIT_MS - GUARD_MS) {
        symbols = Object.keys(symbolSet).sort();
        writeMiddayRollbackSymbols_(symbols);
        return pauseMiddayRollback_(state, symbols, false);
      }

      const endRow = Math.min(state.nextRow, sh.getLastRow());
      if (endRow < DATA_ROW) break;
      const startRow = Math.max(DATA_ROW, endRow - CHUNK_ROWS + 1);
      const numRows = endRow - startRow + 1;
      const values = sh.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, 3).getValues();
      const deleteRows = [];

      values.forEach((row, index) => {
        const sheetRow = startRow + index;
        const ts = row[0];
        const alertId = String(row[1] || "").trim();
        const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
        const isMarkerRow = alertId === state.marker;
        let shouldDelete = isMarkerRow;

        if (!shouldDelete && state.deleteLookbackWindow && symbolSet[symbol]) {
          shouldDelete = ts instanceof Date &&
            !isNaN(ts.getTime()) &&
            ts.getTime() >= state.lookbackStartMs &&
            ts.getTime() <= state.lookbackEndMs;
        }
        if (!shouldDelete) return;

        deleteRows.push(sheetRow);
        const tsKey = ts instanceof Date && !isNaN(ts.getTime()) ? formatYmd_(ts) : "invalid";
        state.dateCounts[tsKey] = (state.dateCounts[tsKey] || 0) + 1;
        if (isMarkerRow) {
          if (!symbolSet[symbol] && symbol) symbolSet[symbol] = true;
        } else {
          state.lookbackWindowCandidates++;
        }

        if (state.samples.length < 20) {
          state.samples.push({
            row: sheetRow,
            timestamp: ts instanceof Date && !isNaN(ts.getTime())
              ? Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm")
              : String(ts || ""),
            symbol: symbol,
            alertId: alertId,
            reason: isMarkerRow ? "midday_marker" : "lookback_window"
          });
        }
      });

      if (!state.dryRun && deleteRows.length > 0) {
        deleteRowsByRowNums_(sh, deleteRows);
        SpreadsheetApp.flush();
      }
      state.deletedRows += deleteRows.length;
      state.nextRow = startRow - 1;
      state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      writeMiddayRollbackState_(state);
    }
  }

  symbols = Object.keys(symbolSet).sort();
  if (!state.dryRun && symbols.length > 0) {
    setOhlcvRepairSymbols_(symbols);
  }
  if (!state.dryRun) clearMiddayOhlcvState_();

  const summary = {
    ok: true,
    complete: true,
    dryRun: state.dryRun,
    marker: state.marker,
    deleteLookbackWindow: state.deleteLookbackWindow,
    lookbackStart: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(state.lookbackStartMs), CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm"),
    lookbackEnd: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(state.lookbackEndMs), CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm"),
    markerCandidates: state.markerCandidates,
    lookbackWindowCandidates: state.lookbackWindowCandidates,
    deleteCandidates: state.deletedRows,
    deletedRows: state.dryRun ? 0 : state.deletedRows,
    queuedRepairSymbols: state.dryRun ? 0 : symbols.length,
    repairSymbols: symbols.length,
    sampleSymbols: symbols.slice(0, 30),
    dateCounts: state.dateCounts,
    samples: state.samples
  };

  clearMiddayRollbackState_();
  console.log(`[rollbackMidday] complete marker=${summary.marker} dryRun=${summary.dryRun} deleteCandidates=${summary.deleteCandidates} repairSymbols=${summary.repairSymbols}`);
  return summary;
}

function resumeMiddayOhlcvRollback() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_RESUME_HANDLER);
  const state = readMiddayRollbackState_();
  if (!state) {
    console.log("[rollbackMidday] 再開状態がないため終了します");
    return { ok: true, skipped: true, reason: "no_state" };
  }
  return rollbackMiddayOhlcvRun_(state.dateKey, state.dryRun === true, { resume: true });
}

function resetMiddayOhlcvRollbackState() {
  clearMiddayRollbackState_();
  console.log("[rollbackMidday] rollback state cleared");
}

function pauseMiddayRollback_(state, symbols, forceSchedule) {
  state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  writeMiddayRollbackState_(state);
  writeMiddayRollbackSymbols_(symbols);
  if (!state.dryRun || forceSchedule === true) {
    scheduleMiddayRollbackResume_(60 * 1000);
  }
  const result = {
    ok: true,
    incomplete: true,
    dryRun: state.dryRun,
    phase: state.phase,
    nextRow: state.nextRow,
    marker: state.marker,
    markerCandidates: state.markerCandidates,
    lookbackWindowCandidates: state.lookbackWindowCandidates,
    deleteCandidatesSoFar: state.deletedRows,
    repairSymbols: symbols.length
  };
  console.log(`[rollbackMidday] paused ${JSON.stringify(result)}`);
  return result;
}

function scheduleMiddayRollbackResume_(delayMs) {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_RESUME_HANDLER);
  ScriptApp.newTrigger(MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_RESUME_HANDLER)
    .timeBased()
    .after(delayMs)
    .create();
}

function readMiddayRollbackState_() {
  try {
    const raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_STATE_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

function writeMiddayRollbackState_(state) {
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
}

function readMiddayRollbackSymbols_() {
  try {
    const raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_SYMBOLS_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw).map(sym => String(sym || "").trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];
  } catch (e) {
    return [];
  }
}

function writeMiddayRollbackSymbols_(symbols) {
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(
    MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_SYMBOLS_KEY,
    JSON.stringify((symbols || []).map(sym => String(sym || "").trim()).filter(Boolean).sort())
  );
}

function clearMiddayRollbackState_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.deleteProperty(MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_STATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_SYMBOLS_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_ROLLBACK_RESUME_HANDLER);
}

function repairBlankTimestampOhlcvRowsNow() {
  const ss = openAppSpreadsheet_();
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) throw new Error("ohlcv_4h シートが見つかりません");

  const scanRows = Math.max(1, sh.getLastRow() - 1);

  const result = purgeInvalidOhlcvTimestampRows_(sh, "manual.blank_timestamp_repair", scanRows);

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  clearMiddayOhlcvState_();

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "timestamp空欄行の削除と再取得キュー登録が完了しました。次回OHLCV取得で120日分を再取得します。", result);

  return result;
}

function clearMiddayOhlcvState_(options) {
  const opts = options || {};
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  props.deleteProperty(MIDDAY_STATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(MIDDAY_SYMBOL_LIST_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(MIDDAY_NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(MIDDAY_LAST_TS_MAP_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(MIDDAY_REFRESH_ID_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(MIDDAY_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY);

  if (opts.keepTrigger !== true) deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_RESUME_HANDLER);
}

function hasMiddayOhlcvState_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  return !!(props.getProperty(MIDDAY_STATE_KEY) ||
    props.getProperty(MIDDAY_SYMBOL_LIST_KEY) ||
    props.getProperty(MIDDAY_NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY) ||
    props.getProperty(MIDDAY_LAST_TS_MAP_KEY) ||
    props.getProperty(MIDDAY_REFRESH_ID_KEY) ||
    props.getProperty(MIDDAY_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY));
}

function finalizeMiddayOhlcvBeforeMainRun_() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(MIDDAY_RESUME_HANDLER);
  if (!hasMiddayOhlcvState_()) return;
  try {
    const ss = openAppSpreadsheet_();
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
    if (sh) {
      SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    }
  } catch (e) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "16:00本番開始前の13:30先行取得整理でエラーが発生しました。状態だけクリアして本番処理を続行します。", {
      message: e && e.message ? e.message : String(e)
    });
  }
  clearMiddayOhlcvState_();
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "16:00本番開始前に13:30先行取得の再開状態をクリアしました。", {});
}

// 変更後
function fetchOHLCVForNewAlertsInternal_(outerStartedAt) {
  const today = getOhlcvBusinessDate_();
  const todayKey = formatYmd_(today);
  if (isMarketHolidayJst_(today, getMarketHolidaySet_())) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `基準日 ${todayKey} は休場日（土日祝・年末年始）のため、OHLCV取得をスキップします。`, {
      businessDate: todayKey,
      isManual: !!getManualOhlcvBusinessDateKey_()
    });
    resetProcessProperties_();
    clearManualOhlcvBusinessDate_();
    return;
  }
  const startTime = outerStartedAt || Date.now();
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  let phase = props.getProperty(PHASE_KEY) || "PHASE1";
  const LIMIT_MS = 4 * 60 * 1000;
  const ss = openAppSpreadsheet_();
  const rawSheet = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.RAW_SHEET);
  const ohlcvSheet = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  const refreshId = props.getProperty("CURRENT_REFRESH_ID") || "new_session";
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `${phase}: 基準日 ${todayKey} として処理を開始します。`, { phase: phase, refreshId: refreshId, businessDate: todayKey });
  debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase.summary", { event: "phase_start", phase: phase, refreshId: refreshId });

if (phase === "PHASE1") {
  const rawAlertVolumeMapData = buildRawAlertVolumeMapForBusinessDate_(rawSheet, todayKey);
  const rawAlertVolumeMap = rawAlertVolumeMapData.volumeMap;

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "alerts_raw のvolumeを16:00本番取得の出来高上書き用に読み込みました。", rawAlertVolumeMapData.stats);

  // 以降、今までのPHASE1処理
    let symbols = JSON.parse(props.getProperty(SYMBOL_LIST_KEY) || "[]");
    let lastTsMap = JSON.parse(props.getProperty("LAST_TS_MAP") || "{}");
    let fullBackfillSymbols = JSON.parse(props.getProperty(MAIN_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY) || "[]");
    let fullBackfillSet = new Set(fullBackfillSymbols);
    let newAlertCount = safeParseInt_(props.getProperty(NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY), 0);

    // ★★★ 追加: 修復対象銘柄を読み取る ★★★
    const repairSymsJson = props.getProperty("OHLCV_REPAIR_SYMBOLS");
    const repairSymsSet = repairSymsJson ? new Set(JSON.parse(repairSymsJson)) : new Set();
    if (repairSymsSet.size > 0) {
      writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `PHASE1: 修復対象 ${repairSymsSet.size} 銘柄を取得対象に追加します。既存OHLCVがある銘柄はlastTs以降のみ取得します。`, {
        repairSymbols: repairSymsSet.size
      });
    }
    // ★★★ 追加ここまで ★★★

    if (symbols.length === 0) {
  const rawData = rawSheet.getRange(5, 1, Math.max(1, rawSheet.getLastRow() - 4), RAW_HEADERS.length).getValues();

  const uniqueSymbols = new Set();
  const todaySet = new Set();

  const businessDateKey = getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_();

  rawData.forEach(r => {
    const signalType = String(r[RAW_IDX.signal_type] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (signalType !== "BOTTOM") return;
    const sym = String(r[RAW_IDX.symbol_code] || "").trim();
    if (!sym) return;

    uniqueSymbols.add(sym);

    const signalDate = r[RAW_IDX.signal_date];
    const signalDateKey = signalDate instanceof Date
      ? Utilities.formatDate(signalDate, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd")
      : String(signalDate || "").slice(0, 10);

    if (signalDateKey === businessDateKey) {
      todaySet.add(sym);
    }
  });

  // 修復対象銘柄も、今回の取得対象に必ず含める
repairSymsSet.forEach(sym => {
  const s = String(sym || "").trim();
  if (s) uniqueSymbols.add(s);
});

symbols = Array.from(uniqueSymbols).sort();

lastTsMap = buildLastTsMapFromOhlcv_(ohlcvSheet, symbols, new Set());

// 本当の120日取得対象 = OHLCVが存在しない銘柄だけ
// 修復対象でも、既存OHLCVがあれば lastTs 以降だけ取得する
const trueNewFullBackfillSymbols = symbols.filter(sym => {
  const s = String(sym || "").trim();
  return s && !lastTsMap[s];
});
fullBackfillSet = new Set(trueNewFullBackfillSymbols);
todaySet.forEach(sym => {
  const s = String(sym || "").trim();
  if (!s) return;
  if (!lastTsMap[s]) fullBackfillSet.add(s);
});
fullBackfillSymbols = Array.from(fullBackfillSet).sort();

const repairDeltaSymbols = symbols.filter(sym => {
  const s = String(sym || "").trim();
  return s && repairSymsSet.has(s) && !!lastTsMap[s];
});

writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "PHASE1: OHLCV取得対象を差分取得型にしました。OHLCV未取得銘柄だけ120日分、既存銘柄は修復対象でもlastTs以降のみ取得します。", {
  totalSymbols: symbols.length,
  trueNewFullBackfillSymbols: trueNewFullBackfillSymbols.length,
  repairDeltaSymbols: repairDeltaSymbols.length,
  existingDeltaSymbols: symbols.length - trueNewFullBackfillSymbols.length,
  todaySignalSymbols: todaySet.size
});

props.setProperties({
  "LAST_TS_MAP": JSON.stringify(lastTsMap),
  [SYMBOL_LIST_KEY]: JSON.stringify(symbols),
  [MAIN_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY]: JSON.stringify(fullBackfillSymbols),
  [STATE_KEY]: "0",
  [NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY]: String(todaySet.size)
});

props.deleteProperty("OHLCV_REPAIR_SYMBOLS");

newAlertCount = todaySet.size;
}

    let startIndex = safeParseInt_(props.getProperty(STATE_KEY), 0);
let allFetchedData = [];
let batchNo = 0;

for (let i = startIndex; i < symbols.length; i += OHLCV_BATCH_SIZE) {
  const elapsedBeforeBatch = Date.now() - startTime;
  if (elapsedBeforeBatch > LIMIT_MS || (LIMIT_MS - elapsedBeforeBatch) < FETCH_GUARD_MS) {
    if (allFetchedData.length > 0) appendRowsToSheet_(ohlcvSheet, allFetchedData);
    props.setProperty(STATE_KEY, i.toString());
    props.setProperty(NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY, newAlertCount.toString());
    props.setProperty("LAST_TS_MAP", JSON.stringify(lastTsMap));
    props.setProperty(MAIN_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY, JSON.stringify(Array.from(fullBackfillSet)));
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `PHASE1: 実行時間が近づいたため、${i}/${symbols.length} 銘柄までの進捗を保存して再開します。`, {
      cursor: i,
      totalSymbols: symbols.length,
      elapsedMs: elapsedBeforeBatch
    });
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase.summary", {
      event: "phase_pause",
      phase: "PHASE1",
      cursor: i,
      totalSymbols: symbols.length,
      elapsedMs: elapsedBeforeBatch
    });
    setupResumeTrigger_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
    return;
  }

      const batchStart = Date.now();
      const batch = symbols.slice(i, Math.min(i + OHLCV_BATCH_SIZE, symbols.length));
      const requests = [];
      const reqMeta = [];
      batch.forEach(sym => {
        try {
          const useLastTs = fullBackfillSet.has(sym) ? null : (lastTsMap[sym] || null);
          const pair = buildOhlcvRequestPairForEndMillis_(sym, useLastTs, getOhlcvRunEndMillis_(), null, null, { forcePeriod: true });

const dailyIdx = requests.length;
requests.push({
  url: pair.dailyUrl,
  muteHttpExceptions: true,
  headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0" }
});

const intraIdx = requests.length;
requests.push({
  url: pair.intraUrl,
  muteHttpExceptions: true,
  headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0" }
});

reqMeta.push({
  symbol: sym,
  dailyIdx: dailyIdx,
  intraIdx: intraIdx
});
        } catch (e) {
          writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `PHASE1: ${sym} のYahoo Finance取得URLを作れませんでした。`, { symbol: sym, message: e.message });
        }
      });
      batchNo++;
      let responses = null;
      let parseErrCount = 0;
      let fetchedRowsInBatch = 0;
      let fetchedSymbolsInBatch = 0;
      let batchFirstTs = null;
      let batchLastTs = null;
      const noRowsSymbols = [];
      const sparseResultSamples = [];
      try {
        responses = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(requests);
      } catch (e) {
        if (isQuotaExhausted_(e)) {
          writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `PHASE1: Google側の実行制限に当たったため、バッチ ${batchNo} から4時間後に再開します。`, { batchNo: batchNo, message: e.message });
          debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase.error", { phase: "PHASE1", stage: "quota_exhausted", batchNo: batchNo, message: e.message });
          if (allFetchedData.length > 0) appendRowsToSheet_(ohlcvSheet, allFetchedData);
          props.setProperty(STATE_KEY, i.toString());
          props.setProperty(NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY, newAlertCount.toString());
          props.setProperty("LAST_TS_MAP", JSON.stringify(lastTsMap));
          props.setProperty(MAIN_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY, JSON.stringify(Array.from(fullBackfillSet)));
          deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
          ScriptApp.newTrigger("resumeOHLCVFetch").timeBased().after(4 * 60 * 60 * 1000).create();
          return;
        }
        writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `PHASE1: バッチ ${batchNo} のOHLCV取得に失敗しました。次の処理に進みます。`, { batchNo: batchNo, message: e.message });
        debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase.error", { phase: "PHASE1", stage: "batch_fetch", batchNo: batchNo, message: e.message });
      }
      if (responses && responses.length > 0) {
        reqMeta.forEach(meta => {
          try {
            const dailyRes = responses[meta.dailyIdx];
const intraRes = responses[meta.intraIdx];

const dailyMap = buildDailyOhlcvMapFromYahooResponse_(dailyRes);
