// 16:00本番では、今日分だけ parseIntraResponse_ 内の日足補正を外す。
// 今日分のAM/PM補正は、既存AM行の有無を見て後段で制御する。
const dailyMapForParse = Object.assign({}, dailyMap);
delete dailyMapForParse[todayKey];

const result = parseIntraResponse_(meta.symbol, intraRes, null, dailyMapForParse);
const validRowsRaw = applyPhase1ResultRows_(meta.symbol, result, refreshId, lastTsMap);

let validRows = prepareMainRunRowsFastPmVolume_(
  meta.symbol,
  validRowsRaw,
  dailyMap,
  todayKey,
  rawAlertVolumeMap
);

// alerts_raw J列 volume で、該当シグナル足のvolumeだけ上書きする
if (validRows.length > 0) {
  allFetchedData = allFetchedData.concat(validRows);
  fetchedRowsInBatch += validRows.length;
  fetchedSymbolsInBatch++;
              validRows.forEach(row => {
                const ts = row && row.length ? row[0] : null;
                if (!(ts instanceof Date)) return;
                if (!batchFirstTs || ts.getTime() < batchFirstTs.getTime()) batchFirstTs = ts;
                if (!batchLastTs || ts.getTime() > batchLastTs.getTime()) batchLastTs = ts;
              });
              if (validRows.length < 2 && sparseResultSamples.length < 5) {
                sparseResultSamples.push({ symbol: meta.symbol, rows: validRows.length });
              }
            } else if (noRowsSymbols.length < 5) {
              noRowsSymbols.push(meta.symbol);
            }
          } catch (e) {
            parseErrCount++;
            writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `PHASE1: ${meta.symbol} のYahoo FinanceレスポンスをOHLCV行に変換できませんでした。`, { symbol: meta.symbol, message: e.message });
          }
        });
      }
      debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase1.batch", {
        batchNo: batchNo,
        startIndex: i,
        symbolsInBatch: batch.length,
        requestCount: requests.length,
        fetchedRows: fetchedRowsInBatch,
        symbolsWithRows: fetchedSymbolsInBatch,
        symbolsWithoutRows: reqMeta.length - fetchedSymbolsInBatch,
        noRowsSamples: noRowsSymbols,
        sparseResultSamples: sparseResultSamples,
        dateRange: batchFirstTs && batchLastTs ? `${batchFirstTs.toISOString()} ~ ${batchLastTs.toISOString()}` : "none",
        parseErrCount: parseErrCount,
        elapsedMs: Date.now() - batchStart
      });
      Utilities.sleep(OHLCV_BATCH_SLEEP_MS);
    }

    if (allFetchedData.length > 0) appendRowsToSheet_(ohlcvSheet, allFetchedData);

    props.setProperties({ [PHASE_KEY]: "PHASE2", [STATE_KEY]: "5", [NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY]: newAlertCount.toString(), "LAST_TS_MAP": JSON.stringify(lastTsMap), [MAIN_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY]: JSON.stringify(Array.from(fullBackfillSet)) });
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "PHASE1: OHLCV取得が完了しました。次は株式分割チェック（PHASE2）へ進みます。", {
      totalSymbols: symbols.length,
      fetchedRows: allFetchedData.length,
      newAlertCount: newAlertCount
    });
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase.summary", {
      event: "phase_complete",
      phase: "PHASE1",
      totalSymbols: symbols.length,
      fetchedRows: allFetchedData.length,
      newAlertCount: newAlertCount,
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - startTime
    });
    setupResumeTrigger_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
    return;
  }

  if (phase === "PHASE2") {
    let cursor = Math.max(5, safeParseInt_(props.getProperty(STATE_KEY), 5));
    const persistedSplitCache = JSON.parse(props.getProperty("OHLCV_SPLIT_CACHE") || "{}");
    const lastRow = rawSheet.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < cursor) {
      props.setProperties({ [PHASE_KEY]: "PHASE3", [STATE_KEY]: "0" });
      debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase.summary", {
        event: "phase_complete",
        phase: "PHASE2",
        elapsedMs: Date.now() - startTime
      });
      setupResumeTrigger_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
      return;
    }
    let chunkNo = 0;
    while (cursor <= lastRow) {
      const elapsedBeforeChunk = Date.now() - startTime;
      if (elapsedBeforeChunk > LIMIT_MS || (LIMIT_MS - elapsedBeforeChunk) < FETCH_GUARD_MS) {
        props.setProperty(STATE_KEY, cursor.toString());
        debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase.summary", {
          event: "phase_pause",
          phase: "PHASE2",
          cursor: cursor,
          elapsedMs: elapsedBeforeChunk
        });
        setupResumeTrigger_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
        return;
      }
      const chunkStartRow = cursor;
      const chunkEndRow = Math.min(lastRow, chunkStartRow + PHASE2_ROW_CHUNK_SIZE - 1);
      const chunkSize = chunkEndRow - chunkStartRow + 1;
      const rawData = rawSheet.getRange(chunkStartRow, 1, chunkSize, RAW_HEADERS.length).getValues();
      const splitCache = {};
      const seen = {};
      rawData.forEach(r => {
        const sym = String(r[RAW_IDX.symbol_code] || "").trim();
        if (sym && !seen[sym]) {
          seen[sym] = true;
          if (persistedSplitCache[sym]) splitCache[sym] = expandSplitInfo_(persistedSplitCache[sym]);
        }
      });
      const uniqueSymbols = Object.keys(seen).filter(sym => !splitCache[sym]);
      let fetchBatches = 0;
      const fetchStart = Date.now();
      let timedOutDuringSplitFetch = false;
      for (let si = 0; si < uniqueSymbols.length; si += PHASE2_SPLIT_BATCH_SIZE) {
        const fetchElapsed = Date.now() - startTime;
        if (fetchElapsed > LIMIT_MS || (LIMIT_MS - fetchElapsed) < FETCH_GUARD_MS) {
          timedOutDuringSplitFetch = true;
          break;
        }
        const symBatch = uniqueSymbols.slice(si, Math.min(si + PHASE2_SPLIT_BATCH_SIZE, uniqueSymbols.length));
        const requests = symBatch.map(sym => {
          return {
            url: buildYahooFinanceDataUrl_(sym),
            muteHttpExceptions: true,
            headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0" }
          };
        });
        let responses = null;
        try {
          responses = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(requests);
          fetchBatches++;
        } catch (e) {
          if (isQuotaExhausted_(e)) {
            writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `PHASE2: 株式分割情報の取得中にGoogle側の実行制限へ当たりました。行 ${chunkStartRow} から4時間後に再開します。`, {
              rowStart: chunkStartRow,
              batchNo: fetchBatches + 1,
              message: e.message
            });
            debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase.error", { phase: "PHASE2", stage: "quota_exhausted", rowStart: chunkStartRow, batchNo: fetchBatches + 1, message: e.message });
            props.setProperty(STATE_KEY, chunkStartRow.toString());
            deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
            ScriptApp.newTrigger("resumeOHLCVFetch").timeBased().after(4 * 60 * 60 * 1000).create();
            return;
          }
          writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `PHASE2: 行 ${chunkStartRow} からの株式分割情報バッチ取得に失敗しました。`, {
            rowStart: chunkStartRow,
            batchNo: fetchBatches + 1,
            message: e.message
          });
          debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase.error", {
            phase: "PHASE2",
            stage: "split_batch_fetch",
            rowStart: chunkStartRow,
            batchNo: fetchBatches + 1,
            message: e.message
          });
        }
        if (responses) {
          symBatch.forEach((sym, idx) => {
            const info = parseYahooFinanceDataResponse_(sym, responses[idx]);
            splitCache[sym] = info;
            persistedSplitCache[sym] = minifySplitInfo_(info);
          });
          safeSetSplitCache_(props, persistedSplitCache, Object.keys(seen));
        }
        Utilities.sleep(PHASE2_SPLIT_BATCH_SLEEP_MS);
      }
      if (timedOutDuringSplitFetch) {
        props.setProperty(STATE_KEY, chunkStartRow.toString());
        debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase.summary", {
          event: "phase_pause",
          phase: "PHASE2",
          cursor: chunkStartRow,
          reason: "split_fetch_guard",
          elapsedMs: Date.now() - startTime
        });
        setupResumeTrigger_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
        return;
      }
      const modifiedRows = [];
      let adjustedRows = 0;
      const adjustedSymbolMap = {};
      rawData.forEach((row, idx) => {
        const symbol = String(row[RAW_IDX.symbol_code] || "").trim();
        if (!symbol) return;
        const splitInfo = splitCache[symbol];
        if (!splitInfo) return;
        if (applySplitAdjustToRawRow_(row, splitInfo)) {
          addSplitQueueItem_(props, symbol, splitInfo.splitRatio, splitInfo.splitDate);
          modifiedRows.push(idx);
          adjustedRows++;
          const splitKey = `${symbol}|${splitInfo.splitDate}|${splitInfo.splitRatio}`;
          if (!adjustedSymbolMap[splitKey]) {
            adjustedSymbolMap[splitKey] = {
              symbol: symbol,
              splitDate: splitInfo.splitDate,
              splitDateKey: splitInfo.splitDate ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(splitInfo.splitDate * 1000), CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd") : "",
              ratio: splitInfo.splitRatio,
              adjustedRows: 0
            };
          }
          adjustedSymbolMap[splitKey].adjustedRows++;
        }
      });
      writeDirtyRowsBatched_(rawSheet, chunkStartRow, rawData, modifiedRows, RAW_HEADERS.length);
      const nextCursor = chunkEndRow + 1;
      props.setProperty(STATE_KEY, nextCursor.toString());
      chunkNo++;
      const adjustedSymbols = Object.keys(adjustedSymbolMap).sort().map(key => adjustedSymbolMap[key]);
      debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase2.chunk", {
        chunkNo: chunkNo,
        rowStart: chunkStartRow,
        rowEnd: chunkEndRow,
        uniqueSymbols: uniqueSymbols.length,
        splitFetchBatches: fetchBatches,
        splitFetchMs: Date.now() - fetchStart,
        adjustedRows: adjustedRows,
        adjustedSymbolCount: adjustedSymbols.length,
        adjustedSymbols: adjustedSymbols,
        nextCursor: nextCursor,
        elapsedMs: Date.now() - startTime
      });
      cursor = nextCursor;
    }
    props.deleteProperty("OHLCV_SPLIT_CACHE");
    props.setProperties({ [PHASE_KEY]: "PHASE3", [STATE_KEY]: "0" });
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase.summary", {
      event: "phase_complete",
      phase: "PHASE2",
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - startTime
    });
    setupResumeTrigger_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
    return;
  }

  if (phase === "PHASE3") {
    const isDone = processSplitQueueBatch_(startTime, LIMIT_MS);
    if (!isDone) {
      writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "PHASE3: 分割調整キューの適用が途中です。進捗を保存して再開します。", {});
      setupResumeTrigger_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
      return;
    }
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "PHASE3: 分割調整キューの適用が完了しました。次は重複排除と並べ替え（PHASE4）です。", {});
    props.setProperties({ [PHASE_KEY]: "PHASE4", [STATE_KEY]: "0" });
    setupResumeTrigger_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
    return;
  }

if (phase === "PHASE4") {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeOHLCVFetch");

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "PHASE4超軽量版: OHLCVシートの掃除処理を行わず、取得フローを完了します。", {});

  const phase4Result = finishOhlcvPhase4UltraLight_(ohlcvSheet, todayKey, startTime);

  const newCount = props.getProperty(NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY) || "0";

  resetProcessProperties_();

  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeOHLCVFetchMidday");

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "OHLCV取得フローが完了しました。続いて日次メンテナンスを1分後に起動します。", {
    newRowsOrSymbols: newCount,
    refreshId: refreshId,
    phase4Mode: "ultra_light",
    phase4Result: phase4Result
  });

  debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase4.complete", {
    newCount: newCount,
    refreshId: refreshId,
    phase4Mode: "ultra_light",
    phase4Result: phase4Result,
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - startTime
  });

  props.setProperty(DAILY_MAINT_NEW_COUNT_KEY, newCount);
  props.setProperty(DAILY_MAINT_REFRESH_ID_KEY, refreshId);

  deleteTriggersByHandler_("runDailyMaintenanceTrigger");

  ScriptApp.newTrigger("runDailyMaintenanceTrigger")
    .timeBased()
    .after(5 * 1000)
    .create();

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "日次メンテナンスのワンショットトリガーを5秒後に設定しました。", {
    handlerName: "runDailyMaintenanceTrigger",
    phase4Mode: "ultra_light"
  });

  return;
}
}

function prepareMainRunRowsFastPmVolume_(symbol, rows, dailyMap, todayKey, rawAlertVolumeMap) {
  const fastSym = String(symbol || "").trim();
  const fastNormalizedRows = (rows || []).map(row => normalizeOhlcvRowForWrite_(row)).filter(Boolean);
  if (!fastSym || !todayKey) return fastNormalizedRows;

  const fastTodayRows = [];
  const fastOtherRows = [];
  fastNormalizedRows.forEach(row => {
    const ts = row[0];
    const rowSym = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (rowSym === fastSym && formatYmd_(ts) === todayKey) {
      fastTodayRows.push(row);
    } else {
      fastOtherRows.push(row);
    }
  });

  const fastDaily = dailyMap && dailyMap[todayKey];
  const fastDailyVol = Math.round(Number(fastDaily && fastDaily.volume));
  const fastDailyClose = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(fastDaily && fastDaily.close);
  if (!Number.isFinite(fastDailyVol) || fastDailyVol < 0 || fastDailyClose === null) {
    return fastOtherRows.concat(sortTodaySessionRows_(fastTodayRows));
  }

  const fastAmRow = fastTodayRows.find(row => getOhlcvSessionName_(row[0]) === "AM") || null;
  let fastPmRow = fastTodayRows.find(row => getOhlcvSessionName_(row[0]) === "PM") || null;
  const fastAmTimestamp = new Date(`${todayKey}T09:00:00+09:00`);
  const fastRawAmVol = rawAlertVolumeMap ? Number(rawAlertVolumeMap[makeRawAlertVolumeKey_(fastAmTimestamp, fastSym)]) : NaN;
  const fastUseProtectedAmVol = Number.isFinite(fastRawAmVol) && fastRawAmVol >= 0;
  const fastAmVol = fastUseProtectedAmVol ? Math.round(fastRawAmVol) : (fastAmRow ? toSafeVolumeNumber_(fastAmRow[7]) : 0);
  const fastHasAmVolume = !!fastAmRow || fastUseProtectedAmVol;
  const fastPmTimestamp = new Date(`${todayKey}T13:00:00+09:00`);
  const fastRawPmVol = rawAlertVolumeMap ? Number(rawAlertVolumeMap[makeRawAlertVolumeKey_(fastPmTimestamp, fastSym)]) : NaN;
  const fastUseProtectedPmVol = Number.isFinite(fastRawPmVol) && fastRawPmVol >= 0;
  const fastHasValidDailyDiff = !fastHasAmVolume || fastDailyVol >= fastAmVol;
  const fastPmVolAfter = fastHasValidDailyDiff ? Math.max(0, Math.round(fastDailyVol - fastAmVol)) : null;

  if (fastPmRow) {
    if (fastUseProtectedPmVol) {
      const fastPmBefore = toSafeVolumeNumber_(fastPmRow[7]);
      fastPmRow[7] = Math.round(fastRawPmVol);
      fastPmRow[1] = makePmLockedMarker_(todayKey);
      debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.mainToday.pmVolumePmLocked", {
        symbol: fastSym, todayKey: todayKey,
        pmBefore: fastPmBefore, pmAfter: fastPmRow[7], source: "alerts_raw_pm_locked"
      });
    } else if ((fastAmRow && fastHasValidDailyDiff) ||
        (!fastAmRow && (isDailyCompatibleWithSessionRow_(fastDaily, fastPmRow) || isSinglePriceDailyOhlcv_(fastDaily)))) {
      const fastPmBefore = toSafeVolumeNumber_(fastPmRow[7]);
      fastPmRow[7] = fastHasAmVolume ? fastPmVolAfter : fastDailyVol;
      debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.mainToday.pmVolumeAdjusted", {
        symbol: fastSym,
        todayKey: todayKey,
        dailyVol: fastDailyVol,
        amVol: fastAmVol,
        amVolumeSource: fastUseProtectedAmVol ? "alerts_raw_locked_am" : "fetched_intra_am",
        pmBefore: fastPmBefore,
        pmAfter: fastPmRow[7],
        mode: fastHasAmVolume ? "daily_minus_am" : "daily_compatible_pm_only"
      });
    }
    return fastOtherRows.concat(sortTodaySessionRows_(fastTodayRows));
  }

  if (fastUseProtectedPmVol) {
    fastPmRow = [
      new Date(`${todayKey}T13:00:00+09:00`),
      makePmLockedMarker_(todayKey),
      fastSym,
      fastDailyClose, fastDailyClose, fastDailyClose, fastDailyClose,
      Math.round(fastRawPmVol)
    ];
    fastTodayRows.push(fastPmRow);
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.mainToday.pmSynthesizedPmLocked", {
      symbol: fastSym, todayKey: todayKey,
      pmVol: Math.round(fastRawPmVol), close: fastDailyClose, source: "alerts_raw_pm_locked"
    });
  } else if (fastHasValidDailyDiff && fastPmVolAfter > 0 && (fastHasAmVolume || isSinglePriceDailyOhlcv_(fastDaily))) {
    const fastMarker = fastAmRow ? String(fastAmRow[1] || "new_session") : "new_session";
    fastPmRow = [
      new Date(`${todayKey}T13:00:00+09:00`),
      fastMarker,
      fastSym,
      fastDailyClose,
      fastDailyClose,
      fastDailyClose,
      fastDailyClose,
      fastPmVolAfter
    ];
    fastTodayRows.push(fastPmRow);
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.mainToday.pmSynthesizedFromDaily", {
      symbol: fastSym,
      todayKey: todayKey,
      dailyVol: fastDailyVol,
      amVol: fastAmVol,
      amVolumeSource: fastUseProtectedAmVol ? "alerts_raw_locked_am" : "fetched_intra_am",
      pmVol: fastPmVolAfter,
      close: fastDailyClose,
      reason: fastHasAmVolume ? "missing_pm_with_am" : "single_price_daily"
    });
  }

  return fastOtherRows.concat(sortTodaySessionRows_(fastTodayRows));

}

function toSafeVolumeNumber_(value) {
  const n = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.round(n) : 0;
}

function sortTodaySessionRows_(rows) {
  return (rows || []).slice().sort((a, b) => {
    const at = a && a[0] instanceof Date ? a[0].getTime() : 0;
    const bt = b && b[0] instanceof Date ? b[0].getTime() : 0;
    return at - bt;
  });
}

function isSinglePriceDailyOhlcv_(daily) {
  const open = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(daily && daily.open);
  const high = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(daily && daily.high);
  const low = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(daily && daily.low);
  const close = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(daily && daily.close);
  if (open === null || high === null || low === null || close === null) return false;
  return open === high && high === low && low === close;
}

function isDailyCompatibleWithSessionRow_(daily, row) {
  const dailyOpen = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(daily && daily.open);
  const dailyHigh = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(daily && daily.high);
  const dailyLow = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(daily && daily.low);
  const dailyClose = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(daily && daily.close);
  if (dailyOpen === null || dailyHigh === null || dailyLow === null || dailyClose === null) return false;
  if (!row || row.length < 7) return false;
  return Number(row[3]) === dailyOpen &&
    Number(row[4]) === dailyHigh &&
    Number(row[5]) === dailyLow &&
    Number(row[6]) === dailyClose;
}

// runDailyMaintenance を引数付きで起動するためのラッパー
function runDailyMaintenanceTrigger() {
  // GASの.after()トリガーは実行後も自動削除されないため、自分自身を明示的に削除
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("runDailyMaintenanceTrigger");
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const newCount = props.getProperty(DAILY_MAINT_NEW_COUNT_KEY) || "0";
  const refreshId = props.getProperty(DAILY_MAINT_REFRESH_ID_KEY) || "unknown";
  // 本体側で再開用メタデータとして保存し直す。
  props.deleteProperty(DAILY_MAINT_NEW_COUNT_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(DAILY_MAINT_REFRESH_ID_KEY);
  runDailyMaintenance(newCount, refreshId);
}

// quickRepairRecentGaps を起動するためのラッパー
function quickRepairTrigger() {
  // GASの.after()トリガーは実行後も自動削除されないため、自分自身を明示的に削除
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("quickRepairTrigger");
  quickRepairRecentGaps();
}

function processSplitQueueBatch_(startTime, limitMs) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  let queue = JSON.parse(props.getProperty("SPLIT_QUEUE") || "[]");
  if (queue.length === 0) return true;
  const ohlcvSheet = openAppSpreadsheet_().getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  const lastRow = ohlcvSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return true;

  while (queue.length > 0) {
    const target = queue[0];
    let startIndex = safeParseInt_(props.getProperty("SPLIT_INDEX"), 2);
    if (startIndex < 2) startIndex = 2;

    const matches = ohlcvSheet
      .getRange(2, 3, lastRow - 1, 1)
      .createTextFinder(String(target.symbol || ""))
      .matchEntireCell(true)
      .findAll();
    const rowNums = matches
      .map(r => r.getRow())
      .filter(rowNum => rowNum >= startIndex)
      .sort((a, b) => a - b);

    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase3.target", {
      symbol: target.symbol,
      splitDate: target.date,
      ratio: target.ratio,
      startIndex: startIndex,
      matchedRows: rowNums.length,
      lastRow: lastRow
    });

    // 連続する rowNum をまとめて読み書きすることで getValues/setValues 呼び出し回数を削減。
    // 連続しない場合は run 長 1 として個別に処理する（既存挙動と同じ）。
    let updatedRows = 0;
    let idx = 0;
    while (idx < rowNums.length) {
      const elapsed = Date.now() - startTime;
      if (elapsed > limitMs - FETCH_GUARD_MS) {
        props.setProperty("SPLIT_INDEX", String(startIndex));
        debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase3.pause", {
          symbol: target.symbol,
          splitIndex: startIndex,
          lastRow: lastRow,
          elapsedMs: elapsed
        });
        return false;
      }

      // 連続区間を検出
      let runEnd = idx;
      while (runEnd + 1 < rowNums.length && rowNums[runEnd + 1] === rowNums[runEnd] + 1) {
        runEnd++;
      }
      const runStartRow = rowNums[idx];
      const runLen = rowNums[runEnd] - runStartRow + 1;
      const rows = ohlcvSheet.getRange(runStartRow, 1, runLen, 8).getValues();

      let anyChanged = false;
      for (let r = 0; r < rows.length; r++) {
        const ts = rows[r][0];
        if ((ts instanceof Date) && (ts.getTime() / 1000 < target.date)) {
          for (let c = 3; c <= 6; c++) if (Number.isFinite(rows[r][c])) rows[r][c] = Math.round((rows[r][c] / target.ratio) * 100) / 100;
          if (Number.isFinite(rows[r][7])) rows[r][7] = Math.round(rows[r][7] * target.ratio);
          anyChanged = true;
          updatedRows++;
        }
      }
      if (anyChanged) {
        const writeData = rows.map(r => r.slice(3, 8));
        ohlcvSheet.getRange(runStartRow, 4, runLen, 5).setValues(writeData);
      }
      startIndex = rowNums[runEnd] + 1;
      props.setProperty("SPLIT_INDEX", String(startIndex));
      idx = runEnd + 1;
    }

    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase3.targetComplete", {
      symbol: target.symbol,
      matchedRows: rowNums.length,
      updatedRows: updatedRows,
      nextQueue: Math.max(0, queue.length - 1)
    });
    queue.shift();
    props.setProperty("SPLIT_QUEUE", JSON.stringify(queue));
    props.setProperty("SPLIT_INDEX", "2");
  }

  return true;
}

function resetProcessProperties_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.deleteProperty(PHASE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(STATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(SYMBOL_LIST_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(MAIN_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty("LAST_TS_MAP");
  props.deleteProperty("CURRENT_REFRESH_ID");
  props.deleteProperty("SPLIT_QUEUE");
  props.deleteProperty("SPLIT_INDEX");
  props.deleteProperty("OHLCV_SPLIT_CACHE");
  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_COMPLETION_NOTICE_PENDING_KEY);

  // 修復対象銘柄は、全PHASEが正常終了してリセットされるタイミングで消す
  props.deleteProperty("OHLCV_REPAIR_SYMBOLS");

  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
  clearMiddayOhlcvState_();
}

function isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts) {
  const MIN_VALID_DATE = new Date("1990-01-01T00:00:00Z");
  return ts instanceof Date && !isNaN(ts.getTime()) && ts >= MIN_VALID_DATE;
}

function jstDateToSheetSerial_(date) {
  const d = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(date);
  if (!d) return null;
  const y = Number(Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy"));
  const m = Number(Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "MM"));
  const day = Number(Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "dd"));
  const hour = Number(Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "HH"));
  const minute = Number(Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "mm"));
  const second = Number(Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "ss"));
  const epoch = Date.UTC(1899, 11, 30);
  const wholeDays = (Date.UTC(y, m - 1, day) - epoch) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  return wholeDays + (hour * 3600 + minute * 60 + second) / (24 * 60 * 60);
}

function sheetSerialToJstDate_(serial) {
  const n = Number(serial);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 20000 || n > 100000) return null;
  const wholeDays = Math.floor(n);
  const seconds = Math.round((n - wholeDays) * 24 * 60 * 60);
  const epoch = Date.UTC(1899, 11, 30);
  const d = new Date(epoch + wholeDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const y = d.getUTCFullYear();
  const m = d.getUTCMonth() + 1;
  const day = d.getUTCDate();
  const hour = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const minute = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
  const second = seconds % 60;
  const iso = y + "-" + String(m).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(day).padStart(2, "0") +
    "T" + String(hour).padStart(2, "0") + ":" + String(minute).padStart(2, "0") + ":" + String(second).padStart(2, "0") + "+09:00";
  const out = new Date(iso);
  return isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(out) ? out : null;
}

function formatOhlcvTimestampTextForSheet_(value) {
  const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(value);
  if (!ts) return "";

  const ymd = Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");

  // 09:00 / 13:00 のどちらかに固定
  const hour = Number(Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "HH"));
  const hhmm = hour < 13 ? "09:00" : "13:00";

  return ymd + " " + hhmm;
}

function normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(value) {
  if (isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(value)) return value;
  if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
    const serialDate = sheetSerialToJstDate_(value);
    if (serialDate) return serialDate;
    const ms = value > 1000000000000 ? value : value * 1000;
    const d = new Date(ms);
    return isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(d) ? d : null;
  }
  if (typeof value === "string" && value.trim()) {
    const raw = value.trim();

    // ★追加：文字列のシリアル値もDate化する
    if (/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(raw)) {
      const serialDate = sheetSerialToJstDate_(Number(raw));
      if (serialDate) return serialDate;
    }

    const m = raw.replace(/-/g, "/").match(/^(\d{4})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})(?:\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?)?/);
    if (m) {
      const iso = m[1] + "-" + String(m[2]).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(m[3]).padStart(2, "0") +
        "T" + String(m[4] || "0").padStart(2, "0") + ":" + String(m[5] || "0").padStart(2, "0") + ":" + String(m[6] || "0").padStart(2, "0") + "+09:00";
      const jst = new Date(iso);
      if (isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(jst)) return jst;
    }
    const d = new Date(raw);
    if (isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(d)) return d;
  }
  return null;
}

function normalizeOhlcvRowForWrite_(row) {
  if (!row || !row.length) return null;
  const out = row.slice(0, 8);
  while (out.length < 8) out.push("");
  const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(out[0]);
  if (!ts) return null;
  if (!isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts)) return null;
  out[0] = new Date(ts.getTime());
  out[2] = String(out[2] || "").trim();
  if (!out[2]) return null;

  const marker = String(out[1] || "").trim();
  if (marker !== "GAP_FAILED") {
    for (let i = 3; i <= 6; i++) {
      const n = Number(out[i]);
      if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return null;
      out[i] = n;
    }
    const vol = Number(out[7]);
    if (!Number.isFinite(vol) || vol < 0) return null;
    out[7] = Math.round(vol);
  }
  return out;
}

function buildOhlcvAppendPreWriteSamples_(rows, maxSamples) {
  const samples = [];
  (rows || []).slice(0, maxSamples || 5).forEach((row, index) => {
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row && row.length ? row[0] : null);
    samples.push({
      index: index,
      timestamp: ts ? Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm") : "",
      marker: row && row.length > 1 ? String(row[1] || "") : "",
      symbol: row && row.length > 2 ? String(row[2] || "") : ""
    });
  });
  return samples;
}

function appendRowsToSheet_(sheet, data) {
  return appendOhlcvRowsToSheetDetailed_(sheet, data, "appendRowsToSheet_").rows;
}

function appendOhlcvRowsToSheetDetailed_(sheet, data, context) {
  const detail = {
    requestedRows: data ? data.length : 0,
    validRows: 0,
    skippedRows: 0,
    rows: 0,
    deletedRows: 0,
    repairSymbols: 0,
    persistedRows: [],
    appendFailed: false,
    preWriteSamples: []
  };

  if (!data || data.length === 0) return detail;
  const validData = [];
  const skippedSamples = [];
  data.forEach((row, index) => {
    const normalized = normalizeOhlcvRowForWrite_(row);
    if (normalized) {
      validData.push(normalized);
    } else if (skippedSamples.length < 5) {
      skippedSamples.push({ index: index, timestamp: row && row.length ? String(row[0]) : "", symbol: row && row.length > 2 ? row[2] : "" });
    }
  });
  const skipped = data.length - validData.length;
  detail.validRows = validData.length;
  detail.skippedRows = skipped;
  if (skipped > 0) {
    debugLogToSheet_("appendRowsToSheet_.skippedInvalidTs", {
      total: data.length, valid: validData.length, skipped: skipped, samples: skippedSamples
    });
  }
  if (validData.length === 0) return detail;
  const startRow = sheet.getLastRow() + 1;
  const preWriteSamples = buildOhlcvAppendPreWriteSamples_(validData, 5);
  detail.preWriteSamples = preWriteSamples;

// Write B:H first, then write A alone so timestamp write failures are isolated.
sheet.getRange(startRow, 2, validData.length, 7).setValues(validData.map(row => row.slice(1, 8)));

// A列timestampはDate オブジェクトとして書き込み、日時表示形式を設定する
const timestampDateValues = validData.map(row => [normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]) || row[0]]);
const timestampRange = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, validData.length, 1);

timestampRange.setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd hh:mm");
timestampRange.setValues(timestampDateValues);

SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  const readback = purgeInvalidRecentlyAppendedOhlcvRows_(sheet, startRow, validData.length, context || "appendRowsToSheet_.readback", validData, preWriteSamples);
  detail.deletedRows = readback.deletedRows || 0;
  detail.repairSymbols = readback.repairSymbols || 0;
  detail.persistedRows = readback.persistedRows || [];
  detail.rows = detail.persistedRows.length;
  detail.appendFailed = validData.length > 0 && (detail.deletedRows > 0 || (readback.readRows || 0) < validData.length);
  return detail;
}

function rewriteOhlcvTimestampRangeAsText_(sheet, startRow, numRows) {
  // タイムスタンプは Date 形式で保存するため、この関数は何もしない
  return { convertedRows: 0 };
}

function restoreRecentlyAppendedOhlcvTimestamps_(sheet, startRow, validData, context) {
  if (!sheet || !validData || !validData.length || !Number.isFinite(startRow)) {
    return { rewrittenRows: 0 };
  }

  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < startRow) return { rewrittenRows: 0 };

  const readRows = Math.min(validData.length, lastRow - startRow + 1);
  if (readRows <= 0) return { rewrittenRows: 0 };

  const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, readRows, 1).getValues();
  let needsRewrite = false;
  const samples = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    const ts = values[i][0];
    if (isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts) && isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts)) continue;
    needsRewrite = true;
    if (samples.length < 10) {
      samples.push({
        row: startRow + i,
        timestamp: ts == null ? "" : String(ts),
        expected: Utilities.formatDate(validData[i][0], CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm"),
        symbol: validData[i][2]
      });
    }
  }

  if (!needsRewrite) return { rewrittenRows: 0 };

  const timestampValues = validData.slice(0, readRows).map(row => [new Date(row[0].getTime())]);
  sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, readRows, 1).setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd hh:mm");
  sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, readRows, 1).setValues(timestampValues);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "OHLCV追記直後にA列timestampが空・無効だったため、追記元の正規timestampをA列へ再書き込みしました。", {
    context: context || "",
    startRow: startRow,
    scannedRows: readRows,
    rewrittenRows: readRows,
    samples: samples
  });
  return { rewrittenRows: readRows };
}

function purgeInvalidRecentlyAppendedOhlcvRows_(sheet, startRow, numRows, context, expectedRows, preWriteSamples) {
  if (!sheet || !Number.isFinite(startRow) || !Number.isFinite(numRows) || numRows <= 0) {
    return { deletedRows: 0, repairSymbols: 0, persistedRows: [], readRows: 0 };
  }

  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < startRow) return { deletedRows: 0, repairSymbols: 0, persistedRows: [], readRows: 0 };

  const readRows = Math.min(numRows, lastRow - startRow + 1);
  if (readRows <= 0) return { deletedRows: 0, repairSymbols: 0, persistedRows: [], readRows: 0 };

  const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, readRows, 3).getValues();
  const deleteRows = [];
  const repairSymbolSet = {};
  const samples = [];
  const persistedRows = [];

  values.forEach((row, index) => {
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]);
    const invalidReason = !isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts)
      ? "invalid_timestamp"
      : (!isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts) ? "non_session_timestamp" : "");
    if (!invalidReason) {
      if (expectedRows && expectedRows[index]) persistedRows.push(expectedRows[index]);
      return;
    }

    const sheetRow = startRow + index;
    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    const expected = expectedRows && expectedRows[index] ? expectedRows[index] : null;
    deleteRows.push(sheetRow);
    if (symbol) repairSymbolSet[symbol] = true;
    if (samples.length < 10) {
      samples.push({
        row: sheetRow,
        postWriteTimestamp: ts == null ? "" : String(ts),
        expectedTimestamp: expected && expected[0] ? Utilities.formatDate(expected[0], CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm") : "",
        reason: invalidReason,
        symbol: symbol
      });
    }
  });

  if (deleteRows.length === 0) {
    return { deletedRows: 0, repairSymbols: 0, persistedRows: expectedRows ? expectedRows.slice(0, readRows) : persistedRows, readRows: readRows };
  }

  deleteRowsByRowNums_(sheet, deleteRows);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  const repairSymbols = Object.keys(repairSymbolSet).sort();
  if (repairSymbols.length > 0) setOhlcvRepairSymbols_(repairSymbols);

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "OHLCV追記直後の読み返しで空・無効timestamp行を検出したため削除しました。", {
    context: context || "",
    startRow: startRow,
    scannedRows: readRows,
    deletedRows: deleteRows.length,
    repairSymbols: repairSymbols.length,
    preWriteSamples: preWriteSamples || buildOhlcvAppendPreWriteSamples_(expectedRows || [], 5),
    samples: samples
  });

  return { deletedRows: deleteRows.length, repairSymbols: repairSymbols.length, persistedRows: persistedRows, readRows: readRows };
}

function scheduleOhlcvResume_(handlerName, delayMs) {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(handlerName);

  const maxRetries = 3;
  const triggerDelayMs = Number.isFinite(delayMs) ? delayMs : 60 * 1000;

  for (let i = 0; i < maxRetries; i++) {
    try {
      ScriptApp.newTrigger(handlerName)
        .timeBased()
        .after(triggerDelayMs)
        .create();
      writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `${handlerName} を ${Math.round(triggerDelayMs / 1000)} 秒後に実行する再開トリガーとして設定しました。`, {
        handlerName: handlerName,
        delayMs: triggerDelayMs,
        attempt: i + 1
      });
      traceOhlcv_("resume.scheduled", {
        handlerName: handlerName,
        delayMs: triggerDelayMs,
        attempt: i + 1
      });
      debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.resume.scheduled", {
        handlerName: handlerName,
        delayMs: triggerDelayMs,
        attempt: i + 1
      });
      return;
    } catch (e) {
      if (i === maxRetries - 1) {
        writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `再開トリガーを ${maxRetries} 回試しましたが作成できませんでした。`, { handlerName: handlerName, message: e.message });
        throw e;
      }
      writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", `再開トリガー作成に失敗しました。少し待って再試行します。`, {
        handlerName: handlerName,
        attempt: i + 1,
        maxRetries: maxRetries,
        message: e.message
      });
      Utilities.sleep(2000 * (i + 1));
    }
  }
}
/**
 * 次回の実行を予約する関数の堅牢化版
 * トリガー作成自体が Google サーバーエラーで失敗した場合にリトライします。
 */
function setupResumeTrigger_(handlerName) {
  scheduleOhlcvResume_(handlerName, 10 * 1000);
}

/**
 * 継続実行のエントリポイント
 * fetchOHLCVForNewAlerts 自体がエラーで止まっても、自動リトライを仕掛けます。
 */
function resumeOHLCVFetch() {
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "resumeOHLCVFetchが起動しました。保存済みのフェーズとカーソルからOHLCV取得を再開します。", {
    businessDate: getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_(),
    isManual: !!getManualOhlcvBusinessDateKey_()
  });
  traceOhlcv_("resume.entry", {});
  debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.resume.entry", getOhlcvRuntimeState_());
  try {
    return fetchOHLCVForNewAlerts();
  } catch (e) {
    const errData = {
      message: e && e.message ? e.message : String(e),
      stack: e && e.stack ? String(e.stack) : "",
      state: getOhlcvRuntimeState_()
    };
    console.error("[OHLCV] resumeOHLCVFetch error " + JSON.stringify(errData));
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.resume.error", errData);
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "再開処理中にエラーが発生しました。1分後に自動リトライします。", { message: errData.message });

    // エラー発生時も、stateが保存されていれば setupResumeTrigger_ により
    // 次回の実行（リトライ）がスケジュールされます。
    setupResumeTrigger_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
    return { ok: false, retried: true, message: errData.message };
  }
}

// ========================================
// Yahoo Finance 取得ロジック（生価格版・期間拡張・無効timestampスキップ）
// ========================================
// ★ 追加: URL ペア構築（fetchAll でバッチ実行するための部品）
//    fetchOHLCVRecent の URL 生成ロジックをそのまま抽出した関数
function buildOhlcvRequestPair_(tvSymbol, lastTs, optStartSec, optEndSec) {
  return buildOhlcvRequestPairForEndMillis_(tvSymbol, lastTs, getOhlcvRunEndMillis_(), optStartSec, optEndSec);
}

function buildOhlcvRequestPairForEndMillis_(tvSymbol, lastTs, runEndMillis, optStartSec, optEndSec, optOptions) {
  const yahooSymbol = convertToYahooSymbol(tvSymbol);
  const options = optOptions || {};
  const forcePeriod = options.forcePeriod === true;
  let timeParam = "";
  let useRecentRange = false;

  // 直近扱いにする日数。
  // lastTsがこれ以内なら、Yahooのrange=5dで十分。
  const RECENT_RANGE_DAYS = 5;

  if (optStartSec && optEndSec) {
    // 明示的に期間指定された場合は、その指定を最優先する。
    timeParam = `period1=${optStartSec}&period2=${optEndSec}`;
    useRecentRange = false;

  } else if (lastTs) {
    const nowSec = Math.floor(runEndMillis / 1000);
    const lastTsSec = Math.floor(lastTs / 1000);
    const diffDays = (nowSec - lastTsSec) / (24 * 60 * 60);
    const lookbackSec = OHLCV_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60;

    if (lastTsSec >= nowSec) {
      // lastTsが未来または取得終了時刻以上なら、異常値対策として直近だけ取り直す。
      const p1 = nowSec - Math.min(RECENT_RANGE_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60, lookbackSec);
      const p2 = nowSec;
      timeParam = `period1=${p1}&period2=${p2}`;
      useRecentRange = false;

      if (isVerboseOhlcvFetchLogEnabled_()) {
        debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.futureLastTs", {
          symbol: tvSymbol,
          diffDays: diffDays.toFixed(1),
          p1: p1,
          p2: p2
        });
      }

    } else if (!forcePeriod && diffDays <= RECENT_RANGE_DAYS) {
      // 最終取得がかなり新しい場合。
      // Yahooの直近5日レンジで十分。祝日・取得漏れも吸収しやすい。
      useRecentRange = true;

      if (isVerboseOhlcvFetchLogEnabled_()) {
        debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.recentRange", {
          symbol: tvSymbol,
          diffDays: diffDays.toFixed(1),
          range: "5d"
        });
      }

    } else if (diffDays <= OHLCV_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_DAYS) {
      // 最終取得が古いが、120日以内の場合。
      // 最終タイムスタンプから現在まで取得する。空白が生まれた場合は後段のgap repairでカバーする。
      let p1 = lastTsSec;
      const p1Limit = nowSec - lookbackSec;
      if (p1 < p1Limit) p1 = p1Limit;

      const p2 = nowSec;
      timeParam = `period1=${p1}&period2=${p2}`;
      useRecentRange = false;

      if (isVerboseOhlcvFetchLogEnabled_()) {
        debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.gapFill", {
          symbol: tvSymbol,
          diffDays: diffDays.toFixed(1),
          p1: p1,
          p2: p2
        });
      }

    } else {
      // 最終取得が120日より古い場合。
      // 評価に必要な範囲を超えているので、120日分だけ取り直す。
      const p1 = nowSec - lookbackSec;
      const p2 = nowSec;
      timeParam = `period1=${p1}&period2=${p2}`;
      useRecentRange = false;

      if (isVerboseOhlcvFetchLogEnabled_()) {
        debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.fullRefresh", {
          symbol: tvSymbol,
          diffDays: diffDays.toFixed(1),
          lookbackDays: OHLCV_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_DAYS
        });
      }
    }

  } else {
    // OHLCV未取得の完全新規銘柄。
    // 120日分取得する。
    const p2 = Math.floor(runEndMillis / 1000);
    const p1 = p2 - OHLCV_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60;
    timeParam = `period1=${p1}&period2=${p2}`;
    useRecentRange = false;
  }

  let dailyUrl;
  let intraUrl;

  if (useRecentRange) {
    dailyUrl = `https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/${yahooSymbol}?interval=1d&range=5d`;
    intraUrl = `https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/${yahooSymbol}?interval=1h&range=5d`;
  } else {
    dailyUrl = `https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/${yahooSymbol}?interval=1d&${timeParam}`;
    intraUrl = `https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/${yahooSymbol}?interval=1h&${timeParam}`;
  }

  return { dailyUrl: dailyUrl, intraUrl: intraUrl };
}

function buildDailyOhlcvMapFromYahooResponse_(dailyRes) {
  const map = {};

  try {
    if (!dailyRes || dailyRes.getResponseCode() !== 200) return map;

    const json = JSON.parse(dailyRes.getContentText());
    const result = json.chart && json.chart.result && json.chart.result[0];
    if (!result || !result.timestamp) return map;

    const q = result.indicators && result.indicators.quote && result.indicators.quote[0];
    if (!q) return map;

    const ts = result.timestamp;

    for (let i = 0; i < ts.length; i++) {
      const d = new Date(ts[i] * 1000);
      const ymd = Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd");

      map[ymd] = {
        open: toFiniteNumberOrNull_(q.open ? q.open[i] : null),
        high: toFiniteNumberOrNull_(q.high ? q.high[i] : null),
        low: toFiniteNumberOrNull_(q.low ? q.low[i] : null),
        close: toFiniteNumberOrNull_(q.close ? q.close[i] : null),
        volume: toFiniteNumberOrZero_(q.volume ? q.volume[i] : null)
      };
    }
  } catch (e) {
    debugLogToSheet_("buildDailyOhlcvMap.error", {
      message: e && e.message ? e.message : String(e)
    });
  }

  return map;
}

function toFiniteNumberOrNull_(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") return null;
  const n = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
}

function toFiniteNumberOrZero_(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") return 0;
  const n = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
}

// ★ 追加: 1h レスポンスの解析（OHLCV 行配列を返す）
//    fetchOHLCVRecent の解析ロジックをそのまま抽出（GAP_REPAIR 修正込み）
function getYahooIntraSessionName_(hour, minute) {
  // YahooのJPX 1h足は区間開始時刻として扱う。
  // AM: 09:00 / 10:00 / 11:00 / 12:00
  // PM: 13:00 / 14:00 / 15:00 / 15:30 or 16:00 snapshot
  if (hour < 9) return "";
  if (hour < 13) return "AM";
  if (hour < 16 || (hour === 16 && minute === 0)) return "PM";
  return "";
}

function parseIntraResponse_(tvSymbol, intraRes, factorMap, dailyMap, options) {
  let res = intraRes;

  try {
    if (!res) {
      debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.intraException", {
        symbol: tvSymbol,
        error: "no response"
      });
      return [];
    }

    if (res.getResponseCode() !== 200) {
      debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.intraError", {
        symbol: tvSymbol,
        status: res.getResponseCode()
      });
      return [];
    }
  } catch (e) {
    debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.intraException", {
      symbol: tvSymbol,
      error: e.message
    });
    return [];
  }

  const json = JSON.parse(res.getContentText());
  const result = json.chart && json.chart.result && json.chart.result[0];

  if (!result || !result.timestamp) {
    debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.noIntraResult", {
      symbol: tvSymbol
    });
    return [];
  }

  const ts = result.timestamp;
  const q = result.indicators && result.indicators.quote && result.indicators.quote[0];

  if (!q) {
    debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.noQuote", {
      symbol: tvSymbol
    });
    return [];
  }

  const candlesByPeriod = {};
  const flatZeroFallbackByPeriod = {};
  const zeroVolumePriceSessionMap = {};
  const round = val => Math.round(Number(val) * 100) / 100;

  options = options || {};
  const metaOut = options.metaOut || null;
  const MIN_VALID_TS = 631152000; // 1990-01-01
  let firstTimestampAt = "";
  let lastTimestampAt = "";
  let lastNonNullOhlcAt = "";
  const todayKeyForMaxAmVolume = String(options.todayKeyForMaxAmVolume || "");

  for (let i = 0; i < ts.length; i++) {
    const rawOpen = q.open ? q.open[i] : null;
    const rawHigh = q.high ? q.high[i] : null;
    const rawLow = q.low ? q.low[i] : null;
    const rawClose = q.close ? q.close[i] : null;
    const rawVolume = q.volume ? q.volume[i] : null;
    const tsValue = ts[i];

    if (tsValue && tsValue >= MIN_VALID_TS) {
      const tsIso = new Date(tsValue * 1000).toISOString();
      if (!firstTimestampAt) firstTimestampAt = tsIso;
      lastTimestampAt = tsIso;
      if (rawOpen != null || rawHigh != null || rawLow != null || rawClose != null) {
        lastNonNullOhlcAt = tsIso;
      }
    }

    // openがnullでもcloseだけあるケースを拾う
    if (rawOpen == null && rawClose == null) continue;

    if (!tsValue || tsValue < MIN_VALID_TS) {
      debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.invalidTs", {
        symbol: tvSymbol,
        tsValue: tsValue,
        index: i
      });
      continue;
    }

    const dateObj = new Date(tsValue * 1000);
    if (isNaN(dateObj.getTime())) {
      debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.invalidTimestamp", {
        symbol: tvSymbol,
        tsValue: ts[i],
        index: i
      });
      continue;
    }

    const dateKey = Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd");
    const hour = parseInt(Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "HH"), 10);
    const minute = parseInt(Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "mm"), 10);

    const sessionName = getYahooIntraSessionName_(hour, minute);
    if (!sessionName) continue;

    const key = `${dateKey}_${sessionName}`;

    const close = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawClose);
    const open = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawOpen) !== null ? toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawOpen) : close;
    const high = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawHigh) !== null ? toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawHigh) : Math.max(open, close);
    const low = toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawLow) !== null ? toFiniteNumberOrNull_(rawLow) : Math.min(open, close);
    const volume = toFiniteNumberOrZero_(rawVolume);

    if (open === null || high === null || low === null || close === null) continue;

    const isFlatZeroVolume =
      volume === 0 &&
      open === high &&
      high === low &&
      low === close;

    const isClosingAuctionTime =
      (hour === 15 && minute >= 30) ||
      (hour === 16 && minute === 0);

    const isClosingSnapshot = isFlatZeroVolume && isClosingAuctionTime;

    // ★ここが質問のコードを入れる場所
    // 価格はあるのに出来高が0のセッションを記録する
    if (volume === 0 && open !== null && high !== null && low !== null && close !== null) {
      zeroVolumePriceSessionMap[key] = true;
    }

    // 15:30 / 16:00 の引けだけ約定をPM足として作る
    if (isClosingSnapshot) {
      zeroVolumePriceSessionMap[key] = true;

      if (!candlesByPeriod[key]) {
        const sessionHour = sessionName === "AM" ? 9 : 13;
        const normalizedTs = new Date(`${dateKey}T${String(sessionHour).padStart(2, "0")}:00:00+09:00`);

        candlesByPeriod[key] = {
          timestamp: normalizedTs,
          symbol: tvSymbol,
          open: round(open),
          high: round(high),
          low: round(low),
          close: round(close),
          volume: 0
        };
      } else {
        candlesByPeriod[key].close = round(close);
        candlesByPeriod[key].high = Math.max(candlesByPeriod[key].high, round(high));
        candlesByPeriod[key].low = Math.min(candlesByPeriod[key].low, round(low));
      }

      continue;
    }

    // その他の V=0 かつ OHLC同値バーは、通常はダミーとしてスキップする。
    // ただし normal バーが一本も無いセッションでは行ゼロになって永久に gap が
    // 埋まらないため、最後に観測した flat-zero バーを「同セッションキーに
    // normal バーが無ければ採用する候補」として一旦控えておく。
    if (isFlatZeroVolume) {
      const sessionHour = sessionName === "AM" ? 9 : 13;
      const normalizedTs = new Date(`${dateKey}T${String(sessionHour).padStart(2, "0")}:00:00+09:00`);
      flatZeroFallbackByPeriod[key] = {
        timestamp: normalizedTs,
        symbol: tvSymbol,
        open: round(open),
        high: round(high),
        low: round(low),
        close: round(close),
        volume: 0
      };
      continue;
    }

    if (!candlesByPeriod[key]) {
      const sessionHour = sessionName === "AM" ? 9 : 13;
      const normalizedTs = new Date(`${dateKey}T${String(sessionHour).padStart(2, "0")}:00:00+09:00`);

      candlesByPeriod[key] = {
        timestamp: normalizedTs,
        symbol: tvSymbol,
        open: round(open),
        high: round(high),
        low: round(low),
        close: round(close),
        volume: volume
      };
    } else {
      const p = candlesByPeriod[key];
      p.high = round(Math.max(p.high, high));
      p.low = round(Math.min(p.low, low));
      p.close = round(close);
            // 13:30暫定実行の今日AMだけは、Yahoo 1h出来高が累積っぽく返る銘柄対策として
      // 単純合算せず、最大値を採用する。
      if (
        todayKeyForMaxAmVolume &&
        dateKey === todayKeyForMaxAmVolume &&
        sessionName === "AM"
      ) {
        p.volume = Math.max(toSafeVolumeNumber_(p.volume), toSafeVolumeNumber_(volume));
      } else {
        p.volume += volume;
      }
    }
  }

  // flat-zero フォールバック: normal バーが一本も無いセッションキーだけ採用する。
  // これで薄商い銘柄（出来高 0 / OHLC 同値しか返らない日）の AM/PM 行を取りこぼさない。
  Object.keys(flatZeroFallbackByPeriod).forEach(key => {
    if (candlesByPeriod[key]) return;
    candlesByPeriod[key] = flatZeroFallbackByPeriod[key];
    zeroVolumePriceSessionMap[key] = true;
  });

  // ★ここで日足出来高による補正を実行する
  repairSessionVolumesWithDailyVolume_(candlesByPeriod, dailyMap, zeroVolumePriceSessionMap);

  const resultCandles = Object.values(candlesByPeriod)
    .sort((a, b) => a.timestamp - b.timestamp)
    .map(p => [
      p.timestamp,
      "",
      p.symbol,
      p.open,
      p.high,
      p.low,
      p.close,
      p.volume
    ]);

  if (metaOut) {
    metaOut.symbol = tvSymbol;
    metaOut.timestampCount = ts.length;
    metaOut.firstTimestampAt = firstTimestampAt;
    metaOut.lastTimestampAt = lastTimestampAt;
    metaOut.lastNonNullOhlcAt = lastNonNullOhlcAt;
    metaOut.candlesCount = resultCandles.length;
  }

  if (isVerboseOhlcvFetchLogEnabled_()) {
    debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.result", {
      symbol: tvSymbol,
      candlesCount: resultCandles.length,
      timestampCount: ts.length,
      firstTimestampAt: firstTimestampAt,
      lastTimestampAt: lastTimestampAt,
      lastNonNullOhlcAt: lastNonNullOhlcAt,
      dateRange: resultCandles.length
        ? `${resultCandles[0][0].toISOString()} ~ ${resultCandles[resultCandles.length - 1][0].toISOString()}`
        : "none"
    });
  }

  return resultCandles;
}

function repairSessionVolumesWithDailyVolume_(candlesByPeriod, dailyMap, zeroVolumePriceSessionMap) {
  return;

  dailyMap = dailyMap || {};

  Object.keys(dailyMap).forEach(ymd => {
    const daily = dailyMap[ymd];
    const dailyVol = Math.round(Number(daily && daily.volume));

    if (!Number.isFinite(dailyVol) || dailyVol < 0) return;

    const amKey = `${ymd}_AM`;
    const pmKey = `${ymd}_PM`;

    const am = candlesByPeriod[amKey] || null;
    const pm = candlesByPeriod[pmKey] || null;

    if (!am && !pm) return;

    const amVol = am ? toSafeVolumeNumber_(am.volume) : 0;
    const pmVol = pm ? toSafeVolumeNumber_(pm.volume) : 0;

    const currentTotal = amVol + pmVol;
    const gap = Math.round(dailyVol - currentTotal);

    if (gap === 0) return;

    // AM/PM両方ある場合：
    // 差分を半分ずつ配分し、割り切れない1株はPMに寄せる。
    if (am && pm) {
      if (gap > 0) {
        const addAm = Math.floor(gap / 2);
        const addPm = gap - addAm; // 奇数ならPMが1多い

        am.volume = Math.round(amVol + addAm);
        pm.volume = Math.round(pmVol + addPm);

        debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.volumeEvenSplitAdjusted", {
          date: ymd,
          dailyVol: dailyVol,
          amBefore: amVol,
          pmBefore: pmVol,
          currentTotal: currentTotal,
          gap: gap,
          addAm: addAm,
          addPm: addPm,
          amAfter: am.volume,
          pmAfter: pm.volume
        });

        return;
      }

      // もし1h合計が日足出来高を超えている場合も、合計一致を優先して減算する。
      // 減らす場合も、割り切れない1株分はPM側から多く減らす。
      reduceSessionVolumesEvenly_(am, pm, -gap, ymd, dailyVol, amVol, pmVol);
      return;
    }

    // AMしかない場合はAMに寄せる
    if (am && !pm) {
      am.volume = Math.max(0, Math.round(amVol + gap));

      debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.volumeSingleSessionAdjusted", {
        date: ymd,
        session: "AM",
        dailyVol: dailyVol,
        before: amVol,
        gap: gap,
        after: am.volume
      });

      return;
    }

    // PMしかない場合はPMに寄せる
    if (!am && pm) {
      pm.volume = Math.max(0, Math.round(pmVol + gap));

      debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.volumeSingleSessionAdjusted", {
        date: ymd,
        session: "PM",
        dailyVol: dailyVol,
        before: pmVol,
        gap: gap,
        after: pm.volume
      });

      return;
    }
  });
}


function reduceSessionVolumesEvenly_(am, pm, overAmount, ymd, dailyVol, amBefore, pmBefore) {
  let amVol = toSafeVolumeNumber_(am.volume);
  let pmVol = toSafeVolumeNumber_(pm.volume);

  let reduceAm = Math.floor(overAmount / 2);
  let reducePm = overAmount - reduceAm; // 奇数ならPM側を1多く減らす

  if (reduceAm > amVol) {
    const overflow = reduceAm - amVol;
    reduceAm = amVol;
    reducePm += overflow;
  }

  if (reducePm > pmVol) {
    const overflow = reducePm - pmVol;
    reducePm = pmVol;
    reduceAm = Math.min(amVol, reduceAm + overflow);
  }

  am.volume = Math.max(0, Math.round(amVol - reduceAm));
  pm.volume = Math.max(0, Math.round(pmVol - reducePm));

  debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.volumeEvenSplitReduced", {
    date: ymd,
    dailyVol: dailyVol,
    amBefore: amBefore,
    pmBefore: pmBefore,
    overAmount: overAmount,
    reduceAm: reduceAm,
    reducePm: reducePm,
    amAfter: am.volume,
    pmAfter: pm.volume
  });
}

// ★ 変更: fetchOHLCVRecent は URL構築 → 1h fetch → 解析の薄いラッパー
//    既存シグネチャは互換。1h取得成功時の生成行は従来同等。
function fetchOHLCVRecent(tvSymbol, lastTs, optStartSec, optEndSec) {
  const pair = buildOhlcvRequestPair_(tvSymbol, lastTs, optStartSec, optEndSec);

  let intraRes;

  try {
    intraRes = UrlFetchApp.fetch(pair.intraUrl, {
      muteHttpExceptions: true,
      headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0" }
    });
  } catch (e) {
    debugLogToSheet_("fetchOHLCVRecent.intraException", {
      symbol: tvSymbol,
      error: e.message
    });
    return [];
  }

  return parseIntraResponse_(tvSymbol, intraRes, null);
}

function convertToYahooSymbol(tvSymbol) {
  let code = String(tvSymbol || "").trim();
  if (!code) return "";
  if (code.startsWith("TYO:")) return code.replace("TYO:", "") + ".T";
  if (/^\d{4}$/.test(code) || /^\d{3}[A-Z\d]$/.test(code)) return code + ".T";
  return code;
}

function sendCompletionNotice_(newCount, totalCount, tag) {
  const webhookUrl = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("DISCORD_WEBHOOK");
  if (!webhookUrl) return;

  const embed = {
    title: "✅ OHLCVデータ同期完了",
    color: 0x2ecc71,  // 緑色
    fields: [
      {
        name: "🤖 スコアリングBot",
        value: "最新の分析結果は `/scan` でご確認いただけます。",
        inline: false
      }
    ],
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    footer: {
      text: "天底極致 OHLCV同期"
    }
  };

  const payload = { embeds: [embed] };

  try {
    UrlFetchApp.fetch(webhookUrl, {
      method: "post",
      contentType: "application/json",
      payload: JSON.stringify(payload),
      muteHttpExceptions: true
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Discord通知失敗: " + e.message);
  }
}
function deleteTriggersByHandler_(handlerName) { ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(t => { if (t.getHandlerFunction() === handlerName) ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); }); }

function isQuotaExhausted_(err) {
  const msg = String((err && err.message) || err || "").toLowerCase();
  return msg.indexOf("too many times") !== -1 ||
         msg.indexOf("service invoked too many") !== -1 ||
         msg.indexOf("quota") !== -1;
}
function minifySplitInfo_(info) {
  if (!info || info.error || !info.splitRatio || !info.splitDate) return { e: 1 };
  return { r: info.splitRatio, d: info.splitDate };
}
function expandSplitInfo_(mini) {
  if (!mini || mini.e) return { price: null, splitRatio: null, splitDate: null, error: "CACHED" };
  return { price: null, splitRatio: mini.r, splitDate: mini.d, error: null };
}
function safeSetSplitCache_(props, obj, keepKeys) {
  const json = JSON.stringify(obj);
  if (json.length <= 8192) {
    props.setProperty("OHLCV_SPLIT_CACHE", json);
  } else {
    const keep = {};
    (keepKeys || []).forEach(key => {
      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key)) keep[key] = obj[key];
    });
    const keepJson = JSON.stringify(keep);
    if (keepJson.length <= 8192 && keepKeys && keepKeys.length > 0) {
      console.log(`[OHLCV_SPLIT_CACHE] ${json.length}B > 8192B - current chunk cache only (${keepJson.length}B)`);
      props.setProperty("OHLCV_SPLIT_CACHE", keepJson);
    } else {
      console.log(`[OHLCV_SPLIT_CACHE] ${json.length}B > 8192B - cache dropped`);
      props.deleteProperty("OHLCV_SPLIT_CACHE");
    }
  }
}

// ========================================
// その他（デバッグなど）
// ========================================
function debugWeekly5bdCandidates() {
  const app = getAppConfig_();
  const rawSh = getOrCreateSheet_(app.spreadsheetId, CONFIG.RAW_SHEET, RAW_HEADERS);
  const reportRange = getLastCompletedBusinessWeekRangeJst_(new Date());
  const rows = loadRecentMappedRawRows_(rawSh, CONFIG.WEEKLY_SCAN_MAX_ROWS);
  const out = { weekStart: formatYmd_(reportRange.start), weekEnd: formatYmd_(reportRange.end), allRows: rows.length, bottomRows: 0, evalDateInRange: 0, perfFinite: 0, notReported: 0, finalCandidates: 0, samples: [] };
  rows.forEach(r => {
    if (r.signalType !== "BOTTOM") return;
    out.bottomRows++;
    const c = r.checkpoints[5];
    const inRange = c.evalDate instanceof Date && isDateBetweenInclusive_(c.evalDate, reportRange.start, reportRange.end);
    if (!inRange) return;
    out.evalDateInRange++;
    if (!Number.isFinite(c.perf)) return;
    out.perfFinite++;
    if (c.reported) return;
    out.notReported++;
    out.finalCandidates++;
    if (out.samples.length < 10) out.samples.push({ rowNum: r.rowNum, symbolCode: r.symbolCode, symbolName: r.symbolName, signalType: r.signalType, evalDate5: formatYmd_(c.evalDate), evalClose5: c.evalClose, perf5: c.perf, reported5: c.reported });
  });
  console.log(JSON.stringify(out, null, 2));
  return out;
}
function getLastCompletedBusinessWeekRangeJst_(now) { return getClosedBusinessWeekRangeJst_(now); }

// ★ 追加: 分割情報 API の URL 構築（fetchAll でバッチ実行するための部品）
function buildYahooFinanceDataUrl_(symbolCode) {
  const code = String(symbolCode || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!code) return null;
  const yahooSymbol = code.endsWith(".T") ? code : code + ".T";
  return `https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/${yahooSymbol}?interval=1d&range=3mo&events=splits`;
}

// ★ 追加: 分割情報レスポンスの解析
//    fetchYahooFinanceDataAPI_ の解析ロジックをそのまま抽出
function parseYahooFinanceDataResponse_(symbolCode, res) {
  const code = String(symbolCode || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!code) return { price: null, splitRatio: null, splitDate: null, error: "NO_CODE" };
  try {
    if (!res) return { price: null, splitRatio: null, splitDate: null, error: "FETCH_ERROR" };
    const responseCode = res.getResponseCode();
    if (responseCode === 404) {
      return { price: null, splitRatio: null, splitDate: null, error: "DELISTED" };
    }
    const json = JSON.parse(res.getContentText());
    const result = json.chart.result?.[0];
    if (!result) return { price: null, splitRatio: null, splitDate: null, error: "NO_DATA" };

    let currentPrice = result.meta?.regularMarketPrice;
    let splitRatio = null;
    let splitDate = null;
    if (result.events && result.events.splits) {
      const splitsArray = Object.values(result.events.splits).sort((a, b) => b.date - a.date);
      if (splitsArray.length > 0) {
        const latestSplit = splitsArray[0];
        splitRatio = latestSplit.numerator / latestSplit.denominator;
        splitDate = latestSplit.date;
      }
    }
    return { price: currentPrice, splitRatio: splitRatio, splitDate: splitDate, error: null };
  } catch (e) {
    return { price: null, splitRatio: null, splitDate: null, error: "FETCH_ERROR" };
  }
}

// ★ 変更: URL 構築 → fetch → 解析の薄いラッパー（既存シグネチャ互換）
function fetchYahooFinanceDataAPI_(symbolCode) {
  const code = String(symbolCode || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!code) return { price: null, splitRatio: null, splitDate: null, error: "NO_CODE" };
  const url = buildYahooFinanceDataUrl_(code);
  try {
    const res = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, {
      muteHttpExceptions: true,
      headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0" }
    });
    return parseYahooFinanceDataResponse_(code, res);
  } catch (e) {
    return { price: null, splitRatio: null, splitDate: null, error: "FETCH_ERROR" };
  }
}

function resetAllOhlcvProperties() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.deleteProperty(PHASE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(STATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(SYMBOL_LIST_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(MAIN_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty("LAST_TS_MAP");
  props.deleteProperty("CURRENT_REFRESH_ID");
  props.deleteProperty("SPLIT_QUEUE");
  props.deleteProperty("SPLIT_INDEX");
  props.deleteProperty("OHLCV_SPLIT_CACHE");
  props.deleteProperty("OHLCV_REPAIR_SYMBOLS");
  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_MANUAL_BUSINESS_DATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_MANUAL_BUSINESS_EXPIRES_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_INTRADAY_STALE_SYMBOLS_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_HANDLER);
  clearMiddayOhlcvState_();
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "OHLCV関連の進捗プロパティをすべてリセットしました。手動基準日の指定もクリア済みです。", {});
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 【修正3】repairEmptyTimestampRows（書き直し版）
//
// 使い方:
//   repairEmptyTimestampRows()          // 本番実行
//   repairEmptyTimestampRows(true)      // DryRun（書き込みなし）
//
// 処理内容:
//   1. ohlcv_4h シートを全読み込み
//   2. タイムスタンプ空欄（epoch含む）の行を検出
//   3. DryRunでは削除候補と対象銘柄を返す
//   4. 本番では空timestamp行を削除し、対象銘柄を OHLCV_REPAIR_SYMBOLS に記録
//   5. 日付推定による書き換えは行わず、次回の正規取得で補填する
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function repairEmptyTimestampRows(dryRun) {
  dryRun = dryRun === true;
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
  );
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) { console.log("ERROR: ohlcv_4h が見つかりません"); return; }

  const DATA_ROW  = 2;  // ohlcv_4h は1行目ヘッダー、2行目からデータ
  const NUM_COLS  = 8;
  const lastRow   = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < DATA_ROW) { console.log("データなし"); return; }

  console.log(`[repair] 開始 lastRow=${lastRow} dryRun=${dryRun}`);

  const numDataRows = lastRow - DATA_ROW + 1;
  const values = sh.getRange(DATA_ROW, 1, numDataRows, NUM_COLS).getValues();

  const deleteRowNums = [];
  const repairSymbolSet = {};
  const samples = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    const ts = values[i][0];
    if (isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts) && isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts)) continue;
    const sheetRow = DATA_ROW + i;
    const sym = String(values[i][2] || "").trim();
    deleteRowNums.push(sheetRow);
    if (sym) repairSymbolSet[sym] = true;
    if (samples.length < 20) {
      samples.push({
        sheetRow: sheetRow,
        timestamp: ts == null ? "" : String(ts),
        symbol: sym,
        alertId: String(values[i][1] || ""),
        open: values[i][3],
        close: values[i][6]
      });
    }
  }

  const repairSymbols = Object.keys(repairSymbolSet).sort();
  const summary = {
    ok: true,
    dryRun: dryRun,
    lastRow: lastRow,
    invalidTimestampRows: deleteRowNums.length,
    deleteCandidates: deleteRowNums.length,
    repairSymbols: repairSymbols.length,
    sampleSymbols: repairSymbols.slice(0, 20),
    samples: samples
  };
  console.log(`[repair] 空/無効timestamp行: ${deleteRowNums.length}, 対象銘柄: ${repairSymbols.length}`);
  if (deleteRowNums.length === 0) {
    console.log("[repair] 修正不要");
    return summary;
  }

  if (dryRun) {
    console.log("[repair] DryRun: 削除と再取得キュー登録をスキップ");
    console.log(`[repair] DryRun summary: ${JSON.stringify(summary)}`);
    return summary;
  }

  if (deleteRowNums.length > 0) {
    const uniqueDeleteRows = [...new Set(deleteRowNums)];
    deleteRowsByRowNums_(sh, uniqueDeleteRows);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    console.log(`[repair] ${uniqueDeleteRows.length}行を削除しました`);
    summary.deletedRows = uniqueDeleteRows.length;
  }

  const sortResult = sortOhlcvSheetByTimestampSafe_(sh);
  console.log(`[repair] sort=${JSON.stringify(sortResult)}`);

  if (repairSymbols.length > 0) {
    setOhlcvRepairSymbols_(repairSymbols);
    console.log(`[repair] ${repairSymbols.length}銘柄を OHLCV_REPAIR_SYMBOLS に記録しました`);
  }
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);

  console.log(`[repair] 完了`);
  return summary;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 4/14(今日)のOHLCVデータを手動で再取得する関数
// repairEmptyTimestampRows() 実行後に呼ぶ
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function refetchTodayOhlcv() {
  // Properties をリセットして fetchOHLCVForNewAlerts を新鮮な状態で実行
  resetAllOhlcvProperties();
  console.log("[refetchTodayOhlcv] Propertiesリセット完了。fetchOHLCVForNewAlerts を実行します");
  fetchOHLCVForNewAlerts();
}


// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 診断関数: 空欄タイムスタンプ行を確認するだけ（書き込みなし）
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function diagOhlcvTimestamps() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
  );
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) { console.log("ohlcv_4h が見つかりません"); return; }
  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) { console.log("データなし"); return; }
  const values = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 4).getValues();
  const MIN_VALID_DATE = new Date("1990-01-01T00:00:00Z");
  const empty = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    const ts = values[i][0];
    const isInvalid = !(ts instanceof Date) || isNaN(ts.getTime()) || ts < MIN_VALID_DATE;
    if (isInvalid) empty.push({ sheetRow: i+2, sym: values[i][2], price: values[i][3] });
  }
  console.log(`タイムスタンプ空欄行数: ${empty.length}`);
  empty.slice(0, 10).forEach(r => console.log(JSON.stringify(r)));
  return { total: empty.length, samples: empty.slice(0, 10) };
}

function diagOneSessionDays(options) {
  options = options || {};
  const daysBack  = options.daysBack  || 14;  // 直近何日間を調べるか
  const minSessions = options.minSessions || 2; // 通常何セッションあるべきか

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
  );
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) { console.log("ohlcv_4h が見つかりません"); return; }

  const now = new Date();
  const cutoff = new Date(now.getTime() - daysBack * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const cutoffStr = Utilities.formatDate(cutoff, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return;

  const values = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 3).getValues();

  // 銘柄×日付 の行数をカウント
  const countMap = {};
  for (const row of values) {
    const ts = row[0], sym = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (!(ts instanceof Date) || !sym) continue;
    const dateStr = Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
    if (dateStr < cutoffStr) continue;
    const key = sym + "|" + dateStr;
    countMap[key] = (countMap[key] || 0) + 1;
  }

  // 1セッションのみの銘柄を抽出
  const missing = {};
  for (const key of Object.keys(countMap)) {
    if (countMap[key] < minSessions) {
      const [sym, date] = key.split("|");
      if (!missing[sym]) missing[sym] = [];
      missing[sym].push(date);
    }
  }

  const symList = Object.keys(missing).sort();
  console.log(`[diagOneSessionDays] 直近${daysBack}日で片方セッション欠落: ${symList.length}銘柄`);
  symList.forEach(sym => console.log(`  ${sym}: ${missing[sym].join(", ")}`));
  return { count: symList.length, details: missing };
}

function refetchSymbolRange(options) {
  options = options || {};
  const daysBack = options.daysBack || 21;

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
  );
  const rawSh  = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.RAW_SHEET);
  const ohlcvSh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!rawSh || !ohlcvSh) { console.log("シートが見つかりません"); return; }

  // 対象銘柄の決定
  let symbols;
  if (options.symbols && options.symbols.length > 0) {
    symbols = options.symbols;
  } else {
    // alerts_raw の全銘柄
    const rawData = rawSh.getRange(
      CONFIG.DATA_START_ROW, 1,
      Math.max(1, rawSh.getLastRow() - CONFIG.DATA_START_ROW + 1),
      RAW_HEADERS.length
    ).getValues();
    const symSet = new Set();
    rawData.forEach(r => {
      const s = String(r[RAW_IDX.symbol_code] || "").trim();
      if (s) symSet.add(s);
    });
    symbols = Array.from(symSet);
  }

  console.log(`[refetchSymbolRange] 対象: ${symbols.length}銘柄 / 直近${daysBack}日`);

  const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const p1 = nowSec - daysBack * 24 * 60 * 60;
  const p2 = nowSec;

  const MIN_VALID_DATE = new Date("1990-01-01T00:00:00Z");
  const LIMIT_MS = 4 * 60 * 1000;
  const startMs = Date.now();

  let fetchedSyms = 0;
  const allData = [];

  for (const sym of symbols) {
    if (Date.now() - startMs > LIMIT_MS) {
      console.log(`[refetchSymbolRange] タイムアウト。${fetchedSyms}/${symbols.length}件完了`);
      break;
    }

    try {
      const result = fetchOHLCVRecent(sym, null, p1, p2);
      if (result && result.length > 0) {
        // タイムスタンプ検証
        const valid = result.filter(row => {
          const ts = row[0];
          return ts instanceof Date && !isNaN(ts.getTime()) && ts >= MIN_VALID_DATE;
        });
        if (valid.length > 0) {
          allData.push(...valid);
          fetchedSyms++;
        }
      }
    } catch(e) {
      console.log(`[refetchSymbolRange] ${sym}: エラー ${e.message}`);
    }

    Utilities.sleep(300);
  }

  console.log(`[refetchSymbolRange] 取得完了: ${fetchedSyms}銘柄 / ${allData.length}行`);

  if (allData.length === 0) {
    console.log("[refetchSymbolRange] 追加データなし");
    return { ok: true, fetched: 0 };
  }

  // シートに追記
  appendRowsToSheet_(ohlcvSh, allData);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  const cleanup = dedupeAndSortOhlcv_(ohlcvSh);
  console.log(`[refetchSymbolRange] cleanup=${JSON.stringify(cleanup)}`);

  console.log("[refetchSymbolRange] 完了");
  return { ok: true, fetched: fetchedSyms, rows: allData.length };
}

// PS は総 500KB の厳しいクォータがあるが、CacheService は 100KB/key × 1000 keys と余裕があり
// 40k キー（~1MB）を 10 チャンクに分けて格納できる。TTL=1800s（resume 600s + 安全マージン）。
const OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_CACHE_META_KEY = "OHLCV_EDT_META";

const EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_RESUME_AFTER_MS = 10 * 1000;
const EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_SAFETY_AFTER_MS = 7 * 60 * 1000;

/**
 * 単一銘柄の特定期間のOHLCVデータを強制再取得し、シートに反映する。
 * データが取得できた場合は B列に "GAP_REPAIR" を記録。
 * データが取得できなかった場合は、B列に "GAP_FAILED" を記録したダミー行を追加。
 */
function refetchSymbolGap(symbol, startDate, endDate) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
  );
  const ohlcvSh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!ohlcvSh) throw new Error("ohlcv_4h シートが見つかりません");

  // 日付文字列を Date に変換（JST基準）
  const start = parseJstDate_(startDate, false);
  const end   = parseJstDate_(endDate, true);
  if (!isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(start) || !isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(end)) {
    throw new Error("日付の形式が不正です。yyyy/mm/dd 形式で指定してください。");
  }

  // マージンを付与（前後3日）
  const MARGIN_DAYS = 3;
  const fetchStart = new Date(start.getTime() - MARGIN_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const fetchEnd   = new Date(end.getTime()   + MARGIN_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

  const startSec = Math.floor(fetchStart.getTime() / 1000);
  const endSec   = Math.floor(fetchEnd.getTime() / 1000);

  console.log(`[refetchSymbolGap] ${symbol} : 指定=${Utilities.formatDate(start, "JST", "MM/dd")}～${Utilities.formatDate(end, "JST", "MM/dd")} / 取得範囲=${Utilities.formatDate(fetchStart, "JST", "MM/dd")}～${Utilities.formatDate(fetchEnd, "JST", "MM/dd")}`);

  // fetchOHLCVRecent を直接呼び出し
  const result = fetchOHLCVRecent(symbol, null, startSec, endSec);

  // 有効なデータ行を抽出（対象日以外の前後マージン分は成功扱いにしない）
  const MIN_VALID_DATE = new Date("1990-01-01T00:00:00Z");
  const targetDateSet = buildDateSetBetween_(start, end);
  const validRows = result ? result.filter(row => {
    const ts = row[0];
    if (!(ts instanceof Date) || isNaN(ts.getTime()) || ts < MIN_VALID_DATE) return false;
    const dateStr = Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
    return !!targetDateSet[dateStr];
  }) : [];

  if (validRows.length > 0) {
    // 成功：B列に "GAP_REPAIR" をセット
    validRows.forEach(row => { row[1] = "GAP_REPAIR"; });

    // シートに追記
    const appended = appendRowsToSheet_(ohlcvSh, validRows);
    if (appended === 0) throw new Error("有効なタイムスタンプ付きOHLCV行がありませんでした");
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();

    const cleanup = dedupeAndSortOhlcv_(ohlcvSh);

    console.log(`[refetchSymbolGap] ${symbol} : appended=${validRows.length}, cleanup=${JSON.stringify(cleanup)}`);
    return { ok: true, symbol, rows: validRows.length, cleanup: cleanup };
  } else {
    // 失敗：ダミー行を追加（B列に "GAP_FAILED"）
    const dummyRows = makeGapFailedRows_(symbol, startDate, {}, {});
    const appended = appendRowsToSheet_(ohlcvSh, dummyRows);
if (appended === 0) throw new Error("GAP_FAILED行のタイムスタンプ生成に失敗しました");

const cleanup = dedupeAndSortOhlcv_(ohlcvSh);
SpreadsheetApp.flush();

console.log(`[refetchSymbolGap] ${symbol} : データなし → ダミー行を追加し、重複排除・ソートしました`);
return { ok: false, symbol, rows: 0, dummy: true, cleanup: cleanup };
  }
}

const HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY = "HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_V1";
const HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_RESUME_HANDLER = "resumeHistoricalOhlcvVolumeRepair";
const HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_BATCH_SYMBOLS = 12;
const HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_MAX_RUN_MS = 3.5 * 60 * 1000;
const HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_RESUME_AFTER_MS = 60 * 1000;
const HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_ROW_CHUNK_SIZE = 800;
const HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_FETCH_BATCH_SYMBOLS = 6;

/**
 * 旧セッション境界で保存済みの ohlcv_4h 履歴を、修正済み parseIntraResponse_ の結果で上書きする。
 * 既存行の B列マーカーは保持し、D:H（open/high/low/close/volume）のみ更新する。
 */
function repairHistoricalOhlcvVolumes() {
  return repairHistoricalOhlcvVolumesInternal_({ dryRun: false });
}

function previewHistoricalOhlcvVolumeRepair() {
  return repairHistoricalOhlcvVolumesInternal_({ dryRun: true, reset: true });
}

function resumeHistoricalOhlcvVolumeRepair() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_RESUME_HANDLER);
  return repairHistoricalOhlcvVolumes();
}

function resetHistoricalOhlcvVolumeRepairState() {
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_RESUME_HANDLER);
  return { ok: true, reset: true };
}

function repairHistoricalOhlcvVolumesInternal_(options) {
  return repairHistoricalOhlcvVolumesChunked_(options);
}

function repairHistoricalOhlcvVolumesLegacyInternal_(options) {
  const opts = options || {};
  const dryRun = !!opts.dryRun;
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const startTime = Date.now();

  let state = readHistoricalVolumeRepairState_(props);
  if (opts.reset || !state || state.dryRun !== dryRun) {
    state = {
      dryRun: dryRun,
      nextIndex: 0,
      totalSymbols: 0,
      updatedRows: 0,
      missingRows: 0,
      fetchedSymbols: 0,
      deferredSymbols: 0,
      parseErrors: 0,
      batches: 0,
      startedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    props.setProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
  }

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
  );
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) throw new Error("ohlcv_4h シートが見つかりません");

  console.log(`[historicalVolumeRepair] start dryRun=${dryRun}, nextIndex=${state.nextIndex}`);
  const context = buildHistoricalVolumeRepairContext_(sh);
  const symbols = context.symbols;
  state.totalSymbols = symbols.length;

  if (symbols.length === 0 || state.nextIndex >= symbols.length) {
    props.deleteProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
    deleteTriggersByHandler_(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_RESUME_HANDLER);
    const complete = buildHistoricalVolumeRepairSummary_(state, true);
    console.log(`[historicalVolumeRepair] complete ${JSON.stringify(complete)}`);
    return complete;
  }

  while (state.nextIndex < symbols.length) {
    if (Date.now() - startTime > HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_MAX_RUN_MS) break;

    const batchSymbols = symbols.slice(
      state.nextIndex,
      Math.min(state.nextIndex + HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_BATCH_SYMBOLS, symbols.length)
    );
    const result = fetchHistoricalVolumeRepairRowsForSymbols_(batchSymbols, context.ranges);

    if (result.transient) {
      state.deferredSymbols += result.deferredSymbols;
      state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      props.setProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
      scheduleHistoricalVolumeRepairResume_();
      console.log(`[historicalVolumeRepair] transient pause nextIndex=${state.nextIndex}: ${result.message}`);
      return buildHistoricalVolumeRepairSummary_(state, false);
    }

    const updates = buildHistoricalVolumeRepairUpdates_(result.rows, context.rowMapByKey);
    const writeSummary = writeHistoricalVolumeRepairUpdates_(sh, updates, dryRun);

    state.updatedRows += writeSummary.rows;
    state.missingRows += countHistoricalVolumeRepairMissingRows_(batchSymbols, context.rowMapByKey, updates.seenKeys);
    state.fetchedSymbols += result.fetchedSymbols;
    state.parseErrors += result.parseErrors;
    state.nextIndex += batchSymbols.length;
    state.batches++;
    state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    props.setProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));

    console.log(`[historicalVolumeRepair] batch nextIndex=${state.nextIndex}/${symbols.length}, rows=${writeSummary.rows}, parseErrors=${result.parseErrors}`);
    if (Date.now() - startTime > HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_MAX_RUN_MS) break;
    Utilities.sleep(200);
  }

  if (state.nextIndex < symbols.length) {
    scheduleHistoricalVolumeRepairResume_();
    return buildHistoricalVolumeRepairSummary_(state, false);
  }

  if (!dryRun) {
    dedupeAndSortOhlcv_(sh);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  }
  props.deleteProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_RESUME_HANDLER);
  const summary = buildHistoricalVolumeRepairSummary_(state, true);
  console.log(`[historicalVolumeRepair] complete ${JSON.stringify(summary)}`);
  return summary;
}

function readHistoricalVolumeRepairState_(props) {
  try {
    const raw = props.getProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

function buildHistoricalVolumeRepairSummary_(state, complete) {
  return {
    ok: true,
    complete: complete,
    dryRun: !!state.dryRun,
    nextIndex: state.nextIndex,
    totalSymbols: state.totalSymbols,
    updatedRows: state.updatedRows,
    missingRows: state.missingRows,
    fetchedSymbols: state.fetchedSymbols,
    deferredSymbols: state.deferredSymbols,
    parseErrors: state.parseErrors,
    batches: state.batches
  };
}

function buildHistoricalVolumeRepairContext_(sheet) {
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  const ranges = {};
  const rowMapByKey = {};
  if (lastRow < 2) return { symbols: [], ranges: ranges, rowMapByKey: rowMapByKey };
