  const values = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 3).getValues();
  for (let i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    const rowNum = i + 2;
    const ts = values[i][0];
    const symbol = String(values[i][2] || "").trim();
    if (!isHistoricalVolumeRepairSessionTs_(ts) || !symbol) continue;

    const key = makeHistoricalVolumeRepairKey_(symbol, ts);
    if (!rowMapByKey[key]) rowMapByKey[key] = [];
    rowMapByKey[key].push(rowNum);

    if (!ranges[symbol]) ranges[symbol] = { min: ts, max: ts };
    if (ts.getTime() < ranges[symbol].min.getTime()) ranges[symbol].min = ts;
    if (ts.getTime() > ranges[symbol].max.getTime()) ranges[symbol].max = ts;
  }

  return {
    symbols: Object.keys(ranges).sort(),
    ranges: ranges,
    rowMapByKey: rowMapByKey
  };
}

function isHistoricalVolumeRepairSessionTs_(ts) {
  if (!(ts instanceof Date) || isNaN(ts.getTime())) return false;
  const hour = parseInt(Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "HH"), 10);
  const minute = parseInt(Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "mm"), 10);
  return minute === 0 && (hour === 9 || hour === 13);
}

function makeHistoricalVolumeRepairKey_(symbol, ts) {
  return String(symbol || "").trim() + "|" + ts.getTime();
}

function fetchHistoricalVolumeRepairRowsForSymbols_(symbols, ranges) {
  const requests = [];
  const metaList = [];
  symbols.forEach(symbol => {
    const range = ranges[symbol];
    if (!range || !range.min || !range.max) return;
    const start = new Date(range.min.getTime() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const end = new Date(range.max.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const pair = buildOhlcvRequestPair_(
      symbol,
      null,
      Math.floor(start.getTime() / 1000),
      Math.floor(end.getTime() / 1000)
    );
const intraIdx = requests.length;
requests.push({
  url: pair.intraUrl,
  muteHttpExceptions: true,
  headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0" }
});

metaList.push({
  symbol: symbol,
  minTime: range.min.getTime(),
  maxTime: range.max.getTime(),
  intraIdx: intraIdx
});
  });

  const summary = {
    rows: [],
    fetchedSymbols: 0,
    deferredSymbols: 0,
    parseErrors: 0,
    transient: false,
    message: ""
  };
  if (requests.length === 0) return summary;

  let responses;
  try {
    responses = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(requests);
  } catch (e) {
    if (isTransientError_(e)) {
      summary.transient = true;
      summary.deferredSymbols = symbols.length;
      summary.message = e.message;
      return summary;
    }
    throw e;
  }

  metaList.forEach(meta => {
    try {
const intraRes = responses[meta.intraIdx];

if (isTransientHttpResponse_(intraRes)) {
  summary.transient = true;
  summary.deferredSymbols++;
  summary.message = "transient HTTP response";
  return;
}

const rows = parseIntraResponse_(meta.symbol, intraRes, null) || [];
      let added = 0;
      rows.forEach(row => {
        const ts = row[0];
        if (!(ts instanceof Date) || isNaN(ts.getTime())) return;
        const time = ts.getTime();
        if (time < meta.minTime || time > meta.maxTime) return;
        summary.rows.push(row);
        added++;
      });
      if (added > 0) summary.fetchedSymbols++;
    } catch (e) {
      summary.parseErrors++;
      console.log(`[historicalVolumeRepair] parse error: ${meta.symbol} - ${e.message}`);
    }
  });

  return summary;
}

function buildHistoricalVolumeRepairUpdates_(fetchedRows, rowMapByKey) {
  const byRowNum = {};
  const seenKeys = {};
  (fetchedRows || []).forEach(row => {
    const ts = row[0];
    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (!(ts instanceof Date) || isNaN(ts.getTime()) || !symbol) return;

    const key = makeHistoricalVolumeRepairKey_(symbol, ts);
    const rowNums = rowMapByKey[key] || [];
    if (rowNums.length === 0) return;

    seenKeys[key] = true;
    const values = row.slice(3, 8);
    rowNums.forEach(rowNum => {
      byRowNum[rowNum] = values.slice();
    });
  });
  return { byRowNum: byRowNum, seenKeys: seenKeys };
}

function writeHistoricalVolumeRepairUpdates_(sheet, updates, dryRun) {
  const rowNums = Object.keys(updates.byRowNum).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  if (rowNums.length === 0) return { rows: 0, chunks: 0 };
  if (dryRun) return { rows: rowNums.length, chunks: 0 };

  let chunks = 0;
  let startRow = rowNums[0];
  let rows = [updates.byRowNum[startRow]];

  for (let i = 1; i < rowNums.length; i++) {
    const rowNum = rowNums[i];
    if (rowNum === rowNums[i - 1] + 1) {
      rows.push(updates.byRowNum[rowNum]);
      continue;
    }
    sheet.getRange(startRow, 4, rows.length, 5).setValues(rows);
    chunks++;
    startRow = rowNum;
    rows = [updates.byRowNum[rowNum]];
  }

  sheet.getRange(startRow, 4, rows.length, 5).setValues(rows);
  chunks++;
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  return { rows: rowNums.length, chunks: chunks };
}

function countHistoricalVolumeRepairMissingRows_(symbols, rowMapByKey, seenKeys) {
  const symbolSet = {};
  symbols.forEach(symbol => symbolSet[symbol] = true);
  let missing = 0;
  Object.keys(rowMapByKey).forEach(key => {
    const symbol = key.split("|")[0];
    if (!symbolSet[symbol] || seenKeys[key]) return;
    missing += rowMapByKey[key].length;
  });
  return missing;
}

function scheduleHistoricalVolumeRepairResume_() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_RESUME_HANDLER);
  ScriptApp.newTrigger(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_RESUME_HANDLER)
    .timeBased()
    .after(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_RESUME_AFTER_MS)
    .create();
}

function repairHistoricalOhlcvVolumesChunked_(options) {
  const opts = options || {};
  const dryRun = !!opts.dryRun;
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const startTime = Date.now();
  const guardMs = 30 * 1000;

  let state = readHistoricalVolumeRepairState_(props);
  if (opts.reset || !state || state.version !== 2 || state.dryRun !== dryRun) {
    state = makeHistoricalVolumeRepairChunkedState_(dryRun);
    props.setProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
  }

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
  );
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) throw new Error("ohlcv_4h シートが見つかりません");

  console.log(`[historicalVolumeRepair] start chunked dryRun=${dryRun}, rowStart=${state.rowStart}, nextSymbolIndex=${state.nextSymbolIndex}`);

  while (Date.now() - startTime < HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_MAX_RUN_MS - guardMs) {
    const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
    state.totalRows = Math.max(0, lastRow - 1);

    if (state.rowStart > lastRow) {
      if (!dryRun) {
        dedupeAndSortOhlcv_(sh);
        SpreadsheetApp.flush();
      }
      props.deleteProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
      deleteTriggersByHandler_(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_RESUME_HANDLER);
      const complete = buildHistoricalVolumeRepairChunkedSummary_(state, true);
      console.log(`[historicalVolumeRepair] complete ${JSON.stringify(complete)}`);
      return complete;
    }

    if (!state.chunkSymbols || state.chunkSymbols.length === 0) {
      const chunk = readHistoricalVolumeRepairChunkSymbols_(sh, state.rowStart, lastRow);
      state.rowEnd = chunk.endRow;
      state.chunkSymbols = chunk.symbols;
      state.nextSymbolIndex = 0;
      state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      props.setProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));

      if (state.chunkSymbols.length === 0) {
        state.scannedRows += Math.max(0, state.rowEnd - state.rowStart + 1);
        state.rowStart = state.rowEnd + 1;
        state.rowEnd = 0;
        state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        props.setProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
        continue;
      }
    }

    const batchSymbols = state.chunkSymbols.slice(
      state.nextSymbolIndex,
      Math.min(state.nextSymbolIndex + HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_FETCH_BATCH_SYMBOLS, state.chunkSymbols.length)
    );
    if (batchSymbols.length === 0) {
      state.scannedRows += Math.max(0, state.rowEnd - state.rowStart + 1);
      state.rowStart = state.rowEnd + 1;
      state.rowEnd = 0;
      state.chunkSymbols = [];
      state.nextSymbolIndex = 0;
      state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      props.setProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
      continue;
    }

    const context = buildHistoricalVolumeRepairChunkContext_(sh, state.rowStart, state.rowEnd, batchSymbols);
    const result = fetchHistoricalVolumeRepairRowsForSymbols_(batchSymbols, context.ranges);
    if (result.transient) {
      state.deferredSymbols += result.deferredSymbols || batchSymbols.length;
      state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      props.setProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
      scheduleHistoricalVolumeRepairResume_();
      console.log(`[historicalVolumeRepair] transient pause rowStart=${state.rowStart}, nextSymbolIndex=${state.nextSymbolIndex}: ${result.message}`);
      return buildHistoricalVolumeRepairChunkedSummary_(state, false);
    }

    const updates = buildHistoricalVolumeRepairUpdates_(result.rows, context.rowMapByKey);
    const writeSummary = writeHistoricalVolumeRepairUpdates_(sh, updates, dryRun);

    state.updatedRows += writeSummary.rows;
    state.missingRows += countHistoricalVolumeRepairMissingRows_(batchSymbols, context.rowMapByKey, updates.seenKeys);
    state.fetchedSymbols += result.fetchedSymbols;
    state.parseErrors += result.parseErrors;
    state.nextSymbolIndex += batchSymbols.length;
    state.batches++;
    state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    props.setProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));

    console.log(`[historicalVolumeRepair] batch row=${state.rowStart}-${state.rowEnd}, symbols=${state.nextSymbolIndex}/${state.chunkSymbols.length}, rows=${writeSummary.rows}, parseErrors=${result.parseErrors}`);

    if (state.nextSymbolIndex >= state.chunkSymbols.length) {
      state.scannedRows += Math.max(0, state.rowEnd - state.rowStart + 1);
      state.rowStart = state.rowEnd + 1;
      state.rowEnd = 0;
      state.chunkSymbols = [];
      state.nextSymbolIndex = 0;
      state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      props.setProperty(HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
    }

    Utilities.sleep(200);
  }

  scheduleHistoricalVolumeRepairResume_();
  console.log(`[historicalVolumeRepair] pause rowStart=${state.rowStart}, nextSymbolIndex=${state.nextSymbolIndex}, updatedRows=${state.updatedRows}`);
  return buildHistoricalVolumeRepairChunkedSummary_(state, false);
}

function makeHistoricalVolumeRepairChunkedState_(dryRun) {
  return {
    version: 2,
    dryRun: dryRun,
    rowStart: 2,
    rowEnd: 0,
    chunkSymbols: [],
    nextSymbolIndex: 0,
    totalRows: 0,
    scannedRows: 0,
    updatedRows: 0,
    missingRows: 0,
    fetchedSymbols: 0,
    deferredSymbols: 0,
    parseErrors: 0,
    batches: 0,
    startedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

function buildHistoricalVolumeRepairChunkedSummary_(state, complete) {
  return {
    ok: true,
    complete: complete,
    dryRun: !!state.dryRun,
    rowStart: state.rowStart,
    rowEnd: state.rowEnd,
    nextSymbolIndex: state.nextSymbolIndex,
    chunkSymbols: state.chunkSymbols ? state.chunkSymbols.length : 0,
    totalRows: state.totalRows,
    scannedRows: state.scannedRows,
    updatedRows: state.updatedRows,
    missingRows: state.missingRows,
    fetchedSymbols: state.fetchedSymbols,
    deferredSymbols: state.deferredSymbols,
    parseErrors: state.parseErrors,
    batches: state.batches
  };
}

function readHistoricalVolumeRepairChunkSymbols_(sheet, rowStart, lastRow) {
  const rowEnd = Math.min(lastRow, rowStart + HISTORICAL_VOLUME_REPAIR_ROW_CHUNK_SIZE - 1);
  const values = sheet.getRange(rowStart, 1, rowEnd - rowStart + 1, 3).getValues();
  const symbolSet = {};

  values.forEach(row => {
    const ts = row[0];
    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (!symbol || !isHistoricalVolumeRepairSessionTs_(ts)) return;
    symbolSet[symbol] = true;
  });

  return {
    endRow: rowEnd,
    symbols: Object.keys(symbolSet).sort()
  };
}

function buildHistoricalVolumeRepairChunkContext_(sheet, rowStart, rowEnd, symbols) {
  const symbolSet = {};
  symbols.forEach(symbol => symbolSet[symbol] = true);

  const ranges = {};
  const rowMapByKey = {};
  if (!rowEnd || rowEnd < rowStart) return { ranges: ranges, rowMapByKey: rowMapByKey };

  const values = sheet.getRange(rowStart, 1, rowEnd - rowStart + 1, 3).getValues();
  for (let i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    const rowNum = rowStart + i;
    const ts = values[i][0];
    const symbol = String(values[i][2] || "").trim();
    if (!symbolSet[symbol] || !isHistoricalVolumeRepairSessionTs_(ts)) continue;

    const key = makeHistoricalVolumeRepairKey_(symbol, ts);
    if (!rowMapByKey[key]) rowMapByKey[key] = [];
    rowMapByKey[key].push(rowNum);

    if (!ranges[symbol]) ranges[symbol] = { min: ts, max: ts };
    if (ts.getTime() < ranges[symbol].min.getTime()) ranges[symbol].min = ts;
    if (ts.getTime() > ranges[symbol].max.getTime()) ranges[symbol].max = ts;
  }

  return { ranges: ranges, rowMapByKey: rowMapByKey };
}

/**
 * 直近N日間のOHLCVデータを高速スキャンし、セッション不足の日を抽出する。
 * 休場日（土日祝・年末年始）は診断対象から除外する。
 * B列に GAP_REPAIR / GAP_FAILED がある日も除外する。
 */
function quickScanMissingSessions(daysBack = 14, minSessions = 2, options) {
  const opts = options || {};
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
  );
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) throw new Error("ohlcv_4h シートが見つかりません");

  const now = getOhlcvBusinessDate_();
  const cutoff = new Date(now.getTime() - daysBack * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const cutoffDateKey = Utilities.formatDate(cutoff, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
  const nowDateKey = Utilities.formatDate(now, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");

  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return { count: 0, details: {} };

  // 休場日セットを取得
  const holidaySet = getMarketHolidaySet_();

  const symbols = getQuickRepairTargetSymbols_(ss);
  const symbolLookup = {};
  symbols.forEach(sym => symbolLookup[sym] = true);

  // ohlcv_4h はA列(timestamp)昇順に整列している前提。直近分だけを末尾から読む。
  const values = readRecentOhlcvRowsByTimestamp_(sh, cutoffDateKey, nowDateKey, 3, {
    deadlineMs: opts.deadlineMs || 0,
    maxRows: opts.maxRows || getQuickRepairRecentScanMaxRows_(symbols.length, daysBack)
  });
  const scanStats = values.scanStats || {};
  if (scanStats.complete === false) {
    writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "直近OHLCVの確認が時間内に終わらなかったため、今回の実行を中断して次回へ回します。", {
      businessDate: formatYmd_(now),
      targetSymbols: symbols.length,
      scannedRows: scanStats.scannedRows || 0,
      maxRows: scanStats.maxRows || 0,
      stoppedBy: scanStats.stoppedBy || ""
    });
    return { count: 0, details: {}, incomplete: true, reason: "scan_incomplete", scanStats: scanStats };
  }

  // 日付×銘柄 ごとの有効セッション数カウント
  const countMap = {};
  const periodMap = {};
  const hasGapMarker = {};
  const dateStrByDayKey = {};
  const seenTargetSymbols = {};

  for (const row of values) {
    const ts = row[0];
    const alertId = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    const sym = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (!(ts instanceof Date) || !sym || !symbolLookup[sym]) continue;
    seenTargetSymbols[sym] = true;

    const dayKey = toJstDayKeyFromDate_(ts);
    const dateStr = dateStrByDayKey[dayKey] || Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
    dateStrByDayKey[dayKey] = dateStr;
    if (dateStr < cutoffDateKey || dateStr > nowDateKey) continue;
    const key = sym + "|" + dateStr;

    // GAP_FAILED はカウントしない（マーカーのみ記録）
    if (alertId === "GAP_FAILED") {
      hasGapMarker[key] = true;
      continue;
    }

    const period = getOhlcvSessionName_(ts);
    if (!period) continue;
    if (!periodMap[key]) periodMap[key] = {};
    periodMap[key][period] = true;
    countMap[key] = Object.keys(periodMap[key]).length;
    if (alertId === "GAP_REPAIR") {
      hasGapMarker[key] = true;
    }
  }

  // 診断対象期間内の全日付を走査し、休場日以外でデータが不足している日を探す
  const missing = {};
  const currentDate = new Date(cutoff);
  while (currentDate <= now) {
    const dateStr = Utilities.formatDate(currentDate, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
    const isHoliday = isMarketHolidayJst_(currentDate, holidaySet);

    // 休場日は診断しない
    if (!isHoliday) {
      symbols.forEach(sym => {
        const key = sym + "|" + dateStr;
        const cnt = countMap[key] || 0;
        if (cnt < minSessions && !hasGapMarker[key]) {
          if (!missing[sym]) missing[sym] = [];
          missing[sym].push(dateStr);
        }
      });
    }
    currentDate.setDate(currentDate.getDate() + 1);
  }

  const symList = Object.keys(missing);
  const seenCount = Object.keys(seenTargetSymbols).length;
  const unseenSymbols = symbols.filter(sym => !seenTargetSymbols[sym]);
  if (scanStats.stoppedBy === "maxRows" && unseenSymbols.length > 0) {
    writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "スキャンウィンドウが maxRows に達し、対象銘柄の一部が走査範囲外でした。QUICK_REPAIR_SCAN_MAX_ROWS の引き上げを検討してください。", {
      businessDate: formatYmd_(now),
      targetSymbols: symbols.length,
      seenSymbols: seenCount,
      unseenSymbols: unseenSymbols.length,
      scannedRows: scanStats.scannedRows || 0,
      maxRows: scanStats.maxRows || 0,
      samples: unseenSymbols.slice(0, 10)
    });
  }
  writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", `直近 ${daysBack} 日を確認しました。休場日を除き、まだ補填対象の不足がある銘柄は ${symList.length} 件です。`, {
    businessDate: formatYmd_(now),
    targetSymbols: symbols.length,
    seenSymbols: seenCount,
    unseenSymbols: unseenSymbols.length,
    missingSymbols: symList.length,
    scannedRows: scanStats.scannedRows || 0,
    maxRows: scanStats.maxRows || 0,
    stoppedBy: scanStats.stoppedBy || ""
  });
  return {
    count: symList.length,
    details: missing,
    sessionInfo: {
      countMap: countMap,
      periodMap: periodMap,
      markerMap: hasGapMarker
    },
    scanStats: scanStats
  };
}

/**
 * JST日付文字列（yyyy/MM/dd）を JST日キー（epoch day）に変換する。
 */
function toJstDayKeyFromDateStr_(dateStr) {
  const normalized = String(dateStr || "").trim().replace(/\//g, "-");
  const d = new Date(normalized + "T00:00:00+09:00");
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;
  return Math.floor((d.getTime() + (9 * 60 * 60 * 1000)) / 86400000);
}

/**
 * Date を JST日キー（epoch day）に変換する。
 */
function toJstDayKeyFromDate_(dateObj) {
  return Math.floor((dateObj.getTime() + (9 * 60 * 60 * 1000)) / 86400000);
}

/**
 * 残タスクに必要なキーだけを対象にセッション数マップを構築する（キー: symbol|yyyy/MM/dd）。
 * GAP_FAILED は除外してカウントする。
 */
function buildSessionCountMapForTasks_(sheet, tasks, startIndex) {
  const targetSymbols = {};
  const targetDayToDateStr = {};
  for (let i = startIndex; i < tasks.length; i++) {
    const t = tasks[i];
    targetSymbols[t.symbol] = true;
    const dayKey = toJstDayKeyFromDateStr_(t.dateStr);
    if (dayKey === null) continue;
    targetDayToDateStr[dayKey] = t.dateStr;
  }

  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return {};
  const values = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 3).getValues();
  const map = {};
  const periodMap = {};
  for (const row of values) {
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]);
    const alertId = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    const sym = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (!ts || !sym || !targetSymbols[sym]) continue;
    if (alertId === "GAP_FAILED") continue;
    const rowDayKey = toJstDayKeyFromDate_(ts);
    const rowDateStr = targetDayToDateStr[rowDayKey];
    if (!rowDateStr) continue;
    const key = sym + "|" + rowDateStr;
    const period = getOhlcvSessionName_(ts);
    if (!period) continue;
    if (!periodMap[key]) periodMap[key] = {};
    periodMap[key][period] = true;
    map[key] = Object.keys(periodMap[key]).length;
  }
  return map;
}

function normalizeDateStrSlash_(value) {
  if (value instanceof Date && !isNaN(value.getTime())) {
    return Utilities.formatDate(value, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
  }
  const s = String(value || "").trim().replace(/-/g, "/");
  const m = s.match(/^(\d{4})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})/);
  if (!m) return "";
  return m[1] + "/" + String(m[2]).padStart(2, "0") + "/" + String(m[3]).padStart(2, "0");
}

function parseJstDate_(value, endOfDay) {
  const dateStr = normalizeDateStrSlash_(value);
  if (!dateStr) return null;
  const isoDate = dateStr.replace(/\//g, "-");
  return new Date(isoDate + (endOfDay ? "T23:59:59+09:00" : "T00:00:00+09:00"));
}

function buildDateSetBetween_(startDate, endDate) {
  const start = parseJstDate_(startDate, false);
  const end = parseJstDate_(endDate, false);
  const set = {};
  if (!start || !end || isNaN(start.getTime()) || isNaN(end.getTime())) return set;
  for (let d = new Date(start.getTime()); d.getTime() <= end.getTime(); d = new Date(d.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)) {
    set[Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd")] = true;
  }
  return set;
}

function getOhlcvSessionName_(ts) {
  if (!isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts)) return "";
  const hour = parseInt(Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "HH"), 10);
  const minute = parseInt(Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "mm"), 10);
  if (hour === 9 && minute === 0) return "AM";
  if (hour === 13 && minute === 0) return "PM";
  return "";
}

function isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts) {
  return getOhlcvSessionName_(ts) !== "";
}

function makeJstSessionTimestamp_(dateStr, sessionName) {
  const normalized = normalizeDateStrSlash_(dateStr);
  if (!normalized) return null;
  const isoDate = normalized.replace(/\//g, "-");
  const hour = sessionName === "PM" ? "13" : "09";
  const ts = new Date(`${isoDate}T${hour}:00:00+09:00`);
  return isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts) ? ts : null;
}

function addSessionPeriod_(periodMap, key, period) {
  if (!period) return;
  if (!periodMap[key]) periodMap[key] = {};
  periodMap[key][period] = true;
}

function countSessionPeriods_(periodMap, key) {
  return periodMap[key] ? Object.keys(periodMap[key]).length : 0;
}

function countMergedSessionPeriods_(basePeriodMap, extraPeriodMap, key) {
  const merged = {};
  if (basePeriodMap[key]) Object.keys(basePeriodMap[key]).forEach(p => merged[p] = true);
  if (extraPeriodMap[key]) Object.keys(extraPeriodMap[key]).forEach(p => merged[p] = true);
  return Object.keys(merged).length;
}

function makeGapFailedRows_(symbol, dateStr, basePeriodMap, extraPeriodMap) {
  const key = symbol + "|" + normalizeDateStrSlash_(dateStr);
  const existing = {};
  if (basePeriodMap && basePeriodMap[key]) Object.keys(basePeriodMap[key]).forEach(p => existing[p] = true);
  if (extraPeriodMap && extraPeriodMap[key]) Object.keys(extraPeriodMap[key]).forEach(p => existing[p] = true);

  const sessions = [];
  if (!existing.AM) sessions.push("AM");
  if (!existing.PM) sessions.push("PM");
  if (sessions.length === 0) return [];

  return sessions.map(sessionName => {
    const ts = makeJstSessionTimestamp_(dateStr, sessionName);
    if (!isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts)) throw new Error("Invalid GAP_FAILED timestamp: " + dateStr);
    return [ts, "GAP_FAILED", symbol, "", "", "", "", ""];
  });
}

function flattenMissingTasks_(details) {
  const tasks = [];
  Object.keys(details || {}).sort().forEach(sym => {
    const seen = {};
    (details[sym] || []).forEach(dateStr => {
      const normalized = normalizeDateStrSlash_(dateStr);
      if (!normalized || seen[normalized]) return;
      seen[normalized] = true;
      tasks.push({ symbol: sym, dateStr: normalized });
    });
  });
  return tasks;
}

function groupQuickRepairTasksBySymbol_(tasks) {
  const map = {};
  tasks.forEach(t => {
    if (!map[t.symbol]) map[t.symbol] = {};
    map[t.symbol][t.dateStr] = true;
  });
  return Object.keys(map).sort().map(sym => ({
    symbol: sym,
    dates: Object.keys(map[sym]).sort()
  }));
}

function countQuickRepairGroupTasks_(groups) {
  return (groups || []).reduce((sum, g) => sum + (g.dates ? g.dates.length : 0), 0);
}

function createQuickRepairState_(version, businessDate, daysBack, minSessions) {
  return {
    version: version,
    businessDate: businessDate,
    daysBack: daysBack,
    minSessions: minSessions,
    totalRows: 0,
    processedGroups: 0,
    processedTasks: 0,
    repairedDates: 0,
    partialDates: 0,
    failedDates: 0,
    deferredDates: 0,
    deferredSymbols: 0,
    staleSymbols: 0,
    staleDates: 0,
    parseErrors: 0,
    markedFailed: 0,

    batches: 0,
    lastScanSymbols: 0,
    lastScanTasks: 0,
    lastProcessedSymbol: "",
    startedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

function loadQuickRepairResumeState_(props, version, businessDate, daysBack, minSessions) {
  try {
    const raw = props.getProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const state = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!state || Number(state.version) !== Number(version)) return null;
    if (state.businessDate !== businessDate) return null;
    if (Number(state.daysBack) !== Number(daysBack)) return null;
    if (Number(state.minSessions) !== Number(minSessions)) return null;
    return state;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

function findQuickRepairStartGroupIndex_(groups, lastProcessedSymbol) {
  const last = String(lastProcessedSymbol || "").trim();
  if (!last) return 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < groups.length; i++) {
    if (String(groups[i].symbol || "") > last) return i;
  }
  return groups.length;
}

function buildQuickRepairSessionInfo_(sheet, tasks) {
  const targetSymbols = {};
  const targetDayToDateStr = {};
  tasks.forEach(t => {
    targetSymbols[t.symbol] = true;
    const dayKey = toJstDayKeyFromDateStr_(t.dateStr);
    if (dayKey !== null) targetDayToDateStr[dayKey] = t.dateStr;
  });

  const info = { countMap: {}, periodMap: {}, markerMap: {} };
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return info;

  const values = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 3).getValues();
  for (const row of values) {
const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]);
const alertId = String(row[1] || "").trim();
const sym = String(row[2] || "").trim();
if (!isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts) || !sym || !targetSymbols[sym]) continue;

    const rowDateStr = targetDayToDateStr[toJstDayKeyFromDate_(ts)];
    if (!rowDateStr) continue;
    const key = sym + "|" + rowDateStr;

    if (alertId === "GAP_FAILED") {
      info.markerMap[key] = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (alertId === "GAP_REPAIR") info.markerMap[key] = true;

    addSessionPeriod_(info.periodMap, key, getOhlcvSessionName_(ts));
    info.countMap[key] = countSessionPeriods_(info.periodMap, key);
  }
  return info;
}

function isTransientHttpResponse_(res) {
  if (!res) return true;
  try {
    const code = res.getResponseCode();
    return code === 429 || code === 500 || code === 502 || code === 503 || code === 504;
  } catch (e) {
    return true;
  }
}

function getQuickRepairRecentScanMaxRows_(symbolCount, daysBack) {
  const count = Math.max(1, Number(symbolCount) || 1);
  const days = Math.max(1, Number(daysBack) || 14) + 7;
  const estimated = Math.ceil(count * days * QUICK_REPAIR_SCAN_ROWS_PER_SYMBOL_DAY);
  return Math.min(QUICK_REPAIR_SCAN_MAX_ROWS, Math.max(QUICK_REPAIR_SCAN_MIN_ROWS, estimated));
}

function purgeInvalidOhlcvTimestampRowsInRange_(sheet, startRow, numRows, context) {
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  const safeStartRow = Math.max(2, Number(startRow) || 2);
  const readRows = Math.min(Math.max(0, Number(numRows) || 0), Math.max(0, lastRow - safeStartRow + 1));
  if (readRows <= 0) return { deletedRows: 0, repairSymbols: 0, convertedRows: 0, scannedRows: 0 };

  const values = sheet.getRange(safeStartRow, 1, readRows, 3).getValues();
  const deleteRows = [];
  const repairSymbolSet = {};
  const samples = [];

  const timestampDateValues = [];
  let convertedRows = 0;
  const convertedSamples = [];

  values.forEach((row, index) => {
    const originalTs = row[0];
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(originalTs);

    if (ts && isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts)) {
      timestampDateValues.push([ts]);

      if (!(originalTs instanceof Date) || !isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(originalTs)) {
        convertedRows++;
        if (convertedSamples.length < 10) {
          convertedSamples.push({
            row: safeStartRow + index,
            before: originalTs == null ? "" : String(originalTs),
            after: Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm"),
            symbol: String(row[2] || "").trim()
          });
        }
      }
      return;
    }

    timestampDateValues.push([originalTs]);

    const invalidReason = !isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts)
      ? "invalid_timestamp"
      : "non_session_timestamp";

    const sheetRow = safeStartRow + index;
    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();

    deleteRows.push(sheetRow);
    if (symbol) repairSymbolSet[symbol] = true;

    if (samples.length < 10) {
      samples.push({
        row: sheetRow,
        timestamp: originalTs == null ? "" : String(originalTs),
        normalizedTimestamp: ts ? Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm") : "",
        reason: invalidReason,
        symbol: symbol
      });
    }
  });

  if (convertedRows > 0) {
    sheet.getRange(safeStartRow, 1, readRows, 1)
      .setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd hh:mm")
      .setValues(timestampDateValues);

    SpreadsheetApp.flush();

    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "OHLCV timestamp を日時形式に変換しました。", {
      context: context || "",
      startRow: safeStartRow,
      scannedRows: readRows,
      convertedRows: convertedRows,
      samples: convertedSamples
    });
  }

  if (deleteRows.length === 0) {
    return {
      deletedRows: 0,
      repairSymbols: 0,
      convertedRows: convertedRows,
      scannedRows: readRows
    };
  }

  deleteRowsByRowNums_(sheet, deleteRows);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  const repairSymbols = Object.keys(repairSymbolSet).sort();
  if (repairSymbols.length > 0) setOhlcvRepairSymbols_(repairSymbols);

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "Invalid OHLCV timestamp rows deleted for a bounded range.", {
    context: context || "",
    startRow: safeStartRow,
    scannedRows: readRows,
    convertedRows: convertedRows,
    deletedRows: deleteRows.length,
    repairSymbols: repairSymbols.length,
    samples: samples
  });

  return {
    deletedRows: deleteRows.length,
    repairSymbols: repairSymbols.length,
    convertedRows: convertedRows,
    scannedRows: readRows
  };
}

function purgeInvalidOhlcvTimestampRows_(sheet, context, maxRows) {
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow <= 1) return { deletedRows: 0, repairSymbols: 0, convertedRows: 0 };

  const scanRows = Math.min(Math.max(1, Number(maxRows) || QUICK_REPAIR_TAIL_CLEANUP_ROWS), lastRow - 1);
  const startRow = lastRow - scanRows + 1;
  const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, scanRows, 3).getValues();

  const deleteRows = [];
  const repairSymbolSet = {};
  const samples = [];

  const timestampDateValues = [];
  let convertedRows = 0;
  const convertedSamples = [];

  values.forEach((row, index) => {
    const originalTs = row[0];
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(originalTs);

    // 正常timestampなら削除しない。A列は Date 形式（日時表示）に統一する。
    if (ts && isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts)) {
      timestampDateValues.push([ts]);

      if (!(originalTs instanceof Date) || !isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(originalTs)) {
        convertedRows++;
        if (convertedSamples.length < 10) {
          convertedSamples.push({
            row: startRow + index,
            before: originalTs == null ? "" : String(originalTs),
            after: Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm"),
            symbol: String(row[2] || "").trim()
          });
        }
      }
      return;
    }

    timestampDateValues.push([originalTs]);

    const invalidReason = !isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts)
      ? "invalid_timestamp"
      : "non_session_timestamp";

    const sheetRow = startRow + index;
    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();

    deleteRows.push(sheetRow);
    if (symbol) repairSymbolSet[symbol] = true;

    if (samples.length < 10) {
      samples.push({
        row: sheetRow,
        timestamp: originalTs == null ? "" : String(originalTs),
        normalizedTimestamp: ts ? Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm") : "",
        reason: invalidReason,
        symbol: symbol
      });
    }
  });

  if (convertedRows > 0) {
    sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, scanRows, 1)
      .setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd hh:mm")
      .setValues(timestampDateValues);

    SpreadsheetApp.flush();

    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "OHLCV timestamp を日時形式に変換しました。", {
      context: context || "",
      scannedRows: scanRows,
      convertedRows: convertedRows,
      samples: convertedSamples
    });
  }

  if (deleteRows.length === 0) {
    return { deletedRows: 0, repairSymbols: 0, convertedRows: convertedRows };
  }

  deleteRowsByRowNums_(sheet, deleteRows);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  const repairSymbols = Object.keys(repairSymbolSet).sort();
  if (repairSymbols.length > 0) setOhlcvRepairSymbols_(repairSymbols);

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "空・無効・対象外時刻のOHLCV行を削除し、対象銘柄を再取得キューへ入れました。", {
    context: context || "",
    scannedRows: scanRows,
    convertedRows: convertedRows,
    deletedRows: deleteRows.length,
    repairSymbols: repairSymbols.length,
    samples: samples
  });

  return {
    deletedRows: deleteRows.length,
    repairSymbols: repairSymbols.length,
    convertedRows: convertedRows
  };
}

function maybePurgeInvalidOhlcvTimestampRows_(sheet, context, maxRows, businessDateKey) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  const key = String(businessDateKey || getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_());
  try {
    const raw = props.getProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
    const state = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    if (state && state.businessDate === key && state.checked === true && Number(state.lastRow || 0) === lastRow && Number(state.maxRows || 0) >= Number(maxRows || 0)) {
      return { skipped: true, deletedRows: 0, repairSymbols: 0, lastCheckedRow: state.lastRow || 0 };
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  const result = purgeInvalidOhlcvTimestampRows_(sheet, context, maxRows);
  if ((result.deletedRows || 0) === 0) {
    props.setProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
      businessDate: key,
      checked: true,
      lastRow: lastRow,
      maxRows: maxRows,
      checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    }));
  } else {
    props.deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
  }
  return result;
}

function purgeSupersededMiddayAmRows_(sheet, businessDateKey, maxRows) {
  if (!sheet) return { deletedRows: 0 };
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow <= 1) return { deletedRows: 0 };

  const dateKey = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(businessDateKey || getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_());
  const middayMarker = makeMiddayRefreshId_(dateKey);
  const scanRows = Math.min(Math.max(1, Number(maxRows) || OHLCV_APPEND_INTEGRITY_SCAN_ROWS), lastRow - 1);
  const startRow = lastRow - scanRows + 1;
  const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, scanRows, 3).getValues();
  const hasMainBySymbol = {};
  const middayRows = [];
  const samples = [];

  values.forEach((row, index) => {
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]);
    if (!ts || formatYmd_(ts) !== dateKey || getOhlcvSessionName_(ts) !== "AM") return;

    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (!symbol) return;

    const marker = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    const sheetRow = startRow + index;
    if (marker === middayMarker) {
      middayRows.push({ row: sheetRow, symbol: symbol });
      return;
    }

    if (marker !== "GAP_FAILED" && !marker.match(/^MIDDAY_/)) {
      hasMainBySymbol[symbol] = true;
    }
  });

  const deleteRows = [];
  middayRows.forEach(item => {
    if (!hasMainBySymbol[item.symbol]) return;
    deleteRows.push(item.row);
    if (samples.length < 10) samples.push(item);
  });

  if (deleteRows.length === 0) return { deletedRows: 0 };

  deleteRowsByRowNums_(sheet, deleteRows);
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "16:00本番行で置き換えられる13:30先行AM行を削除しました。", {
    businessDate: dateKey,
    scannedRows: scanRows,
    deletedRows: deleteRows.length,
    samples: samples
  });
  return { deletedRows: deleteRows.length };
}

function dedupeAndSortOhlcv_(sheet) {
  if (!sheet) return { deletedRows: 0 };

  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow <= 1) return { deletedRows: 0 };

  const invalidCleanup = { deletedRows: 0, convertedRows: 0 };

  const duplicateCleanup = deleteDuplicateOhlcvRowsRecentFast_(
    sheet,
    OHLCV_FINAL_DEDUPE_SCAN_ROWS
  );
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  const afterCleanup = { deletedRows: 0, convertedRows: 0 };

  const sortResult = sortOhlcvSheetByTimestampSafe_(sheet);

  return {
    invalidDeletedRows: invalidCleanup.deletedRows || 0,
    invalidConvertedRows: invalidCleanup.convertedRows || 0,
    duplicateDeletedRows: duplicateCleanup.deletedRows || 0,
    afterInvalidDeletedRows: afterCleanup.deletedRows || 0,
    afterInvalidConvertedRows: afterCleanup.convertedRows || 0,
    sortedRows: sortResult.sortedRows || 0,
    sortSkipped: sortResult.skipped || false
  };
}

function sortOhlcvSheetByTimestampSafe_(sheet) {
  if (!sheet) return { sortedRows: 0, skipped: true, reason: "missing_sheet" };

  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow <= 2) return { sortedRows: 0, skipped: true, reason: "not_enough_rows" };

  const dataRows = lastRow - 1;
  if (dataRows > OHLCV_SAFE_SORT_MAX_ROWS) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "OHLCVソートをスキップしました。対象行数が安全上限を超えています。", {
      dataRows: dataRows,
      maxRows: OHLCV_SAFE_SORT_MAX_ROWS
    });
    return { sortedRows: 0, skipped: true, reason: "too_many_rows", dataRows: dataRows };
  }

  sheet.getRange(2, 1, dataRows, 8).sort([
    { column: 1, ascending: true },
    { column: 3, ascending: true }
  ]);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return { sortedRows: dataRows, skipped: false };
}

function compareOhlcvDuplicatePriority_(a, b) {
  const ar = getOhlcvRowPriority_(a);
  const br = getOhlcvRowPriority_(b);

  if (ar !== br) return ar - br;

  const av = toSafeVolumeNumber_(a[7]);
  const bv = toSafeVolumeNumber_(b[7]);

  // 同じ優先度同士なら、GAP_FAILED回避目的でVolume大きい方を残す
  return bv - av;
}

function getOhlcvRowPriority_(row) {
  const marker = String(row[1] || "").trim();

  if (isProtectedMiddayOhlcvMarker_(marker)) return 0;
  if (isPmLockedMarker_(marker)) return 0;

  // 本番取得・通常取得を最優先
  if (marker && !marker.match(/^MIDDAY_/) && !isPmLockedMarker_(marker) && marker !== "GAP_FAILED") return 1;

  // GAP_REPAIRもMIDDAYより優先
  if (marker === "GAP_REPAIR") return 2;

  // 13:30暫定行は本番より下
  if (marker.match(/^MIDDAY_/)) return 3;

  // 何もない通常行
  if (!marker) return 4;

  // GAP_FAILEDは最後
  if (marker === "GAP_FAILED") return 9;

  return 5;
}

function prepareOhlcvSheetForQuickRepairPause_(sheet, context, rowsAddedThisRun) {
  if (!sheet || (Number(rowsAddedThisRun) || 0) <= 0) return;
  writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "再開前提を壊さないため、追記済みOHLCVを重複排除してtimestamp昇順に戻します。", {
    context: context || "",
    rowsAddedThisRun: rowsAddedThisRun
  });
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();
}

function scheduleQuickRepairResume_(delayMs) {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeQuickRepair");
  ScriptApp.newTrigger("resumeQuickRepair").timeBased().after(delayMs).create();
  writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", `ギャップ修復の再開トリガーを ${Math.round(delayMs / 1000)} 秒後に設定しました。`, {
    handlerName: "resumeQuickRepair",
    delayMs: delayMs
  });
}

function addQuickRepairFailedMarkers_(failedRows, sessionInfo, group) {
  group.dates.forEach(dateStr => {
    const key = group.symbol + "|" + dateStr;
    if (sessionInfo.markerMap[key]) return;
    makeGapFailedRows_(group.symbol, dateStr, sessionInfo.periodMap, null)
      .forEach(row => failedRows.push(row));
    sessionInfo.markerMap[key] = true;
  });
}

function processQuickRepairGroups_(sheet, groups, sessionInfo, minSessions) {
  const MARGIN_DAYS = 3;
  const requests = [];
  const metaList = [];
  const pendingGroups = [];

  groups.forEach(group => {
    const pendingDates = group.dates.filter(dateStr => {
      const key = group.symbol + "|" + dateStr;
      return !sessionInfo.markerMap[key] && (sessionInfo.countMap[key] || 0) < minSessions;
    });
    if (pendingDates.length === 0) return;

    const start = parseJstDate_(pendingDates[0], false);
    const end = parseJstDate_(pendingDates[pendingDates.length - 1], true);
    if (!start || !end) return;

    const fetchStart = new Date(start.getTime() - MARGIN_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const fetchEnd = new Date(end.getTime() + MARGIN_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const pair = buildOhlcvRequestPair_(
      group.symbol,
      null,
      Math.floor(fetchStart.getTime() / 1000),
      Math.floor(fetchEnd.getTime() / 1000)
    );
    const targetDateSet = {};
    pendingDates.forEach(d => targetDateSet[d] = true);
    metaList.push({
      symbol: group.symbol,
      dates: pendingDates,
      targetDateSet: targetDateSet,
      intraIdx: requests.length
    });
    pendingGroups.push({ symbol: group.symbol, dates: pendingDates });
    requests.push({ url: pair.intraUrl, muteHttpExceptions: true, headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0" } });
  });

  const summary = {
    groups: pendingGroups.length,
    tasks: countQuickRepairGroupTasks_(pendingGroups),
    repairedDates: 0,
    partialDates: 0,
    failedDates: 0,
    deferredDates: 0,
    noDataDates: 0,
    markedFailed: 0,

    missingSamples: [],
    rows: 0,
    parseErrors: 0,
    staleSymbols: 0,
    staleDates: 0,
    staleSamples: [],
    appendFailed: false,
    appendFailure: null
  };
  if (requests.length === 0) return summary;

  const responses = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(requests);
  const repairRows = [];
  const failedRows = [];
  const fetchedPeriodMap = {};
  const deferredDateMap = {};
  const staleSymbolMap = {};
  const staleRecords = [];

  metaList.forEach(meta => {
    try {
      const intraRes = responses[meta.intraIdx];
      if (isTransientHttpResponse_(intraRes)) {
        summary.deferredDates += meta.dates.length;
        meta.dates.forEach(dateStr => { deferredDateMap[meta.symbol + "|" + dateStr] = true; });
        return;
      }

      const metaOut = {};
      const result = parseIntraResponse_(meta.symbol, intraRes, null, null, { metaOut: metaOut });
      const staleRecord = buildQuickRepairStaleRecord_(meta, metaOut);
      if (staleRecord) {
        staleSymbolMap[meta.symbol] = staleRecord;
        staleRecords.push(staleRecord);
        summary.staleSymbols++;
        summary.staleDates += meta.dates.length;
        if (summary.staleSamples.length < 10) summary.staleSamples.push(staleRecord);
        return;
      }

      (result || []).forEach(row => {
        const ts = row[0];
        if (!(ts instanceof Date) || isNaN(ts.getTime())) return;
        const rowDateStr = Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
        if (!meta.targetDateSet[rowDateStr]) return;
        row[1] = "GAP_REPAIR";
        repairRows.push(row);
        addSessionPeriod_(fetchedPeriodMap, meta.symbol + "|" + rowDateStr, getOhlcvSessionName_(ts));
      });

    } catch (e) {
      summary.parseErrors++;
      summary.deferredDates += meta.dates.length;
      meta.dates.forEach(dateStr => { deferredDateMap[meta.symbol + "|" + dateStr] = true; });
      writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", `${meta.symbol} の補填レスポンスを解析できませんでした。この銘柄の日付は次回へ回します。`, {
        symbol: meta.symbol,
        dates: meta.dates,
        message: e.message
      });
    }
  });

  // === 日足フォールバック ===
  // 1h で 0 行しか取れなかった (symbol, date) だけを対象に 1d を後追い取得し、
  // AM(09:00)/PM(13:00) を 50/50 出来高分割で合成する。1418 のような薄商い銘柄で
  // Yahoo の 1h データが完全に欠落している日を救うための保険。
  // staleSymbolMap (1h のタイムスタンプ枠だけ来て OHLC が null) もここで救うため、
  // stale 判定でスキップせず必ず 1d を試す。1d でも 0 件 (=1h と 1d の両方が空) なら
  // しきい値判定をスキップして即 GAP_FAILED を書き込み、それ以降の修復対象から外す。
  const dailyFallbackTargets = [];
  const dailyFallbackFailedSet = new Set();
  metaList.forEach(meta => {
    const fallbackDates = meta.dates.filter(dateStr => {
      const key = meta.symbol + "|" + dateStr;
      if (deferredDateMap[key]) return false;
      if (fetchedPeriodMap[key]) return false;
      return true;
    });
    if (fallbackDates.length > 0) {
      dailyFallbackTargets.push({ symbol: meta.symbol, dates: fallbackDates });
    }
  });

  if (dailyFallbackTargets.length > 0) {
    const dailyRequests = [];
    const dailyTargetMeta = [];
    dailyFallbackTargets.forEach(target => {
      const start = parseJstDate_(target.dates[0], false);
      const end = parseJstDate_(target.dates[target.dates.length - 1], true);
      if (!start || !end) return;
      const fetchStart = new Date(start.getTime() - MARGIN_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
      const fetchEnd = new Date(end.getTime() + MARGIN_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
      let pair;
      try {
        pair = buildOhlcvRequestPair_(
          target.symbol,
          null,
          Math.floor(fetchStart.getTime() / 1000),
          Math.floor(fetchEnd.getTime() / 1000)
        );
      } catch (err) {
        return;
      }
      dailyTargetMeta.push(target);
      dailyRequests.push({ url: pair.dailyUrl, muteHttpExceptions: true, headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0" } });
    });

    if (dailyRequests.length > 0) {
      const dailyResponses = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(dailyRequests);
      const dailyFallbackSamples = [];
      const dailyMissSamples = [];
      let dailyMissCount = 0;
      dailyTargetMeta.forEach((target, idx) => {
        const dailyRes = dailyResponses[idx];
        if (!dailyRes || isTransientHttpResponse_(dailyRes)) return;
        let dailyMap;
        try {
          dailyMap = buildDailyOhlcvMapFromYahooResponse_(dailyRes);
        } catch (err) {
          return;
        }
        const dailyMapKeys = Object.keys(dailyMap);
        target.dates.forEach(dateStr => {
          const ymd = dateStr.replace(/\//g, "-");
          const daily = dailyMap[ymd];
          if (!daily || (daily.open == null && daily.close == null)) {
            dailyMissCount++;
            dailyFallbackFailedSet.add(target.symbol + "|" + dateStr);
            if (dailyMissSamples.length < 10) {
              dailyMissSamples.push({
                symbol: target.symbol,
                date: dateStr,
                reason: !daily ? "no_bar_for_date" : "all_null_ohlc",
                availableDates: dailyMapKeys.slice(-5)
              });
            }
            return;
          }

          const open = daily.open != null ? daily.open : daily.close;
          const close = daily.close != null ? daily.close : daily.open;
          const high = daily.high != null ? daily.high : Math.max(open, close);
          const low = daily.low != null ? daily.low : Math.min(open, close);
          const dailyVol = Math.round(Number(daily.volume) || 0);
          const amVol = Math.floor(dailyVol / 2);
          const pmVol = dailyVol - amVol; // 奇数は PM 側を 1 多く

          const key = target.symbol + "|" + dateStr;
          const existing = sessionInfo.periodMap[key] || {};
          let synthRowsForDate = 0;

          if (!existing.AM) {
            const ts = makeJstSessionTimestamp_(dateStr, "AM");
            if (isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts)) {
              repairRows.push([ts, "GAP_REPAIR", target.symbol, open, high, low, close, amVol]);
              addSessionPeriod_(fetchedPeriodMap, key, "AM");
              synthRowsForDate++;
            }
          }
          if (!existing.PM) {
            const ts = makeJstSessionTimestamp_(dateStr, "PM");
            if (isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts)) {
              repairRows.push([ts, "GAP_REPAIR", target.symbol, open, high, low, close, pmVol]);
              addSessionPeriod_(fetchedPeriodMap, key, "PM");
              synthRowsForDate++;
            }
          }
          if (synthRowsForDate > 0) {
            summary.dailyFallbackDates++;
            summary.dailyFallbackRows += synthRowsForDate;
            if (dailyFallbackSamples.length < 10) {
              dailyFallbackSamples.push({ symbol: target.symbol, date: dateStr, dailyVol: dailyVol, rows: synthRowsForDate });
            }
          }
        });
      });
      if (summary.dailyFallbackDates > 0) {
        writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "1h レスポンスに無かった日付を日足から AM/PM 50:50 で合成しました。", {
          fallbackDates: summary.dailyFallbackDates,
          fallbackRows: summary.dailyFallbackRows,
          requestedSymbols: dailyRequests.length,
          samples: dailyFallbackSamples
        });
      }
      if (dailyMissCount > 0) {
        writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "日足フォールバックでも欠落していた (symbol, date) を検出しました。Yahoo 1d がまだ未確定の可能性があります。", {
          missCount: dailyMissCount,
          requestedSymbols: dailyRequests.length,
          samples: dailyMissSamples
        });
      }
    }
  }

  const rowsToAppend = repairRows.concat(failedRows);
  let appendDetail = { rows: 0, persistedRows: [], deletedRows: 0, appendFailed: false };
  if (rowsToAppend.length > 0) {
    appendDetail = appendOhlcvRowsToSheetDetailed_(sheet, rowsToAppend, "quickRepair.append");
    summary.rows = appendDetail.rows || 0;
    summary.appendFailed = !!appendDetail.appendFailed;
    if (summary.appendFailed) {
      summary.appendFailure = {
        requestedRows: appendDetail.requestedRows || rowsToAppend.length,
        validRows: appendDetail.validRows || 0,
        persistedRows: appendDetail.rows || 0,
        deletedRows: appendDetail.deletedRows || 0,
        preWriteSamples: appendDetail.preWriteSamples || []
      };
    }
  }

  const savedPeriodMap = {};
  (appendDetail.persistedRows || []).forEach(row => {
    const ts = row[0];
    const marker = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (marker !== "GAP_REPAIR" || !symbol || !(ts instanceof Date) || isNaN(ts.getTime())) return;
    const dateStr = Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
    addSessionPeriod_(savedPeriodMap, symbol + "|" + dateStr, getOhlcvSessionName_(ts));
  });

  Object.keys(savedPeriodMap).forEach(key => {
    Object.keys(savedPeriodMap[key]).forEach(period => addSessionPeriod_(sessionInfo.periodMap, key, period));
    sessionInfo.countMap[key] = countSessionPeriods_(sessionInfo.periodMap, key);
  });

  if (staleRecords.length > 0) {
    recordIntradayStaleSymbols_(staleRecords, getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_());
  }

  const failMap = readQuickRepairFailCounts_();
  const gapFailedRows = [];
  const gapFailedSamples = [];
  metaList.forEach(meta => {
    meta.dates.forEach(dateStr => {
      const key = meta.symbol + "|" + dateStr;
      if (deferredDateMap[key]) return;
      if (staleSymbolMap[meta.symbol]) return;
      const savedCount = countMergedSessionPeriods_(sessionInfo.periodMap, savedPeriodMap, key);
      const fetchedCount = countMergedSessionPeriods_(sessionInfo.periodMap, fetchedPeriodMap, key);
      if (savedCount >= minSessions) {
        summary.repairedDates++;
        delete failMap[key];
        return;
      }
      if (savedCount >= 1) {
        summary.partialDates++;
        delete failMap[key];
        return;
      }
      // savedCount === 0 → 真の失敗。連続失敗回数を加算し、閾値に達したら GAP_FAILED を書き込む。
      summary.noDataDates++;
      summary.failedDates++;
      if (summary.missingSamples.length < 10) {
        summary.missingSamples.push({
          symbol: meta.symbol,
          date: dateStr,
          sessionsAfterFetch: fetchedCount,
          sessionsAfterSave: savedCount
        });
      }
      const prevCount = Number(failMap[key]) || 0;
      const nextCount = prevCount + 1;
      const dualFailed = dailyFallbackFailedSet.has(key);
      if (dualFailed || nextCount >= QUICK_REPAIR_FAIL_THRESHOLD) {
        try {
          const failedRowsForKey = makeGapFailedRows_(meta.symbol, dateStr, sessionInfo.periodMap, savedPeriodMap);
          failedRowsForKey.forEach(r => gapFailedRows.push(r));
          if (failedRowsForKey.length > 0) {
            summary.markedFailed++;
            if (gapFailedSamples.length < 10) {
              gapFailedSamples.push({
                symbol: meta.symbol,
                date: dateStr,
                attempts: nextCount,
                reason: dualFailed ? "1h_and_1d_empty" : "threshold_reached"
              });
            }
          }
          delete failMap[key];
        } catch (err) {
          // GAP_FAILED 行が作れない場合は失敗カウンタだけ残し、次回再試行。
          failMap[key] = nextCount;
          writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", `${meta.symbol} ${dateStr} の GAP_FAILED 行を作れませんでした。次回も再試行します。`, {
            symbol: meta.symbol,
            date: dateStr,
            message: err && err.message
          });
        }
      } else {
        failMap[key] = nextCount;
      }
    });
  });

  if (gapFailedRows.length > 0) {
    const gapFailedAppend = appendOhlcvRowsToSheetDetailed_(sheet, gapFailedRows, "quickRepair.gapFailed");
    summary.rows += gapFailedAppend.rows || 0;
    if (gapFailedAppend.appendFailed) {
      summary.appendFailed = true;
      summary.appendFailure = summary.appendFailure || {
        requestedRows: gapFailedAppend.requestedRows || gapFailedRows.length,
        validRows: gapFailedAppend.validRows || 0,
        persistedRows: gapFailedAppend.rows || 0,
        deletedRows: gapFailedAppend.deletedRows || 0,
        preWriteSamples: gapFailedAppend.preWriteSamples || []
      };
    }
    writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", `1h と 1d の両方が空、または 0行が ${QUICK_REPAIR_FAIL_THRESHOLD} 回続いた銘柄+日付に GAP_FAILED マーカーを記録しました。次回以降は missing から除外されます。`, {
      markedFailed: summary.markedFailed,
      rowsWritten: gapFailedAppend.rows || 0,
      samples: gapFailedSamples
    });
  }

  writeQuickRepairFailCounts_(failMap);

  return summary;
}

function readQuickRepairFailCounts_() {
  try {
    const raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_FAIL_COUNTS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return {};
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object") return {};
    return parsed;
  } catch (e) {
    return {};
  }
}

function writeQuickRepairFailCounts_(failMap) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const cleaned = {};
  const now = new Date();
  const cutoff = new Date(now.getTime() - QUICK_REPAIR_FAIL_RETAIN_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const cutoffKey = Utilities.formatDate(cutoff, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
  Object.keys(failMap || {}).forEach(key => {
    const count = Number(failMap[key]) || 0;
    if (count <= 0) return;
    const parts = key.split("|");
    if (parts.length !== 2) return;
    const dateStr = normalizeDateStrSlash_(parts[1]);
    if (!dateStr || dateStr < cutoffKey) return;
    cleaned[key] = count;
  });
  if (Object.keys(cleaned).length === 0) {
    props.deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_FAIL_COUNTS_KEY);
  } else {
    props.setProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_FAIL_COUNTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(cleaned));
  }
}

function readOhlcvRepairSymbols_() {
  try {
    const repairSymsJson = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("OHLCV_REPAIR_SYMBOLS");
    return repairSymsJson ? JSON.parse(repairSymsJson).map(sym => String(sym || "").trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];
  } catch (e) {
    return [];
  }
}

function setOhlcvRepairSymbols_(symbols) {
  const set = {};
  readOhlcvRepairSymbols_().forEach(sym => { if (sym) set[sym] = true; });
  (symbols || []).forEach(sym => {
    const s = String(sym || "").trim();
    if (s) set[s] = true;
  });
  const merged = Object.keys(set).sort();
  if (merged.length > 0) {
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("OHLCV_REPAIR_SYMBOLS", JSON.stringify(merged));
  }
  return merged;
}

function readIntradayStaleSymbolsState_() {
  try {
    const raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(OHLCV_INTRADAY_STALE_SYMBOLS_KEY);
    const state = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    if (!state || !Array.isArray(state.records)) return { version: 1, records: [] };
    return state;
  } catch (e) {
    return { version: 1, records: [] };
  }
}

function readIntradayStaleSymbolMap_(businessDateKey) {
  const dateKey = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(businessDateKey || getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_());
  const map = {};
  readIntradayStaleSymbolsState_().records.forEach(record => {
    const symbol = String(record && record.symbol || "").trim();
    if (!symbol) return;
    if (String(record.businessDate || "") !== dateKey) return;
    map[symbol] = record;
  });
  return map;
}

function getActiveIntradayStaleSymbolSet_(businessDateKey) {
  return new Set(Object.keys(readIntradayStaleSymbolMap_(businessDateKey)));
}

function recordIntradayStaleSymbols_(records, businessDateKey) {
  const dateKey = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(businessDateKey || getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_());
  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
  const current = readIntradayStaleSymbolsState_();
  const map = {};

  (current.records || []).forEach(record => {
    const symbol = String(record && record.symbol || "").trim();
    if (!symbol || String(record.businessDate || "") !== dateKey) return;
    map[symbol] = record;
  });

  (records || []).forEach(record => {
    const symbol = String(record && record.symbol || "").trim();
    if (!symbol) return;
    map[symbol] = {
      symbol: symbol,
      businessDate: dateKey,
      targetStartDate: String(record.targetStartDate || ""),
      targetEndDate: String(record.targetEndDate || ""),
      firstTimestampAt: String(record.firstTimestampAt || ""),
      lastTimestampAt: String(record.lastTimestampAt || ""),
      lastNonNullOhlcAt: String(record.lastNonNullOhlcAt || ""),
      reason: String(record.reason || "yahoo_1h_ohlc_stale"),
      updatedAt: nowIso
    };
  });

  const symbols = Object.keys(map).sort().slice(0, OHLCV_INTRADAY_STALE_MAX_RECORDS);
  const nextRecords = symbols.map(sym => map[sym]);
  const nextState = {
    version: 1,
    businessDate: dateKey,
    updatedAt: nowIso,
    records: nextRecords
  };

  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  if (nextRecords.length > 0) {
    props.setProperty(OHLCV_INTRADAY_STALE_SYMBOLS_KEY, JSON.stringify(nextState));
  } else {
    props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_INTRADAY_STALE_SYMBOLS_KEY);
  }

  if ((records || []).length > 0) {
    writeProcessLog_("GAP_REPAIR", "Yahoo 1h stale symbols were recorded and excluded from this business date repair loop.", {
      businessDate: dateKey,
      recordedThisRun: records.length,
      activeRecords: nextRecords.length,
      samples: nextRecords.slice(0, 10)
    });
  }

  return { records: nextRecords.length };
}

function getIsoDateKey_(iso) {
  if (!iso) return "";
  const d = new Date(iso);
  if (!(d instanceof Date) || isNaN(d.getTime())) return "";
  return formatYmd_(d);
}

function buildQuickRepairStaleRecord_(meta, metaOut) {
  if (!meta || !meta.dates || meta.dates.length === 0 || !metaOut) return null;
  if (!Number(metaOut.timestampCount || 0)) return null;

  const sortedDates = meta.dates.map(d => normalizeBusinessDateKey_(d)).sort();
  const targetStartDate = sortedDates[0];
  const targetEndDate = sortedDates[sortedDates.length - 1];
  const lastTimestampDate = getIsoDateKey_(metaOut.lastTimestampAt);
  const lastNonNullDate = getIsoDateKey_(metaOut.lastNonNullOhlcAt);

  if (!lastTimestampDate || lastTimestampDate < targetStartDate) return null;
  if (lastNonNullDate && lastNonNullDate >= targetStartDate) return null;

  return {
    symbol: meta.symbol,
    targetStartDate: targetStartDate,
    targetEndDate: targetEndDate,
    firstTimestampAt: metaOut.firstTimestampAt || "",
    lastTimestampAt: metaOut.lastTimestampAt || "",
    lastNonNullOhlcAt: metaOut.lastNonNullOhlcAt || "",
    reason: "yahoo_1h_ohlc_stale"
  };
}

function getQuickRepairTargetSymbols_(ss) {
  const rawSh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.RAW_SHEET);
  if (!rawSh) return [];
  const lastRow = rawSh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < dataStartRow_()) return [];

  const rowCount = lastRow - dataStartRow_() + 1;
  const signalTypeCol = RAW_IDX.signal_type + 1;
  const symbolCodeCol = RAW_IDX.symbol_code + 1;
  const tvSymbolCol = RAW_IDX.tv_symbol + 1;
  const firstCol = Math.min(signalTypeCol, symbolCodeCol, tvSymbolCol);
  const lastCol = Math.max(signalTypeCol, symbolCodeCol, tvSymbolCol);
  const values = rawSh.getRange(dataStartRow_(), firstCol, rowCount, lastCol - firstCol + 1).getValues();
  const signalTypeOffset = signalTypeCol - firstCol;
  const symbolCodeOffset = symbolCodeCol - firstCol;
  const tvSymbolOffset = tvSymbolCol - firstCol;
  const set = {};

  values.forEach(row => {
    const signalType = String(row[signalTypeOffset] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (signalType !== "BOTTOM") return;
    const symbolCode = String(row[symbolCodeOffset] || "").trim();
    const tvSymbol = String(row[tvSymbolOffset] || "").trim();
    const sym = symbolCode || tvSymbol;
    if (sym) set[sym] = true;
  });

  readOhlcvRepairSymbols_().forEach(sym => { if (sym) set[sym] = true; });

  const staleSet = getActiveIntradayStaleSymbolSet_(getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_());
  const symbols = Object.keys(set).filter(sym => !staleSet.has(sym)).sort();
  if (staleSet.size > 0) {
    writeProcessLog_("GAP_REPAIR", "Skipping symbols whose Yahoo 1h OHLC is stale for this business date.", {
      skippedSymbols: staleSet.size,
      targetSymbolsAfterSkip: symbols.length,
      samples: Array.from(staleSet).slice(0, 10)
    });
  }
  return symbols;
}

function fetchQuickRepairRecentRowsForSymbols_(symbols, fetchStartSec, fetchEndSec, targetStartKey, targetEndKey) {
  const requests = [];
  const metaList = [];
  symbols.forEach(sym => {
    try {
      const pair = buildOhlcvRequestPair_(sym, null, fetchStartSec, fetchEndSec);
      metaList.push({ symbol: sym, intraIdx: requests.length });
      requests.push({ url: pair.intraUrl, muteHttpExceptions: true, headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0" } });
    } catch (e) {
      writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", `${sym} の補填取得URLを作れませんでした。`, { symbol: sym, message: e.message });
    }
  });

  const summary = { symbols: symbols.length, fetchedSymbols: 0, rows: 0, deferredSymbols: 0, parseErrors: 0 };
  if (requests.length === 0) return { rows: [], summary: summary };

  const responses = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(requests);
  const rows = [];
  metaList.forEach(meta => {
    try {
      const intraRes = responses[meta.intraIdx];
      if (isTransientHttpResponse_(intraRes)) {
        summary.deferredSymbols++;
        return;
      }

      const result = parseIntraResponse_(meta.symbol, intraRes, null);
      let added = 0;
      (result || []).forEach(row => {
        const ts = row[0];
        if (!(ts instanceof Date) || isNaN(ts.getTime())) return;
        const dateStr = Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
        if (dateStr < targetStartKey || dateStr > targetEndKey) return;
        row[1] = "GAP_REPAIR";
        rows.push(row);
        added++;
      });
      if (added > 0) {
        summary.fetchedSymbols++;
        summary.rows += added;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      summary.parseErrors++;
      writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", `${meta.symbol} の補填レスポンスをOHLCV行に変換できませんでした。`, {
        symbol: meta.symbol,
        message: e.message
      });
    }
  });

  return { rows: rows, summary: summary };
}

function readRecentOhlcvRowsByTimestamp_(sheet, cutoffDateKey, nowDateKey, numCols, options) {
  const opts = options || {};
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) {
    const emptyRows = [];
    emptyRows.scanStats = { complete: true, scannedRows: 0, maxRows: 0, stoppedBy: "", mode: "empty" };
    return emptyRows;
  }
  const chunkSize = Math.max(100, Number(opts.chunkSize) || QUICK_REPAIR_READ_CHUNK_ROWS);
  const rows = [];
  const maxRows = Math.max(0, Number(opts.maxRows) || 0);
  const deadlineMs = Math.max(0, Number(opts.deadlineMs) || 0);
  const cutoffDayKey = toJstDayKeyFromDateStr_(cutoffDateKey);
  const nowDayKey = toJstDayKeyFromDateStr_(nowDateKey);

  if (opts.assumeSorted !== true) {
    let row = lastRow;
    let scannedRows = 0;
    let stoppedBy = "";

    while (row >= 2) {
      if (deadlineMs && deadlineMs - Date.now() < QUICK_REPAIR_MIN_READ_REMAINING_MS) {
        stoppedBy = "deadline";
        break;
      }
      if (maxRows && scannedRows >= maxRows) {
        stoppedBy = "maxRows";
        break;
      }

      const remainingMaxRows = maxRows ? Math.max(0, maxRows - scannedRows) : chunkSize;
      const numRows = Math.min(chunkSize, row - 1, remainingMaxRows);
      if (numRows <= 0) {
        stoppedBy = "maxRows";
        break;
      }

      const startRow = row - numRows + 1;
      const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, numCols).getValues();
      scannedRows += numRows;

      for (let i = values.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(values[i][0]);
        if (!ts) continue;
        values[i][0] = ts;

        const dayKey = cutoffDayKey !== null ? toJstDayKeyFromDate_(ts) : null;
        const dateStr = dayKey === null ? Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd") : "";
        const afterCutoff = dayKey !== null ? dayKey >= cutoffDayKey : dateStr >= cutoffDateKey;
        const beforeNow = dayKey !== null ? (nowDayKey === null || dayKey <= nowDayKey) : dateStr <= nowDateKey;
        if (afterCutoff && beforeNow) rows.push(values[i]);
      }

      row = startRow - 1;
    }

    rows.scanStats = {
      complete: stoppedBy !== "deadline",
      scannedRows: scannedRows,
      chunkSize: chunkSize,
      maxRows: maxRows,
      stoppedBy: stoppedBy,
      lastRow: lastRow,
      mode: "tail_chunk_unsorted"
    };
    return rows;
  }

  if (cutoffDayKey !== null && nowDayKey !== null && opts.forceChunked !== true) {
    const boundary = findFirstOhlcvRowOnOrAfterDayKey_(sheet, cutoffDayKey, lastRow, deadlineMs);
    if (boundary.incomplete) {
      rows.scanStats = {
        complete: false,
        scannedRows: 0,
        chunkSize: chunkSize,
        maxRows: maxRows,
        stoppedBy: boundary.stoppedBy || "deadline",
        lastRow: lastRow,
        probes: boundary.probes || 0,
        mode: "binary_bulk"
      };
      return rows;
    }

    if (boundary.row > lastRow) {
      rows.scanStats = {
        complete: true,
        scannedRows: 0,
        chunkSize: 0,
        maxRows: maxRows,
        stoppedBy: "",
        lastRow: lastRow,
        startRow: boundary.row,
        probes: boundary.probes || 0,
        mode: "binary_bulk"
      };
      return rows;
    }

    let startRow = Math.max(2, boundary.row - QUICK_REPAIR_SCAN_BOUNDARY_PADDING_ROWS);
    let readRows = lastRow - startRow + 1;
    let stoppedBy = "";
    const effectiveMaxRows = maxRows ? maxRows + QUICK_REPAIR_SCAN_BOUNDARY_PADDING_ROWS : 0;
    if (effectiveMaxRows && readRows > effectiveMaxRows) {
      startRow = lastRow - effectiveMaxRows + 1;
      readRows = effectiveMaxRows;
      stoppedBy = "maxRows";
    }

    if (deadlineMs && deadlineMs - Date.now() < QUICK_REPAIR_MIN_READ_REMAINING_MS) {
      rows.scanStats = {
        complete: false,
        scannedRows: 0,
        chunkSize: readRows,
        maxRows: maxRows,
        stoppedBy: "deadline",
        lastRow: lastRow,
        startRow: startRow,
        probes: boundary.probes || 0,
        mode: "binary_bulk"
      };
      return rows;
    }

    const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, readRows, numCols).getValues();
    for (let i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
      const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(values[i][0]);
      if (!ts) continue;
      values[i][0] = ts;
      const dayKey = toJstDayKeyFromDate_(ts);
      if (dayKey < cutoffDayKey || dayKey > nowDayKey) continue;
      rows.push(values[i]);
    }

    rows.scanStats = {
      complete: !stoppedBy,
      scannedRows: readRows,
      chunkSize: readRows,
      maxRows: maxRows,
      stoppedBy: stoppedBy,
      lastRow: lastRow,
      startRow: startRow,
      probes: boundary.probes || 0,
      mode: "binary_bulk"
    };
    return rows;
  }

  let row = lastRow;
  let scannedRows = 0;
  let stoppedBy = "";

  while (row >= 2) {
    if (deadlineMs && deadlineMs - Date.now() < QUICK_REPAIR_MIN_READ_REMAINING_MS) {
      stoppedBy = "deadline";
      break;
    }
    if (maxRows && scannedRows >= maxRows) {
      stoppedBy = "maxRows";
      break;
    }

    const remainingMaxRows = maxRows ? Math.max(0, maxRows - scannedRows) : chunkSize;
    const numRows = Math.min(chunkSize, row - 1, remainingMaxRows);
    if (numRows <= 0) {
      stoppedBy = "maxRows";
      break;
    }
    const startRow = row - numRows + 1;
    const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, numCols).getValues();
    let sawOlderThanCutoff = false;
    scannedRows += numRows;

    for (let i = values.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(values[i][0]);
      if (!ts) continue;
      values[i][0] = ts;
      const dayKey = cutoffDayKey !== null ? toJstDayKeyFromDate_(ts) : null;
      const dateStr = dayKey === null ? Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd") : "";
      if ((dayKey !== null && dayKey < cutoffDayKey) || (dayKey === null && dateStr < cutoffDateKey)) {
        sawOlderThanCutoff = true;
        break;
      }
      if ((dayKey !== null && (nowDayKey === null || dayKey <= nowDayKey)) || (dayKey === null && dateStr <= nowDateKey)) {
        rows.push(values[i]);
      }
    }

    if (sawOlderThanCutoff) break;
    if (deadlineMs && deadlineMs - Date.now() < QUICK_REPAIR_MIN_READ_REMAINING_MS) {
      stoppedBy = "deadline";
      break;
    }
    row = startRow - 1;
  }

  rows.scanStats = {
    complete: !stoppedBy,
    scannedRows: scannedRows,
    chunkSize: chunkSize,
    maxRows: maxRows,
    stoppedBy: stoppedBy,
    lastRow: lastRow,
    mode: "chunked"
  };
  return rows;
}

function findFirstOhlcvRowOnOrAfterDayKey_(sheet, cutoffDayKey, lastRow, deadlineMs) {
  let lo = 2;
  let hi = lastRow;
  let answer = lastRow + 1;
  let probes = 0;

  while (lo <= hi) {
    if (deadlineMs && deadlineMs - Date.now() < QUICK_REPAIR_MIN_READ_REMAINING_MS) {
      return { incomplete: true, stoppedBy: "deadline", probes: probes };
    }

    const mid = Math.floor((lo + hi) / 2);
    const value = sheet.getRange(mid, 1).getValue();
    probes++;
    const normalized = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(value);
    const dayKey = normalized ? toJstDayKeyFromDate_(normalized) : null;

    if (dayKey === null || dayKey >= cutoffDayKey) {
      answer = mid;
      hi = mid - 1;
    } else {
      lo = mid + 1;
    }
  }

  return { row: answer, probes: probes };
}

/**
 * 一時的なGASサーバーエラーか判定する
 */
function isTransientError_(e) {
  const msg = String((e && e.message) || e || "").toLowerCase();

  return msg.includes("server error") ||
         msg.includes("we're sorry") ||
         msg.includes("javascript engine") ||
         msg.includes("unexpected error") ||
         msg.includes("internal error") ||
         msg.includes("service unavailable") ||
         msg.includes("quota") ||
         msg.includes("timeout") ||
         /\bhttp\s*50[23]\b/.test(msg) ||
         /\b50[23]\s+server\b/.test(msg) ||
         /\bstatus\s*50[23]\b/.test(msg);
}

/**
 * 一時的エラーに対して指数バックオフでリトライするラッパー
 * @param {Function} fn
 * @param {number} maxRetries - デフォルト3回
 * @param {number} baseDelayMs - 初回待機ms（デフォルト2000）
 */
function withRetry_(fn, maxRetries, baseDelayMs) {
  maxRetries = maxRetries || 3;
  baseDelayMs = baseDelayMs || 2000;
  for (var attempt = 0; attempt <= maxRetries; attempt++) {
    try {
      return fn();
    } catch (e) {
      if (attempt < maxRetries && isTransientError_(e)) {
        var delay = baseDelayMs * Math.pow(2, attempt); // 2s→4s→8s
        writeProcessLog_("リトライ", "一時的なエラーのため、少し待って同じ処理を再試行します。", {
          attempt: attempt + 1,
          maxAttempts: maxRetries + 1,
          waitMs: delay,
          message: e.message
        });
        Utilities.sleep(delay);
      } else {
        throw e;
      }
    }
  }
}

/**
 * ギャップ自動修復（再開機能付き）
 * 毎日20:00にトリガー実行されるメイン関数
 */
function quickRepairRecentGaps(options) {
  const opts = options || {};
  const daysBack = opts.daysBack || 14;
  const minSessions = opts.minSessions || 2;
  const batchSymbols = opts.batchSymbols || QUICK_REPAIR_BATCH_SYMBOLS;
  const STATE_VERSION = QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_VERSION;
  const businessDateKey = getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_();

  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", `直近 ${daysBack} 日のOHLCV不足セッションを確認し、必要な銘柄だけ補填します。`, {
    businessDate: businessDateKey,
    minSessions: minSessions,
    batchSymbols: batchSymbols,
    isManual: !!getManualOhlcvBusinessDateKey_()
  });

  // ★ ハードタイムアウト対策: 先にリトライトリガーをセット（10分後）
  // 正常完了時は最後に削除。GASに強制終了されても自動再開できる。
  scheduleQuickRepairResume_(QUICK_REPAIR_SAFETY_AFTER_MS);

  let lock = null;
  let lockAcquired = false;
  try {
    lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
    if (!lock.tryLock(QUICK_REPAIR_LOCK_WAIT_MS)) {
      writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "別のギャップ修復が実行中のため、1分後に再試行します。", {
        businessDate: businessDateKey
      });
      scheduleQuickRepairResume_(QUICK_REPAIR_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
      return { ok: true, incomplete: true, reason: "lock_busy" };
    }
    lockAcquired = true;

    const startTime = Date.now();
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
      PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
    );
    const ohlcvSh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
    if (!ohlcvSh) throw new Error("ohlcv_4h シートが見つかりません");

    const state = loadQuickRepairResumeState_(props, STATE_VERSION, businessDateKey, daysBack, minSessions)
      || createQuickRepairState_(STATE_VERSION, businessDateKey, daysBack, minSessions);
    props.setProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));

    const DEDUPE_GUARD_MS = 90 * 1000;
    const scanDeadlineMs = Math.min(
      startTime + QUICK_REPAIR_SCAN_BUDGET_MS,
      startTime + QUICK_REPAIR_MAX_RUN_MS - DEDUPE_GUARD_MS
    );
    const entryCleanup = maybePurgeInvalidOhlcvTimestampRows_(ohlcvSh, "quickRepairRecentGaps.entry", QUICK_REPAIR_ENTRY_CLEANUP_ROWS, businessDateKey);
    if ((entryCleanup.deletedRows || 0) > 0) {
      writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "入口で空・無効timestamp行を削除したため、今回のスキャンはクリーンな状態で続行します。", entryCleanup);
    }
    const scan = quickScanMissingSessions(daysBack, minSessions, { deadlineMs: scanDeadlineMs });
    if (scan.incomplete) {
      state.deferredDates += 1;
      state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      props.setProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
      scheduleQuickRepairResume_(QUICK_REPAIR_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
      return {
        ok: true,
        incomplete: true,
        reason: scan.reason || "scan_incomplete",
        scanStats: scan.scanStats || {}
      };
    }
    const tasks = flattenMissingTasks_(scan.details);
    const groups = groupQuickRepairTasksBySymbol_(tasks);
    state.lastScanSymbols = scan.count;
    state.lastScanTasks = tasks.length;
    state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    props.setProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
    writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", `補填候補をスキャンしました。対象銘柄グループは ${groups.length}、対象日数は ${tasks.length} です。`, {
      groups: groups.length,
      tasks: tasks.length
    });

    if (groups.length === 0) {
      writeProcessLog_("GAP_REPAIR", "No gap rows needed. Scheduling post-repair cleanup instead of inline full dedupe/sort.", { rowsAddedThisRun: state.totalRows });
      const completeSummary = { ok: true, complete: true, processedGroups: state.processedGroups, processedTasks: state.processedTasks, rows: state.totalRows, deferredDates: state.deferredDates, staleSymbols: state.staleSymbols || 0, staleDates: state.staleDates || 0, parseErrors: state.parseErrors };
      return finalizeQuickRepairWithPostCleanup_(props, state, completeSummary);
      writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "ギャップ修復が完了しました。今回補填対象はありませんでした。", {
        rowsAddedThisRun: state.totalRows,
        deferredDates: state.deferredDates,
        parseErrors: state.parseErrors
      });
    }

    const sessionInfo = scan.sessionInfo || { countMap: {}, periodMap: {}, markerMap: {} };
    let groupIndex = findQuickRepairStartGroupIndex_(groups, state.lastProcessedSymbol);
    if (groupIndex > 0) {
      writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "保存済みの再開位置からGAP修復を続行します。", {
        lastProcessedSymbol: state.lastProcessedSymbol || "",
        resumeGroupIndex: groupIndex,
        totalGroups: groups.length
      });
    }
    while (groupIndex < groups.length) {
      if (Date.now() - startTime > QUICK_REPAIR_MAX_RUN_MS - DEDUPE_GUARD_MS) break;

      const batchGroups = groups.slice(groupIndex, groupIndex + batchSymbols);
      const batchTasks = flattenQuickRepairGroupsToTasks_(batchGroups);
      writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", `不足セッションを補填中です。銘柄グループ ${groupIndex + 1}〜${groupIndex + batchGroups.length} / ${groups.length} を処理します。`, {
        tasks: batchTasks.length
      });
      const result = processQuickRepairGroups_(ohlcvSh, batchGroups, sessionInfo, minSessions);
      SpreadsheetApp.flush();

      if (result.appendFailed) {
        state.stopReason = "append_write_failed";
        state.appendFailure = result.appendFailure || {};
        state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        props.setProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
        deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeQuickRepair");
        writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "OHLCV補填行のA列timestampを保存できなかったため、再開ループを止めました。", {
          groupStart: groupIndex + 1,
          groupEnd: groupIndex + batchGroups.length,
          appendFailure: state.appendFailure
        });
        return { ok: false, stopped: true, reason: "append_write_failed", appendFailure: state.appendFailure };
      }

      groupIndex += batchGroups.length;
      state.processedGroups += batchGroups.length;
      state.processedTasks += countQuickRepairGroupTasks_(batchGroups);
      state.repairedDates += result.repairedDates || 0;
      state.partialDates = (state.partialDates || 0) + (result.partialDates || 0);
      state.failedDates += result.failedDates || 0;
      state.deferredDates += result.deferredDates || 0;
      state.staleSymbols += result.staleSymbols || 0;
      state.staleDates += result.staleDates || 0;
      state.totalRows += result.rows || 0;
      state.parseErrors += result.parseErrors || 0;
      state.markedFailed = (state.markedFailed || 0) + (result.markedFailed || 0);

      state.batches++;
      if ((result.deferredDates || 0) === 0) {
        state.lastProcessedSymbol = batchGroups.length ? batchGroups[batchGroups.length - 1].symbol : (state.lastProcessedSymbol || "");
      }
      state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      props.setProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));

      writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", `補填バッチが完了しました。進捗は ${groupIndex}/${groups.length} 銘柄グループです。`, {
        rowsAdded: result.rows || 0,
        repairedDates: result.repairedDates || 0,
        partialDates: result.partialDates || 0,
        failedDates: result.failedDates || 0,
        deferredDates: result.deferredDates || 0,
        staleSymbols: result.staleSymbols || 0,
        staleDates: result.staleDates || 0,
        markedFailed: result.markedFailed || 0,

        parseErrors: result.parseErrors || 0
      });
      if ((result.deferredDates || 0) > 0) {
        prepareOhlcvSheetForQuickRepairPause_(ohlcvSh, "deferred_dates", state.totalRows);
        scheduleQuickRepairResume_(QUICK_REPAIR_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
        return { ok: true, incomplete: true, reason: "deferred_dates", processedGroups: state.processedGroups, processedTasks: state.processedTasks, rows: state.totalRows, deferredDates: state.deferredDates };
      }
      if (Date.now() - startTime > QUICK_REPAIR_MAX_RUN_MS - DEDUPE_GUARD_MS) break;
      Utilities.sleep(200);
