    }

    if (groupIndex < groups.length) {
      prepareOhlcvSheetForQuickRepairPause_(ohlcvSh, "time_guard_pause", state.totalRows);
      scheduleQuickRepairResume_(QUICK_REPAIR_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
      writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", `実行時間が近づいたため、${groupIndex}/${groups.length} 銘柄グループまで保存して再開します。`, {
        rowsAddedThisRun: state.totalRows
      });
      return { ok: true, incomplete: true, groupIndex: groupIndex, totalGroups: groups.length, rows: state.totalRows };
    }

    writeProcessLog_("GAP_REPAIR", "Gap append phase finished. Scheduling post-repair cleanup instead of inline full dedupe/sort.", { rowsAddedThisRun: state.totalRows });
const completeSummary = {
  ok: true,
  complete: true,
  processedGroups: state.processedGroups,
  processedTasks: state.processedTasks,
  totalGroups: groups.length,
  totalTasks: tasks.length,
  rows: state.totalRows,
  repairedDates: state.repairedDates,
  partialDates: state.partialDates || 0,
  failedDates: state.failedDates,
  deferredDates: state.deferredDates,
  staleSymbols: state.staleSymbols || 0,
  staleDates: state.staleDates || 0,
  markedFailed: state.markedFailed || 0,
  parseErrors: state.parseErrors
};

return finalizeQuickRepairWithPostCleanup_(props, state, completeSummary);

  } catch (e) {
    if (isTransientError_(e)) {
      // 一時的サーバーエラー: throwせずトリガーで再開（GASに「失敗」と記録させない）
      writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "Yahoo FinanceまたはGoogle側の一時的なエラーです。失敗扱いにせず、再開トリガーに任せます。", { message: e.message });
      scheduleQuickRepairResume_(QUICK_REPAIR_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
      return { ok: false, transient: true, message: e.message };
    }
    // 予期しないエラー: 1分後にリトライ
    console.error(`[quickRepair] unexpected error: ${e.message}`);
    writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "予期しないエラーが発生しました。再開トリガーを設定したうえで、GAS側にも失敗として記録します。", { message: e.message });
    scheduleQuickRepairResume_(QUICK_REPAIR_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
    throw e;
  } finally {
    if (lockAcquired && lock) {
      try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (releaseErr) {}
    }
  }
}

/**
 * 再開用トリガーハンドラ
 */
function resumeQuickRepair() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeQuickRepair");
  try {
    return quickRepairRecentGaps();
  } catch (e) {
    if (isTransientError_(e)) {
      writeProcessLog_("ギャップ修復", "再開トリガーで一時的なエラーが発生したため、1分後に再試行します。", {
        message: e.message
      });
      scheduleQuickRepairResume_(QUICK_REPAIR_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
      return { ok: false, transient: true, retried: true, message: e.message };
    }
    throw e;
  }
}

/**
 * クイック修復の進捗状態をリセットする（手動用）
 */
function resetQuickRepairState() {
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_FAIL_COUNTS_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeQuickRepair");
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("quickRepairTrigger");
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_HANDLER);
  console.log("クイック修復の状態をリセットしました。");
}

/**
 * GAP修復の監査用。
 * quickScanMissingSessions は「未処理タスク」だけを見るため、GAP_FAILED/GAP_REPAIR
 * マーカーが付いた未充足日を意図的に除外する。この関数はマーカー付きの未充足も数える。
 */
function auditGapRepairCoverage(daysBack, minSessions) {
  daysBack = daysBack || 14;
  minSessions = minSessions || 2;

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
  );
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) throw new Error("ohlcv_4h シートが見つかりません");

  const now = getOhlcvBusinessDate_();
  const cutoff = new Date(now.getTime() - daysBack * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const cutoffDateKey = Utilities.formatDate(cutoff, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
  const nowDateKey = Utilities.formatDate(now, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
  const holidaySet = getMarketHolidaySet_();
  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return { ok: true, daysBack: daysBack, untreatedShort: 0, attemptedButShort: 0 };

  const targetSymbols = getQuickRepairTargetSymbols_(ss);
  const targetSymbolSet = {};
  targetSymbols.forEach(sym => targetSymbolSet[sym] = true);
  const values = readRecentOhlcvRowsByTimestamp_(sh, cutoffDateKey, nowDateKey, 3);
  const periodMap = {};
  const markerMap = {};
  const failedMarkerMap = {};
  const repairMarkerMap = {};

  for (const row of values) {
    const ts = row[0];
    const alertId = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    const sym = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (!(ts instanceof Date) || !sym || !targetSymbolSet[sym]) continue;

    const dateStr = Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
    if (dateStr < cutoffDateKey || dateStr > nowDateKey) continue;

    const key = sym + "|" + dateStr;
    if (alertId === "GAP_FAILED") {
      markerMap[key] = true;
      failedMarkerMap[key] = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (alertId === "GAP_REPAIR") {
      markerMap[key] = true;
      repairMarkerMap[key] = true;
    }
    addSessionPeriod_(periodMap, key, getOhlcvSessionName_(ts));
  }

  const summary = {
    ok: true,
    daysBack: daysBack,
    minSessions: minSessions,
    symbols: targetSymbols.length,
    scannedRecentRows: values.length,
    untreatedShort: 0,
    attemptedButShort: 0,
    failedMarkedShort: 0,
    repairMarkedShort: 0,
    filledDays: 0,
    samplesUntreated: [],
    samplesAttemptedShort: []
  };

  const symbols = targetSymbols;
  for (let d = new Date(cutoff.getTime()); d <= now; d = new Date(d.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)) {
    if (isMarketHolidayJst_(d, holidaySet)) continue;
    const dateStr = Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
    symbols.forEach(sym => {
      const key = sym + "|" + dateStr;
      const cnt = countSessionPeriods_(periodMap, key);
      if (cnt >= minSessions) {
        summary.filledDays++;
        return;
      }
      const sample = { symbol: sym, dateStr: dateStr, sessions: cnt };
      if (markerMap[key]) {
        summary.attemptedButShort++;
        if (failedMarkerMap[key]) summary.failedMarkedShort++;
        if (repairMarkerMap[key]) summary.repairMarkedShort++;
        if (summary.samplesAttemptedShort.length < 20) summary.samplesAttemptedShort.push(sample);
      } else {
        summary.untreatedShort++;
        if (summary.samplesUntreated.length < 20) summary.samplesUntreated.push(sample);
      }
    });
  }

  console.log(`[auditGapRepair] ${JSON.stringify(summary)}`);
  return summary;
}

function auditEvaluationOhlcvCoverage120() {
  return auditEvaluationOhlcvCoverage({ daysBack: OHLCV_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_DAYS, minSessions: 2 });
}

function auditEvaluationOhlcvCoverage(options) {
  const opts = options || {};
  const daysBack = opts.daysBack || OHLCV_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_DAYS;
  const minSessions = opts.minSessions || 2;
  const scan = quickScanMissingSessions(daysBack, minSessions);
  const audit = auditGapRepairCoverage(daysBack, minSessions);
  const pendingTasks = flattenMissingTasks_(scan.details);
  const summary = Object.assign({}, audit, {
    pendingSymbols: scan.count,
    pendingTasks: pendingTasks.length,
    pendingSamples: pendingTasks.slice(0, 20)
  });
  console.log(`[auditEvaluationOhlcvCoverage] ${JSON.stringify(summary)}`);
  return summary;
}

function repairEvaluationOhlcvCoverage120(options) {
  const opts = Object.assign({}, options || {}, {
    daysBack: (options && options.daysBack) || OHLCV_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_DAYS,
    minSessions: (options && options.minSessions) || 2
  });
  return repairEvaluationOhlcvCoverageInternal_(opts);
}

function resumeEvaluationOhlcvCoverageRepair() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_RESUME_HANDLER);
  return repairEvaluationOhlcvCoverageInternal_({
    daysBack: OHLCV_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_DAYS,
    minSessions: 2,
    resume: true
  });
}

function resetEvaluationOhlcvCoverageRepairState() {
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_STATE_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_RESUME_HANDLER);
  return { ok: true, reset: true };
}

function repairEvaluationOhlcvCoverageInternal_(options) {
  const opts = options || {};
  const daysBack = opts.daysBack || OHLCV_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_DAYS;
  const minSessions = opts.minSessions || 2;
  const isResume = opts.resume === true;
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const startTime = Date.now();
  const guardMs = 30 * 1000;

  let state = isResume ? readEvaluationOhlcvCoverageState_(props) : null;
  if (!state || state.version !== 1 || state.daysBack !== daysBack || state.minSessions !== minSessions) {
    state = makeEvaluationOhlcvCoverageState_(daysBack, minSessions);
  }
  state.runs++;
  state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  props.setProperty(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
  scheduleEvaluationOhlcvCoverageResume_(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_SAFETY_AFTER_MS);

  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
      PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
    );
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
    if (!sh) throw new Error("ohlcv_4h シートが見つかりません");

    while (Date.now() - startTime < EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_MAX_RUN_MS - guardMs) {
      const scan = quickScanMissingSessions(daysBack, minSessions);
      const tasks = flattenMissingTasks_(scan.details);
      state.lastScanSymbols = scan.count;
      state.lastScanTasks = tasks.length;
      state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      props.setProperty(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));

      if (tasks.length === 0) {
        dedupeAndSortOhlcv_(sh);
        SpreadsheetApp.flush();
        props.deleteProperty(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_STATE_KEY);
        deleteTriggersByHandler_(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_RESUME_HANDLER);
        const complete = buildEvaluationOhlcvCoverageSummary_(state, true, { remainingTasks: 0 });
        console.log(`[repairEvaluationOhlcvCoverage] 完了: ${JSON.stringify(complete)}`);
        return complete;
      }

      const groups = groupQuickRepairTasksBySymbol_(tasks);
      const batchGroups = groups.slice(0, EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_BATCH_GROUPS);
      const batchTasks = flattenQuickRepairGroupsToTasks_(batchGroups);
      const sessionInfo = buildRecentSessionInfoForTasks_(sh, batchTasks, daysBack);
      const result = processQuickRepairGroups_(sh, batchGroups, sessionInfo, minSessions);
      SpreadsheetApp.flush();

      state.processedGroups += batchGroups.length;
      state.processedTasks += countQuickRepairGroupTasks_(batchGroups);
      state.repairedDates += result.repairedDates || 0;
      state.failedDates += result.failedDates || 0;
      state.deferredDates += result.deferredDates || 0;
      state.rows += result.rows || 0;
      state.parseErrors += result.parseErrors || 0;
      state.batches++;
      state.lastResult = result;
      state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      props.setProperty(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));

      console.log(`[repairEvaluationOhlcvCoverage] batch=${state.batches} groups=${batchGroups.length} result=${JSON.stringify(result)}`);
      if ((result.deferredDates || 0) > 0) {
        scheduleEvaluationOhlcvCoverageResume_(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
        return buildEvaluationOhlcvCoverageSummary_(state, false, { reason: "deferred_dates" });
      }
      Utilities.sleep(200);
    }

    scheduleEvaluationOhlcvCoverageResume_(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
    return buildEvaluationOhlcvCoverageSummary_(state, false, { reason: "time_limit" });
  } catch (e) {
    scheduleEvaluationOhlcvCoverageResume_(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
    if (isTransientError_(e)) {
      return buildEvaluationOhlcvCoverageSummary_(state, false, { transient: true, message: e.message });
    }
    throw e;
  }
}

function makeEvaluationOhlcvCoverageState_(daysBack, minSessions) {
  return {
    version: 1,
    daysBack: daysBack,
    minSessions: minSessions,
    runs: 0,
    processedGroups: 0,
    processedTasks: 0,
    repairedDates: 0,
    failedDates: 0,
    deferredDates: 0,
    rows: 0,
    parseErrors: 0,
    batches: 0,
    lastScanSymbols: 0,
    lastScanTasks: 0,
    startedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

function readEvaluationOhlcvCoverageState_(props) {
  try {
    const raw = props.getProperty(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_STATE_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

function buildEvaluationOhlcvCoverageSummary_(state, complete, extra) {
  return Object.assign({
    ok: true,
    complete: complete,
    daysBack: state.daysBack,
    minSessions: state.minSessions,
    runs: state.runs,
    processedGroups: state.processedGroups,
    processedTasks: state.processedTasks,
    repairedDates: state.repairedDates,
    failedDates: state.failedDates,
    deferredDates: state.deferredDates,
    rows: state.rows,
    parseErrors: state.parseErrors,
    batches: state.batches,
    lastScanSymbols: state.lastScanSymbols,
    lastScanTasks: state.lastScanTasks
  }, extra || {});
}

function scheduleEvaluationOhlcvCoverageResume_(delayMs) {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_RESUME_HANDLER);
  ScriptApp.newTrigger(EVAL_OHLCV_COVERAGE_RESUME_HANDLER)
    .timeBased()
    .after(delayMs)
    .create();
}

function flattenQuickRepairGroupsToTasks_(groups) {
  const tasks = [];
  (groups || []).forEach(group => {
    (group.dates || []).forEach(dateStr => tasks.push({ symbol: group.symbol, dateStr: dateStr }));
  });
  return tasks;
}

function buildRecentSessionInfoForTasks_(sheet, tasks, daysBack, options) {
  const opts = options || {};
  const info = { countMap: {}, periodMap: {}, markerMap: {} };
  if (!tasks || tasks.length === 0) return info;

  const targetSymbols = {};
  const targetDayToDateStr = {};
  tasks.forEach(t => {
    targetSymbols[t.symbol] = true;
    const dayKey = toJstDayKeyFromDateStr_(t.dateStr);
    if (dayKey !== null) targetDayToDateStr[dayKey] = t.dateStr;
  });

  const now = getOhlcvBusinessDate_();
  const cutoff = new Date(now.getTime() - daysBack * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const cutoffDateKey = Utilities.formatDate(cutoff, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
  const nowDateKey = Utilities.formatDate(now, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd");
  const values = readRecentOhlcvRowsByTimestamp_(sheet, cutoffDateKey, nowDateKey, 3, {
    deadlineMs: opts.deadlineMs || 0,
    maxRows: opts.maxRows || getQuickRepairRecentScanMaxRows_(Object.keys(targetSymbols).length, daysBack)
  });
  const scanStats = values.scanStats || {};
  if (scanStats.complete === false) {
    info.incomplete = true;
    info.scanStats = scanStats;
    return info;
  }

  for (const row of values) {
const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]);
const alertId = String(row[1] || "").trim();
const sym = String(row[2] || "").trim();
if (!isValidOhlcvTimestamp_(ts) || !sym || !targetSymbols[sym]) continue;

    const rowDateStr = targetDayToDateStr[toJstDayKeyFromDate_(ts)];
    if (!rowDateStr) continue;
    const key = sym + "|" + rowDateStr;

    if (alertId === "GAP_FAILED") {
      info.markerMap[key] = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (alertId === "GAP_REPAIR") info.markerMap[key] = true;

    addSessionPeriod_(info.periodMap, key, getOhlcvSessionName_(ts));
    info.countMap[key] = countSessionPeriods_(info.periodMap, key);
  }
  return info;
}

/**
 * 既存の不良 GAP_REPAIR 行（V=0 かつ O=H=L=C）を一括削除する one-shot 関数。
 * 一回だけ手動実行する想定。
 *
 * 削除条件: B=GAP_REPAIR AND V=0 AND O===H===L===C
 * → これに該当しない正常な補填行（V>0 や OHLC バラつきあり）は保持される
 */
function purgeBogusGapRepairRows() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
  );
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) { console.log("ohlcv_4h が見つかりません"); return; }
  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) { console.log("データなし"); return; }
  const values = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 8).getValues();
  const deleteRows = [];
  values.forEach((r, i) => {
    if (String(r[1]) !== "GAP_REPAIR") return;
    const o = Number(r[3]), h = Number(r[4]), l = Number(r[5]), c = Number(r[6]);
    const v = Number(r[7]);
    const vIsZero = (v === 0 || !Number.isFinite(v));
    const ohlcSame = Number.isFinite(o) && o === h && h === l && l === c;
    if (vIsZero && ohlcSame) deleteRows.push(i + 2);
  });
  if (deleteRows.length === 0) { console.log("[purgeBogus] 削除対象なし"); return { ok: true, deleted: 0 }; }
  deleteRowsByRowNums_(sh, deleteRows);
  console.log(`[purgeBogus] 削除: ${deleteRows.length} 行`);
  return { ok: true, deleted: deleteRows.length };
}


const CLEANUP_LEGACY_AUTO_QUICK_REPAIR_KEY = "CLEANUP_LEGACY_AUTO_QUICK_REPAIR_V1";
const CLEANUP_LEGACY_QUICK_REPAIR_DELAY_MS = 60 * 1000;
const OHLCV_RECOVERY_AUDIT_SCAN_ROWS = 90000;
const OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_STATE_KEY = "OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_NORMALIZE_STATE_V1";
const OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_RESUME_HANDLER = "resumeOhlcvRecoveryTimestampNormalization";
const OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_CHUNK_ROWS = 5000;
const OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_MAX_RUN_MS = 3.5 * 60 * 1000;

function previewOhlcvRecovery20260513() {
  return previewOhlcvRecoveryForDate_("2026-05-13");
}

function previewOhlcvRecoveryForDate_(dateKey) {
  return auditOhlcvRecoveryForDate_(normalizeBusinessDateKey_(dateKey));
}

function startOhlcvRecovery20260513() {
  return startOhlcvRecoveryForDate_("2026-05-13");
}

function startOhlcvRecoveryForDate_(dateKey) {
  const normalizedDateKey = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(dateKey);
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_STATE_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_RESUME_HANDLER);
  const normalization = runOhlcvRecoveryTimestampNormalization_(normalizedDateKey, false, true);
  if (normalization.complete) {
    return startOhlcvRecoveryCleanupAndGapRepair_(normalizedDateKey, normalization);
  }
  return normalization;
}

function resumeOhlcvRecoveryTimestampNormalization() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_RESUME_HANDLER);
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const state = readJsonProperty_(props, OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_STATE_KEY, null);
  if (!state) return { ok: true, skipped: true, reason: "no_state" };
  const normalization = runOhlcvRecoveryTimestampNormalization_(state.dateKey, !!state.dryRun, false);
  if (normalization.complete && !normalization.dryRun) {
    return startOhlcvRecoveryCleanupAndGapRepair_(state.dateKey, normalization);
  }
  return normalization;
}

function startOhlcvRecoveryCleanupAndGapRepair_(dateKey, normalizationSummary) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.setProperty(CLEANUP_LEGACY_AUTO_QUICK_REPAIR_KEY, "1");
  const cleanup = cleanupLegacyGapFailedAndEmptyTimestamps(false);
  return {
    ok: true,
    dateKey: dateKey,
    normalization: normalizationSummary || {},
    cleanup: cleanup,
    next: "cleanup completion schedules quickRepairTrigger; quickRepair performs the final protected dedupe"
  };
}

function runOhlcvRecoveryTimestampNormalization_(dateKey, dryRun, reset) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const ss = openAppSpreadsheet_();
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) throw new Error("Missing sheet: " + CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);

  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  let state = readJsonProperty_(props, OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_STATE_KEY, null);
  if (reset || !state || state.dateKey !== dateKey || state.dryRun !== !!dryRun) {
    const startRow = Math.max(2, lastRow - OHLCV_RECOVERY_AUDIT_SCAN_ROWS + 1);
    state = {
      dateKey: dateKey,
      dryRun: !!dryRun,
      nextRow: startRow,
      endRow: lastRow,
      scannedRows: 0,
      convertedRows: 0,
      chunks: 0,
      samples: [],
      startedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    props.setProperty(OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
  }

  const startedAt = Date.now();
  while (state.nextRow <= state.endRow && Date.now() - startedAt < OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_MAX_RUN_MS) {
    const numRows = Math.min(OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_CHUNK_ROWS, state.endRow - state.nextRow + 1);
    const values = sh.getRange(state.nextRow, 1, numRows, 3).getValues();
    const timestampValues = values.map(row => [row[0]]);
    let convertedInChunk = 0;

    values.forEach(function(row, index) {
      const before = row[0];
      if (before instanceof Date && isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(before)) return;
      const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(before);
      if (!ts || !isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts)) return;

      timestampValues[index][0] = ts;
      convertedInChunk++;
      if (state.samples.length < 20) {
        state.samples.push({
          row: state.nextRow + index,
          before: before == null ? "" : String(before),
          after: Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm"),
          symbol: String(row[2] || "")
        });
      }
    });

    if (!dryRun && convertedInChunk > 0) {
      const range = sh.getRange(state.nextRow, 1, numRows, 1);
      range.setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd hh:mm");
      range.setValues(timestampValues);
      SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    }

    state.scannedRows += numRows;
    state.convertedRows += convertedInChunk;
    state.chunks++;
    state.nextRow += numRows;
    state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    props.setProperty(OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
  }

  if (state.nextRow <= state.endRow) {
    scheduleOhlcvRecoveryTimestampNormalization_();
    return buildOhlcvRecoveryTimestampSummary_(state, false);
  }

  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_STATE_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_RESUME_HANDLER);
  return buildOhlcvRecoveryTimestampSummary_(state, true);
}

function buildOhlcvRecoveryTimestampSummary_(state, complete) {
  return {
    ok: true,
    complete: complete,
    dryRun: !!state.dryRun,
    dateKey: state.dateKey,
    scannedRows: state.scannedRows,
    convertedRows: state.convertedRows,
    chunks: state.chunks,
    nextRow: state.nextRow,
    endRow: state.endRow,
    samples: state.samples || []
  };
}

function scheduleOhlcvRecoveryTimestampNormalization_() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_RESUME_HANDLER);
  ScriptApp.newTrigger(OHLCV_RECOVERY_TS_RESUME_HANDLER)
    .timeBased()
    .after(60 * 1000)
    .create();
}

function auditOhlcvRecoveryForDate_(dateKey) {
  const ss = openAppSpreadsheet_();
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  const rawSheet = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.RAW_SHEET);
  if (!sh) throw new Error("Missing sheet: " + CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!rawSheet) throw new Error("Missing sheet: " + CONFIG.RAW_SHEET);

  const bottomSymbols = collectBottomSymbolsForRecoveryDate_(rawSheet, dateKey);
  const targetSymbolSet = {};
  bottomSymbols.forEach(sym => targetSymbolSet[sym] = true);

  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  const scanRows = lastRow > 1 ? Math.min(OHLCV_RECOVERY_AUDIT_SCAN_ROWS, lastRow - 1) : 0;
  const startRow = scanRows > 0 ? lastRow - scanRows + 1 : 2;
  const values = scanRows > 0 ? sh.getRange(startRow, 1, scanRows, 8).getValues() : [];

  const seenKeys = {};
  const sessionMap = {};
  const samples = { emptyTimestamp: [], invalidTimestamp: [], unpaddedTimestamp: [], duplicate: [], protectedAm: [] };
  const summary = {
    ok: true,
    dryRun: true,
    dateKey: dateKey,
    scannedRows: scanRows,
    scanStartRow: startRow,
    bottomSymbols: bottomSymbols.length,
    emptyTimestamp: 0,
    invalidTimestamp: 0,
    nonSessionTimestamp: 0,
    unpaddedTimestamp: 0,
    protectedAmRows: 0,
    duplicateCandidates: 0,
    missingAmSymbols: [],
    missingPmSymbols: [],
    samples: samples
  };

  values.forEach(function(row, index) {
    const sheetRow = startRow + index;
    const rawTs = row[0];
    const rawText = rawTs == null ? "" : String(rawTs).trim();
    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    const marker = String(row[1] || "").trim();

    if (!rawText) {
      summary.emptyTimestamp++;
      pushSample_(samples.emptyTimestamp, { row: sheetRow, symbol: symbol, marker: marker });
      return;
    }

    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(rawTs);
    if (!ts) {
      summary.invalidTimestamp++;
      pushSample_(samples.invalidTimestamp, { row: sheetRow, timestamp: rawText, symbol: symbol, marker: marker });
      return;
    }

    if (!isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts)) {
      summary.nonSessionTimestamp++;
      pushSample_(samples.invalidTimestamp, { row: sheetRow, timestamp: rawText, symbol: symbol, marker: marker });
      return;
    }

    const formatted = formatOhlcvTimestampTextForSheet_(ts);
    if (rawText && rawText !== formatted && rawText.match(/\s9:00(?::00)?$/)) {
      summary.unpaddedTimestamp++;
      pushSample_(samples.unpaddedTimestamp, { row: sheetRow, before: rawText, after: formatted, symbol: symbol });
    }

    if (isProtectedMiddayOhlcvMarker_(marker)) {
      summary.protectedAmRows++;
      pushSample_(samples.protectedAm, { row: sheetRow, timestamp: formatted, symbol: symbol, marker: marker });
    }

    const key = formatted + "|" + symbol;
    if (symbol && seenKeys[key]) {
      summary.duplicateCandidates++;
      pushSample_(samples.duplicate, { row: sheetRow, key: key, firstRow: seenKeys[key], marker: marker });
    } else if (symbol) {
      seenKeys[key] = sheetRow;
    }

    if (formatYmd_(ts) !== dateKey || !targetSymbolSet[symbol]) return;
    if (!sessionMap[symbol]) sessionMap[symbol] = {};
    sessionMap[symbol][getOhlcvSessionName_(ts)] = true;
  });

  bottomSymbols.forEach(function(symbol) {
    const sessions = sessionMap[symbol] || {};
    if (!sessions.AM) summary.missingAmSymbols.push(symbol);
    if (!sessions.PM) summary.missingPmSymbols.push(symbol);
  });

  return summary;
}

function collectBottomSymbolsForRecoveryDate_(rawSheet, dateKey) {
  const lastRow = rawSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < dataStartRow_()) return [];

  const numRows = lastRow - dataStartRow_() + 1;
  const values = rawSheet.getRange(dataStartRow_(), 1, numRows, RAW_HEADERS.length).getValues();
  const set = new Set();

  values.forEach(function(row) {
    const signalType = String(row[RAW_IDX.signal_type] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (signalType !== "BOTTOM") return;

    const symbol = String(row[RAW_IDX.symbol_code] || "").trim();
    if (!symbol) return;

    const receivedAt = row[RAW_IDX.received_at];
    const signalDate = row[RAW_IDX.signal_date];
    const d = receivedAt instanceof Date && !isNaN(receivedAt.getTime())
      ? receivedAt
      : (signalDate instanceof Date && !isNaN(signalDate.getTime()) ? signalDate : null);
    if (!d) return;
    if (formatYmd_(d) === dateKey) set.add(symbol);
  });

  return Array.from(set).sort();
}

function pushSample_(list, item) {
  if (list.length < 20) list.push(item);
}

function readJsonProperty_(props, key, fallback) {
  try {
    const raw = props.getProperty(key);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : fallback;
  } catch (e) {
    return fallback;
  }
}

function scheduleQuickRepairTriggerAfterCleanup_(cleanupSummary) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  if (props.getProperty(CLEANUP_LEGACY_AUTO_QUICK_REPAIR_KEY) !== "1") {
    return { scheduled: false, reason: "not_requested" };
  }

  props.deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeQuickRepair");
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("quickRepairTrigger");
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_HANDLER);

  try {
    ScriptApp.newTrigger("quickRepairTrigger")
      .timeBased()
      .after(CLEANUP_LEGACY_QUICK_REPAIR_DELAY_MS)
      .create();
    props.deleteProperty(CLEANUP_LEGACY_AUTO_QUICK_REPAIR_KEY);
    const payload = {
      handlerName: "quickRepairTrigger",
      delayMs: CLEANUP_LEGACY_QUICK_REPAIR_DELAY_MS,
      deletedRows: cleanupSummary && cleanupSummary.deletedRows ? cleanupSummary.deletedRows : 0,
      invalidSessionTimestamp: cleanupSummary && cleanupSummary.invalidSessionTimestamp ? cleanupSummary.invalidSessionTimestamp : 0,
      emptyTimestamp: cleanupSummary && cleanupSummary.emptyTimestamp ? cleanupSummary.emptyTimestamp : 0
    };
    writeProcessLog_("緊急OHLCV整理", "OHLCV整理が完了したため、ギャップ修復を1分後に自動起動します。", payload);
    console.log(`[cleanupLegacy] quickRepairTrigger scheduled: ${JSON.stringify(payload)}`);
    return { scheduled: true, handlerName: "quickRepairTrigger", delayMs: CLEANUP_LEGACY_QUICK_REPAIR_DELAY_MS };
  } catch (e) {
    const message = e && e.message ? e.message : String(e);
    writeProcessLog_("緊急OHLCV整理", "OHLCV整理後のギャップ修復トリガー作成に失敗しました。", { message: message });
    return { scheduled: false, error: message };
  }
}

/**
 * 旧ロジック由来の残骸を安全に整理する one-shot（再開可能バッチ版）。
 * - 空timestamp行を削除
 * - 09:00 / 13:00 以外のOHLCV timestamp行を削除
 * - 長期滞留の GAP_FAILED を削除
 *
 * 使い方:
 *   cleanupLegacyGapFailedAndEmptyTimestamps(true)   // DryRun（書き込みなし）
 *   cleanupLegacyGapFailedAndEmptyTimestamps(false)  // 本番削除
 */
function cleanupLegacyGapFailedAndEmptyTimestamps(dryRun) {
  const STATE_KEY = "CLEANUP_LEGACY_STATE_V1";
  const RESUME_HANDLER = "resumeCleanupLegacyGapFailedAndEmptyTimestamps";
  const MAX_RUN_MS = 3.5 * 60 * 1000;
  const BATCH_SIZE = 8000;
  const RESUME_DELAY_MS = 60 * 1000;
  const FORCE_PURGE_DAYS = 90; // 長期滞留GAP_FAILEDのみ対象

  const isDryRun = dryRun !== false;
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const nowMs = Date.now();
  const forcePurgeMs = FORCE_PURGE_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  const startTime = Date.now();

  let state = null;
  try {
    const raw = props.getProperty(STATE_KEY);
    state = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
  } catch (e) {
    state = null;
  }

  if (!state || state.dryRun !== isDryRun) {
    state = {
      dryRun: isDryRun,
      nextRow: 2,
      scannedRows: 0,
      emptyTimestamp: 0,
      invalidSessionTimestamp: 0,
      staleGapFailed: 0,
      deletedRows: 0,
      chunks: 0
    };
    props.setProperty(STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
  }

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPREADSHEET_ID")
  );
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) { console.log("ohlcv_4h が見つかりません"); return { ok: false, message: "sheet not found" }; }

  while (Date.now() - startTime < MAX_RUN_MS) {
    const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
    if (state.nextRow > lastRow) {
      deleteTriggersByHandler_(RESUME_HANDLER);
      props.deleteProperty(STATE_KEY);
      const summary = {
        ok: true,
        complete: true,
        dryRun: isDryRun,
        scannedRows: state.scannedRows,
        emptyTimestamp: state.emptyTimestamp,
        invalidSessionTimestamp: state.invalidSessionTimestamp || 0,
        staleGapFailed: state.staleGapFailed,
        deletedRows: state.deletedRows,
        chunks: state.chunks
      };
      if (!isDryRun && (state.deletedRows || 0) > 0) {
        purgeInvalidOhlcvTimestampRows_(sh, "cleanupLegacy.final_timestamp_guard", OHLCV_APPEND_INTEGRITY_SCAN_ROWS);
        SpreadsheetApp.flush();
        PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
        PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
      }
      if (!isDryRun) {
        summary.autoQuickRepair = scheduleQuickRepairTriggerAfterCleanup_(summary);
      }
      console.log(`[cleanupLegacy] 完了: ${JSON.stringify(summary)}`);
      return summary;
    }

    const numRows = Math.min(BATCH_SIZE, lastRow - state.nextRow + 1);
    const values = sh.getRange(state.nextRow, 1, numRows, 3).getValues();
    const deleteRows = [];
    const repairSymbolSet = {};
    let chunkEmpty = 0;
    let chunkInvalidSession = 0;
    let chunkStale = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
      const rowNum = state.nextRow + i;
      const ts = values[i][0];
      const alertId = String(values[i][1] || "").trim();
      const sym = String(values[i][2] || "").trim();

      const isEmptyTs = !(ts instanceof Date) || isNaN(ts.getTime());
      if (isEmptyTs) {
        deleteRows.push(rowNum);
        if (sym) repairSymbolSet[sym] = true;
        chunkEmpty++;
        continue;
      }

      if (!isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts)) {
        deleteRows.push(rowNum);
        if (sym) repairSymbolSet[sym] = true;
        chunkInvalidSession++;
        continue;
      }

      if (alertId !== "GAP_FAILED") continue;
      const ageMs = nowMs - ts.getTime();
      if (ageMs >= forcePurgeMs) {
        deleteRows.push(rowNum);
        if (sym) repairSymbolSet[sym] = true;
        chunkStale++;
      }
    }

    if (!isDryRun && deleteRows.length > 0) {
      deleteRowsByRowNums_(sh, deleteRows);
      const repairSymbols = Object.keys(repairSymbolSet).sort();
      if (repairSymbols.length > 0) setOhlcvRepairSymbols_(repairSymbols);
      state.deletedRows += deleteRows.length;
      state.nextRow = state.nextRow + numRows - deleteRows.length;
    } else {
      state.nextRow += numRows;
    }

    state.scannedRows += numRows;
    state.emptyTimestamp += chunkEmpty;
    state.invalidSessionTimestamp = (state.invalidSessionTimestamp || 0) + chunkInvalidSession;
    state.staleGapFailed += chunkStale;
    state.chunks += 1;
    props.setProperty(STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));

    console.log(`[cleanupLegacy] chunk=${state.chunks} scanned=${state.scannedRows} deleteCandidates=${chunkEmpty + chunkInvalidSession + chunkStale} invalidSession=${chunkInvalidSession} nextRow=${state.nextRow}`);
  }

  deleteTriggersByHandler_(RESUME_HANDLER);
  ScriptApp.newTrigger(RESUME_HANDLER).timeBased().after(RESUME_DELAY_MS).create();
  props.setProperty(STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
  const autoQuickRepairPending = !isDryRun && props.getProperty(CLEANUP_LEGACY_AUTO_QUICK_REPAIR_KEY) === "1";
  console.log(`[cleanupLegacy] 中断: scanned=${state.scannedRows} nextRow=${state.nextRow} 1分後に再開`);
  return { ok: true, incomplete: true, scannedRows: state.scannedRows, nextRow: state.nextRow, autoQuickRepairPending: autoQuickRepairPending };
}

function resumeCleanupLegacyGapFailedAndEmptyTimestamps() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeCleanupLegacyGapFailedAndEmptyTimestamps");
  let dryRun = true;
  try {
    const raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("CLEANUP_LEGACY_STATE_V1");
    const state = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    dryRun = !(state && state.dryRun === false);
  } catch (e) {
    dryRun = true;
  }
  cleanupLegacyGapFailedAndEmptyTimestamps(dryRun);
}

function emergencyStopQuickRepairAndCleanOhlcv() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeQuickRepair");
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("quickRepairTrigger");
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_HANDLER);
  props.deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
  props.setProperty(CLEANUP_LEGACY_AUTO_QUICK_REPAIR_KEY, "1");

  const result = cleanupLegacyGapFailedAndEmptyTimestamps(false);
  if (result && result.incomplete) {
    result.autoQuickRepairPending = true;
    writeProcessLog_("緊急OHLCV整理", "OHLCV整理が未完了のため、整理完了後にギャップ修復を自動起動します。", {
      nextRow: result.nextRow || "",
      scannedRows: result.scannedRows || 0
    });
  }
  return result;
}

function collectTodayRawSymbols_(rawSheet, todayKey) {
  const lastRow = rawSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 5) return [];

  const dataRows = lastRow - 4;
  const scanRows = Math.min(dataRows, 3000);
  const startRow = lastRow - scanRows + 1;

  const values = rawSheet.getRange(startRow, 1, scanRows, RAW_HEADERS.length).getValues();
  const set = {};

  values.forEach(r => {
    const signalType = String(r[RAW_IDX.signal_type] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (signalType !== "BOTTOM") return;
    const symbol = String(r[RAW_IDX.symbol_code] || "").trim();
    if (!symbol) return;

    const signalDateKey = toYmdKeySafe_(r[RAW_IDX.signal_date]);
    const receivedDateKey = toYmdKeySafe_(r[RAW_IDX.received_at]);

    if (signalDateKey === todayKey || receivedDateKey === todayKey) {
      set[symbol] = true;
    }
  });

  return Object.keys(set).sort();
}

function toYmdKeySafe_(v) {
  if (v instanceof Date && !isNaN(v.getTime())) {
    return formatYmd_(v);
  }

  const s = String(v || "").trim();
  if (!s) return "";

  const m = s.match(/(\d{4})[\/\-](\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{1,2})/);
  if (!m) return "";

  const y = m[1];
  const mo = ("0" + m[2]).slice(-2);
  const d = ("0" + m[3]).slice(-2);

  return `${y}-${mo}-${d}`;
}

function emergencyStopOhlcvTimeoutLoop() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  props.deleteProperty(PHASE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(STATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(SYMBOL_LIST_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(MAIN_FULL_BACKFILL_SYMBOLS_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty("LAST_TS_MAP");
  props.deleteProperty("CURRENT_REFRESH_ID");
  props.deleteProperty("SPLIT_QUEUE");
  props.deleteProperty("SPLIT_INDEX");
  props.deleteProperty("OHLCV_SPLIT_CACHE");
  props.deleteProperty("OHLCV_REPAIR_SYMBOLS");

  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
  clearMiddayOhlcvState_();

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "タイムアウトループ停止: OHLCV進捗と修復キューを強制クリアしました。", {});
}

function isUrlFetchDailyLimitError_(err) {
  const msg = err && err.message ? String(err.message) : String(err);
  return msg.indexOf("Service invoked too many times for one day: urlfetch") !== -1;
}

// 当日の rawAlertVolumeMap を CacheService にキャッシュするキー。
// 13:30 と 16:00 で同じ営業日のマップを 2 回計算する無駄を避けるため。
// businessDate が変わったら自動的にミスする（payload 内の businessDate と照合）。
const RAW_ALERT_VOLUME_MAP_CACHE_KEY = "RAW_ALERT_VOLUME_MAP_V1";
const RAW_ALERT_VOLUME_MAP_CACHE_TTL_SEC = 6 * 60 * 60; // 6 時間。13:30→16:00 の再利用に十分
const RAW_ALERT_VOLUME_MAP_CACHE_MAX_BYTES = 90 * 1024; // CacheService の 100KB 上限から安全マージン

function loadCachedRawAlertVolumeMap_(businessDateKey) {
  try {
    const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    const cached = cache.get(RAW_ALERT_VOLUME_MAP_CACHE_KEY);
    if (!cached) return null;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(cached);
    if (!parsed || parsed.businessDate !== businessDateKey) return null;
    return { volumeMap: parsed.volumeMap || {}, stats: parsed.stats || {} };
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

function saveCachedRawAlertVolumeMap_(businessDateKey, result) {
  try {
    const payload = JSON.stringify({
      businessDate: businessDateKey,
      volumeMap: result.volumeMap || {},
      stats: result.stats || {},
      builtAt: new Date().toISOString()
    });
    if (payload.length > RAW_ALERT_VOLUME_MAP_CACHE_MAX_BYTES) return; // 大きすぎる場合はキャッシュしない
    CacheService.getScriptCache().put(RAW_ALERT_VOLUME_MAP_CACHE_KEY, payload, RAW_ALERT_VOLUME_MAP_CACHE_TTL_SEC);
  } catch (e) {
    // ベストエフォート。失敗しても本処理に影響しない
  }
}

function buildRawAlertVolumeMapForBusinessDate_(rawSheet, businessDateKey, options) {
  const map = {};
  const stats = {
    businessDateKey: businessDateKey,
    scannedRows: 0,
    mappedRows: 0,
    skippedNoSymbol: 0,
    skippedNoReceivedAt: 0,
    skippedInvalidVolume: 0,
    skippedOtherDate: 0,
    skippedNonBottom: 0,
    duplicatedKeys: 0
  };

  if (!rawSheet) return { volumeMap: map, stats: stats };

  const lastRow = rawSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < dataStartRow_()) return { volumeMap: map, stats: stats };

  // expectedKeys 付き呼び出しは post-repair cleanup の AM 保護でフィルタ済みの部分集合を返す特殊用途なのでキャッシュをスキップ。
  const cacheOk = !options || !options.expectedKeys || options.expectedKeys.length === 0;
  if (cacheOk) {
    const cached = loadCachedRawAlertVolumeMap_(businessDateKey);
    if (cached) {
      cached.stats = Object.assign({}, cached.stats, { cacheHit: true });
      return cached;
    }
  }

  const result = buildRawAlertVolumeMapForBusinessDateChunked_(rawSheet, businessDateKey, options);
  if (cacheOk && result && result.volumeMap) {
    saveCachedRawAlertVolumeMap_(businessDateKey, result);
  }
  return result;

  const numRows = lastRow - dataStartRow_() + 1;
  const values = rawSheet.getRange(dataStartRow_(), 1, numRows, RAW_HEADERS.length).getValues();

  values.forEach(function(r) {
    stats.scannedRows++;

    const signalType = String(r[RAW_IDX.signal_type] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (signalType !== "BOTTOM") {
      stats.skippedNonBottom++;
      return;
    }

    const symbol = String(r[RAW_IDX.symbol_code] || "").trim();
    if (!symbol) {
      stats.skippedNoSymbol++;
      return;
    }

    const receivedAt = r[RAW_IDX.received_at];
    if (!receivedAt) {
      stats.skippedNoReceivedAt++;
      return;
    }

    const volume = toRawAlertVolumeNumber_(r[RAW_IDX.volume]);
    if (!Number.isFinite(volume)) {
      stats.skippedInvalidVolume++;
      return;
    }

    const ts = rawAlertReceivedAtToOhlcvTimestamp_(receivedAt);
    if (!ts) {
      stats.skippedNoReceivedAt++;
      return;
    }

    const dateKey = Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd");
    if (dateKey !== businessDateKey) {
      stats.skippedOtherDate++;
      return;
    }

    const key = makeRawAlertVolumeKey_(ts, symbol);

    if (map[key] !== undefined) {
      stats.duplicatedKeys++;
      // 同一銘柄・同一4時間足に複数シグナルがある場合は大きい方を採用。
      // 通常は同じ値になる想定。
      map[key] = Math.max(toRawAlertVolumeNumber_(map[key]), volume);
    } else {
      map[key] = volume;
      stats.mappedRows++;
    }
  });

  return { volumeMap: map, stats: stats };
}

function buildRawAlertVolumeMapForBusinessDateChunked_(rawSheet, businessDateKey, options) {
  options = options || {};
  const normalizedBusinessDateKey = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(businessDateKey);
  const map = {};
  const stats = {
    businessDateKey: normalizedBusinessDateKey,
    scannedRows: 0,
    mappedRows: 0,
    skippedNoSymbol: 0,
    skippedNoReceivedAt: 0,
    skippedInvalidVolume: 0,
    skippedOtherDate: 0,
    skippedNonBottom: 0,
    duplicatedKeys: 0,
    readMode: "chunked_minimal_columns",
    stoppedBy: "",
    remainingExpectedKeys: 0
  };

  if (!rawSheet) return { volumeMap: map, stats: stats };

  const lastRow = rawSheet.getLastRow();
  const firstDataRow = dataStartRow_();
  if (lastRow < firstDataRow) return { volumeMap: map, stats: stats };

  const expectedKeys = options.expectedKeys || [];
  const expectedKeySet = new Set(expectedKeys);
  const hasExpectedKeys = expectedKeys.length > 0;
  const readStartIndex = Math.min(RAW_IDX.received_at, RAW_IDX.signal_type, RAW_IDX.symbol_code, RAW_IDX.volume);
  const readEndIndex = Math.max(RAW_IDX.received_at, RAW_IDX.signal_type, RAW_IDX.symbol_code, RAW_IDX.volume);
  const readStartCol = readStartIndex + 1;
  const readCols = readEndIndex - readStartIndex + 1;
  const idxReceivedAt = RAW_IDX.received_at - readStartIndex;
  const idxSignalType = RAW_IDX.signal_type - readStartIndex;
  const idxSymbol = RAW_IDX.symbol_code - readStartIndex;
  const idxVolume = RAW_IDX.volume - readStartIndex;

  const totalDataRows = lastRow - firstDataRow + 1;
  const chunkRows = Math.max(500, Number(options.chunkRows || OHLCV_RAW_ALERT_VOLUME_MAP_CHUNK_ROWS || 5000));
  const maxScanRows = Number(options.maxScanRows || 0);
  let rowsBudget = maxScanRows > 0 ? Math.min(maxScanRows, totalDataRows) : totalDataRows;
  let endRow = lastRow;

  while (endRow >= firstDataRow && rowsBudget > 0) {
    if (options.deadlineMs && Date.now() > Number(options.deadlineMs) - 15000) {
      stats.stoppedBy = "deadline";
      break;
    }

    const count = Math.min(chunkRows, rowsBudget, endRow - firstDataRow + 1);
    const startRow = endRow - count + 1;
    const values = rawSheet.getRange(startRow, readStartCol, count, readCols).getValues();
    let datedRows = 0;
    let olderDatedRows = 0;
    let targetOrNewerDatedRows = 0;

    values.forEach(function(r) {
      stats.scannedRows++;

      const signalType = String(r[idxSignalType] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
      if (signalType !== "BOTTOM") {
        stats.skippedNonBottom++;
        return;
      }

      const symbol = String(r[idxSymbol] || "").trim();
      if (!symbol) {
        stats.skippedNoSymbol++;
        return;
      }

      const receivedAt = r[idxReceivedAt];
      if (!receivedAt) {
        stats.skippedNoReceivedAt++;
        return;
      }

      const volume = toRawAlertVolumeNumber_(r[idxVolume]);
      if (!Number.isFinite(volume)) {
        stats.skippedInvalidVolume++;
        return;
      }

      const ts = rawAlertReceivedAtToOhlcvTimestamp_(receivedAt);
      if (!ts) {
        stats.skippedNoReceivedAt++;
        return;
      }

      const dateKey = Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd");
      datedRows++;
      if (dateKey < normalizedBusinessDateKey) olderDatedRows++;
      if (dateKey >= normalizedBusinessDateKey) targetOrNewerDatedRows++;

      if (dateKey !== normalizedBusinessDateKey) {
        stats.skippedOtherDate++;
        return;
      }

      const key = makeRawAlertVolumeKey_(ts, symbol);
      if (hasExpectedKeys && !expectedKeySet.has(key)) return;

      if (map[key] !== undefined) {
        stats.duplicatedKeys++;
        map[key] = Math.max(toRawAlertVolumeNumber_(map[key]), volume);
      } else {
        map[key] = volume;
        stats.mappedRows++;
      }
      if (hasExpectedKeys) expectedKeySet.delete(key);
    });

    if (hasExpectedKeys && expectedKeySet.size === 0) {
      stats.stoppedBy = "all_expected_keys_found";
      break;
    }

    endRow = startRow - 1;
    rowsBudget -= count;

    if (options.stopAtOlderDateBoundary && datedRows > 0 && olderDatedRows === datedRows && targetOrNewerDatedRows === 0) {
      stats.stoppedBy = "older_date_boundary";
      break;
    }
  }

  if (!stats.stoppedBy && rowsBudget <= 0 && endRow >= firstDataRow) stats.stoppedBy = "max_scan_rows";
  stats.remainingExpectedKeys = expectedKeySet.size;

  return { volumeMap: map, stats: stats };
}

function rawAlertReceivedAtToOhlcvTimestamp_(receivedAt) {
  const d = receivedAt instanceof Date ? receivedAt : new Date(receivedAt);
  if (!(d instanceof Date) || isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;

  const dateKey = Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy-MM-dd");
  const hour = Number(Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "H"));
  const minute = Number(Utilities.formatDate(d, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "m"));

  // TradingViewの4時間足アラート想定:
  // 13:00〜13:30頃に来るAM確定アラート → 09:00行
  // 15:30以降に来るPM確定アラート → 13:00行
  //
  // 14:00未満はAM扱い、14:00以降はPM扱いにする。
  const sessionHour = (hour < 14 || (hour === 14 && minute === 0 && false)) ? 9 : 13;

  return new Date(`${dateKey}T${String(sessionHour).padStart(2, "0")}:00:00+09:00`);
}

function makeRawAlertVolumeKey_(timestamp, symbol) {
  const tsText = formatOhlcvTimestampTextForSheet_(timestamp);
  return tsText + "|" + String(symbol || "").trim();
}

function toRawAlertVolumeNumber_(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") return NaN;
  const n = Number(String(value).replace(/,/g, ""));
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : NaN;
}

function applyRawAlertVolumesToOhlcvRows_(rows, rawAlertVolumeMap) {
  let overridden = 0;

  const out = (rows || []).map(function(row) {
    const copied = row.slice();

    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(copied[0]);
    const symbol = String(copied[2] || "").trim();

    if (!ts || !symbol) return copied;

    const key = makeRawAlertVolumeKey_(ts, symbol);
    const rawVolume = rawAlertVolumeMap[key];

    if (rawVolume !== undefined && Number.isFinite(Number(rawVolume))) {
      copied[7] = Number(rawVolume);
      copied[1] = makeProtectedMiddayRefreshId_(formatYmd_(ts));
      overridden++;
    }

    return copied;
  });

  return {
    rows: out,
    overridden: overridden
  };
}

const OHLCV_PHASE4_RECENT_SCAN_ROWS_LIGHT = 30000;
const OHLCV_PHASE4_INVALID_SCAN_ROWS_LIGHT = 2000;

function finishOhlcvPhase4Light_(ohlcvSheet, todayKey, startTime) {
  const beforeLastRow = ohlcvSheet.getLastRow();

  const result = {
    beforeLastRow: beforeLastRow,
    invalidDeletedRows: 0,
    invalidConvertedRows: 0,
    supersededMiddayRows: 0,
    duplicateDeletedRows: 0,
    afterLastRow: beforeLastRow
  };

  if (beforeLastRow <= 1) {
    return result;
  }

  const invalidCleanup = { deletedRows: 0, convertedRows: 0 };

  result.invalidDeletedRows = 0;
  result.invalidConvertedRows = 0;

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  const middayCleanup = purgeSupersededMiddayAmRows_(
    ohlcvSheet,
    todayKey,
    OHLCV_PHASE4_RECENT_SCAN_ROWS_LIGHT
  );

  result.supersededMiddayRows = middayCleanup.deletedRows || 0;

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  result.duplicateDeletedRows = 0;
  result.afterLastRow = ohlcvSheet.getLastRow();

  debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase4.light.complete", {
    beforeLastRow: result.beforeLastRow,
    afterLastRow: result.afterLastRow,
    invalidDeletedRows: result.invalidDeletedRows,
    invalidConvertedRows: result.invalidConvertedRows,
    supersededMiddayRows: result.supersededMiddayRows,
    duplicateDeletedRows: result.duplicateDeletedRows,
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - startTime
  });

  return result;
}

function finishOhlcvPhase4UltraLight_(ohlcvSheet, todayKey, startTime) {
  const beforeLastRow = ohlcvSheet.getLastRow();
  const result = {
    beforeLastRow: beforeLastRow,
    invalidDeletedRows: 0,
    invalidConvertedRows: 0,
    duplicateDeletedRows: 0,
    afterLastRow: beforeLastRow
  };

  if (beforeLastRow <= 1) return result;

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  result.invalidDeletedRows = 0;
  result.invalidConvertedRows = 0;
  result.afterLastRow = ohlcvSheet.getLastRow();

  debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.phase4.ultra_light.complete", {
    beforeLastRow: result.beforeLastRow,
    afterLastRow: result.afterLastRow,
    invalidDeletedRows: result.invalidDeletedRows,
    invalidConvertedRows: result.invalidConvertedRows,
    duplicateDeletedRows: result.duplicateDeletedRows,
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - startTime
  });

  return result;
}

function deleteDuplicateOhlcvRowsRecentFast_(sheet, maxRows) {
  if (!sheet) return { deletedRows: 0 };

  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow <= 2) return { deletedRows: 0 };

  const scanRows = Math.min(Math.max(1, Number(maxRows) || 30000), lastRow - 1);
  const startRow = lastRow - scanRows + 1;

  const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, scanRows, 8).getValues();

  const bestByKey = {};
  const items = [];

  values.forEach(function(row, index) {
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]);
    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();

    if (!ts || !symbol) return;

    const key = formatOhlcvTimestampTextForSheet_(ts) + "|" + symbol;
    const sheetRow = startRow + index;

    const item = {
      key: key,
      row: row,
      sheetRow: sheetRow
    };

    items.push(item);

    const current = bestByKey[key];

    if (!current) {
      bestByKey[key] = item;
      return;
    }

    // 既存の優先順位関数があるのでそれを使う。
    // 通常行 > MIDDAY行、より信頼できる行を残す想定。
    const cmp = compareOhlcvDuplicatePriority_(row, current.row);

    if (cmp < 0 || (cmp === 0 && sheetRow > current.sheetRow)) {
      bestByKey[key] = item;
    }
  });

  const deleteRows = [];
  const samples = [];

  items.forEach(function(item) {
    if (isProtectedMiddayOhlcvMarker_(item.row[1])) return;
    if (isPmLockedMarker_(item.row[1])) return;
    const best = bestByKey[item.key];
    if (!best) return;

    if (item.sheetRow !== best.sheetRow) {
      deleteRows.push(item.sheetRow);

      if (samples.length < 10) {
        samples.push({
          row: item.sheetRow,
          key: item.key,
          keptRow: best.sheetRow
        });
      }
    }
  });

  if (deleteRows.length === 0) {
    return {
      scannedRows: scanRows,
      deletedRows: 0
    };
  }

  deleteRowsByRowNums_(sheet, deleteRows);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "PHASE4軽量版: 直近OHLCVの重複行を削除しました。", {
    scannedRows: scanRows,
    deletedRows: deleteRows.length,
    samples: samples
  });

  return {
    scannedRows: scanRows,
    deletedRows: deleteRows.length
  };
}

function stopOhlcvResumeLoopNow() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeOHLCVFetch");
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeOHLCVFetchMidday");

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "OHLCV再開トリガーを削除しました。", {
    handlers: ["resumeOHLCVFetch", "resumeOHLCVFetchMidday"]
  });

  return { ok: true };
}

function forceFinishOhlcvPhase4LightNow() {
  let lock = null;

  try {
    lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
    lock.waitLock(30000);

    const startTime = Date.now();
    const app = getAppConfig_();
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(app.spreadsheetId);
    const ohlcvSheet = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);

    if (!ohlcvSheet) {
      throw new Error("Missing sheet: " + CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
    }

    const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    const todayKey = getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_();
    const refreshId = "manual_phase4_light_" + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss");

    deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeOHLCVFetch");

    const lightResult = finishOhlcvPhase4Light_(ohlcvSheet, todayKey, startTime);

    const newCount = props.getProperty(NEW_ALERT_COUNT_KEY) || "0";

    resetProcessProperties_();

    props.setProperty(DAILY_MAINT_NEW_COUNT_KEY, newCount);
    props.setProperty(DAILY_MAINT_REFRESH_ID_KEY, refreshId);

    deleteTriggersByHandler_("runDailyMaintenanceTrigger");

    ScriptApp.newTrigger("runDailyMaintenanceTrigger")
      .timeBased()
      .after(5 * 1000)
      .create();

    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV", "PHASE4軽量版でOHLCV取得フローを完了扱いにしました。日次メンテナンスを5秒後に起動します。", {
      newRowsOrSymbols: newCount,
      refreshId: refreshId,
      lightResult: lightResult
    });

    return {
      ok: true,
      newRowsOrSymbols: newCount,
      refreshId: refreshId,
      lightResult: lightResult
    };

  } finally {
    if (lock && lock.hasLock()) lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

  let lock = null;

  try {
    lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

    const startTime = Date.now();
