  }
}

// =====================================
// OHLCV掃除専用チェーン（日次メンテ後）
// =====================================

const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_HANDLER = "runOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanupTrigger";

const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_STEP_KEY = "OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_STEP";
const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY = "OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR";
const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_START_ROW_KEY = "OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_START_ROW";
const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_LAST_ROW_KEY = "OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_LAST_ROW";
const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_TODAY_KEY = "OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_TODAY_KEY";
const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_MAIN_SYMBOLS_KEY = "OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_MAIN_SYMBOLS";
const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_NEW_COUNT_KEY = "OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_NEW_COUNT";
const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_REFRESH_ID_KEY = "OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_REFRESH_ID";

const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_SCAN_ROWS = 30000;       // 毎回掃除する直近行数
const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CHUNK_ROWS = 800;        // 1回で処理する行数
const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_DUP_CHUNK_ROWS = 1200;   // 重複チェックの1回あたり行数
const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_LIMIT_MS = 90000;        // 1実行の安全上限
const OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_RESUME_AFTER_MS = 60 * 1000;

function scheduleOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanup_(newCount, refreshId) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const app = getAppConfig_();
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(app.spreadsheetId);
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);

  if (!sh) {
    throw new Error("Missing sheet: " + CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  }

  clearOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanupState_();

  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  const startRow = Math.max(2, lastRow - OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_SCAN_ROWS + 1);
  const todayKey = getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_();

  props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_STEP_KEY, "TS");
  props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY, String(lastRow));
  props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_START_ROW_KEY, String(startRow));
  props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_LAST_ROW_KEY, String(lastRow));
  props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_TODAY_KEY, todayKey);
  props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_MAIN_SYMBOLS_KEY, JSON.stringify([]));
  props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_NEW_COUNT_KEY, String(newCount || "0"));
  props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_REFRESH_ID_KEY, String(refreshId || ""));

  scheduleSingleOhlcvPostCleanupTrigger_(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_RESUME_AFTER_MS);

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV掃除", "日次メンテナンス後のOHLCV掃除チェーンを予約しました。", {
    handlerName: OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_HANDLER,
    startRow: startRow,
    lastRow: lastRow,
    scanRows: Math.max(0, lastRow - startRow + 1),
    todayKey: todayKey
  });
}

function runOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanupTrigger() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_HANDLER);
  return runOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanup_();
}

function runOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanup_() {
  let lock = null;
  const startedAt = Date.now();

  try {
    lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
    lock.waitLock(30000);

    const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    const app = getAppConfig_();
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(app.spreadsheetId);
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);

    if (!sh) {
      throw new Error("Missing sheet: " + CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
    }

    // TS ステップは廃止。既存の TS 状態をそのまま引き継がず MAIN_SCAN に読み替える
    const rawStep = props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_STEP_KEY) || "MAIN_SCAN";
    let step = rawStep === "TS" ? "MAIN_SCAN" : rawStep;
    let cursor = safeParseInt_(props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY), sh.getLastRow());
    const startRow = safeParseInt_(props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_START_ROW_KEY), Math.max(2, sh.getLastRow() - OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_SCAN_ROWS + 1));
    const todayKey = props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_TODAY_KEY) || getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_();

    let totalConverted = 0;
    let totalMiddayDeleted = 0;
    let totalDuplicateDeleted = 0;
    let totalMainSymbols = 0;

    while (Date.now() - startedAt < OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_LIMIT_MS) {
      step = props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_STEP_KEY) || step;
      // TS ステップは廃止: MAIN_SCAN に読み替え
      if (step === "TS") {
        step = "MAIN_SCAN";
        props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_STEP_KEY, "MAIN_SCAN");
      }
      cursor = safeParseInt_(props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY), cursor);

      if (cursor < startRow) {
        if (step === "TS") {
          props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_STEP_KEY, "MAIN_SCAN");
          props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY, props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_LAST_ROW_KEY) || String(sh.getLastRow()));
          writeProcessLog_("OHLCV掃除", "timestamp文字列化が完了しました。次にMIDDAY整理用の本番AM行を検出します。", {});
          continue;
        }

        if (step === "MAIN_SCAN") {
          props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_STEP_KEY, "MIDDAY_DELETE");
          props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY, props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_LAST_ROW_KEY) || String(sh.getLastRow()));
          writeProcessLog_("OHLCV掃除", "本番AM行の検出が完了しました。次に置き換え済みMIDDAY行を削除します。", {});
          continue;
        }

        if (step === "MIDDAY_DELETE") {
          completeOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanup_({
            totalConverted: totalConverted,
            totalMainSymbols: totalMainSymbols,
            totalMiddayDeleted: totalMiddayDeleted,
            totalDuplicateDeleted: 0,
            skippedDuplicateBeforeGap: true,
            elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt
          });
          return { ok: true, completed: true };
        }
        if (step === "DUP") {
          completeOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanup_({
            totalConverted: totalConverted,
            totalMainSymbols: totalMainSymbols,
            totalMiddayDeleted: totalMiddayDeleted,
            totalDuplicateDeleted: totalDuplicateDeleted,
            elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt
          });
          return { ok: true, completed: true };
        }
      }

      if (step === "TS") {
        const r = processOhlcvTimestampCleanupChunk_(sh, startRow, cursor);
        totalConverted += r.convertedRows || 0;
        props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY, String(r.nextCursor));
        continue;
      }

      if (step === "MAIN_SCAN") {
        const r = processOhlcvMainAmScanChunk_(sh, startRow, cursor, todayKey);
        totalMainSymbols = r.mainSymbolsCount || totalMainSymbols;
        props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY, String(r.nextCursor));
        continue;
      }

      if (step === "MIDDAY_DELETE") {
        const r = processOhlcvMiddayDeleteChunk_(sh, startRow, cursor, todayKey);
        totalMiddayDeleted += r.deletedRows || 0;
        props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY, String(r.nextCursor));
        continue;
      }

      if (step === "DUP") {
        props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY, String(startRow - 1));
        continue;
      }

      throw new Error("Unknown OHLCV cleanup step: " + step);
    }

    scheduleSingleOhlcvPostCleanupTrigger_(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_RESUME_AFTER_MS);

    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV掃除", "OHLCV掃除チェーンを分割継続します。", {
      step: props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_STEP_KEY),
      cursor: props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY),
      startRow: startRow,
      elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt
    });

    return {
      ok: true,
      continuation: true,
      step: props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_STEP_KEY),
      cursor: props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY)
    };

  } catch (err) {
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.post_cleanup.error", {
      message: err && err.message ? err.message : String(err),
      stack: err && err.stack ? String(err.stack) : ""
    });

    scheduleSingleOhlcvPostCleanupTrigger_(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_RESUME_AFTER_MS);

    return {
      ok: false,
      continuation: true,
      error: err && err.message ? err.message : String(err)
    };

  } finally {
    if (lock && lock.hasLock()) lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function processOhlcvTimestampCleanupChunk_(sheet, scanStartRow, cursor) {
  const endRow = Math.min(cursor, sheet.getLastRow());
  if (endRow < scanStartRow) return { nextCursor: scanStartRow - 1, convertedRows: 0 };

  const startRow = Math.max(scanStartRow, endRow - OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CHUNK_ROWS + 1);
  const numRows = endRow - startRow + 1;

  const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, 8).getValues();
  const timestampValues = values.map(row => [row[0]]);

  let convertedRows = 0;
  const samples = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    const before = values[i][0];
    if (before instanceof Date && isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(before)) continue;
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(before);
    if (!ts || !isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts)) continue;

    timestampValues[i][0] = ts;
    convertedRows++;

    if (samples.length < 5) {
      samples.push({
        row: startRow + i,
        before: String(before),
        after: Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm"),
        symbol: String(values[i][2] || "")
      });
    }
  }

  if (convertedRows > 0) {
    const timestampRange = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, 1);
    timestampRange.setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd hh:mm");
    timestampRange.setValues(timestampValues);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();

    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.post_cleanup.ts", {
      startRow: startRow,
      endRow: endRow,
      convertedRows: convertedRows,
      samples: samples
    });
  }

  return {
    nextCursor: startRow - 1,
    convertedRows: convertedRows
  };
}

function processOhlcvMainAmScanChunk_(sheet, scanStartRow, cursor, todayKey) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  const endRow = Math.min(cursor, sheet.getLastRow());
  if (endRow < scanStartRow) {
    const symbols = JSON.parse(props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_MAIN_SYMBOLS_KEY) || "[]");
    return { nextCursor: scanStartRow - 1, mainSymbolsCount: symbols.length };
  }

  const startRow = Math.max(scanStartRow, endRow - OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CHUNK_ROWS + 1);
  const numRows = endRow - startRow + 1;

  const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, 8).getValues();
  const mainSet = new Set(JSON.parse(props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_MAIN_SYMBOLS_KEY) || "[]"));

  values.forEach(function(row) {
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]);
    if (!ts) return;

    if (formatYmd_(ts) !== todayKey) return;
    if (getOhlcvSessionName_(ts) !== "AM") return;

    const marker = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    if (marker === "GAP_FAILED") return;
    if (marker.match(/^MIDDAY_/)) return;

    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (symbol) mainSet.add(symbol);
  });

  const symbols = Array.from(mainSet);
  props.setProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_MAIN_SYMBOLS_KEY, JSON.stringify(symbols));

  return {
    nextCursor: startRow - 1,
    mainSymbolsCount: symbols.length
  };
}

function processOhlcvMiddayDeleteChunk_(sheet, scanStartRow, cursor, todayKey) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const mainSymbols = new Set(JSON.parse(props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_MAIN_SYMBOLS_KEY) || "[]"));

  if (mainSymbols.size === 0) {
    return { nextCursor: scanStartRow - 1, deletedRows: 0 };
  }

  const endRow = Math.min(cursor, sheet.getLastRow());
  if (endRow < scanStartRow) return { nextCursor: scanStartRow - 1, deletedRows: 0 };

  const startRow = Math.max(scanStartRow, endRow - OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CHUNK_ROWS + 1);
  const numRows = endRow - startRow + 1;

  const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, 8).getValues();
  const deleteRows = [];
  const samples = [];

  values.forEach(function(row, index) {
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]);
    if (!ts) return;

    if (formatYmd_(ts) !== todayKey) return;
    if (getOhlcvSessionName_(ts) !== "AM") return;

    const marker = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    if (isProtectedMiddayOhlcvMarker_(marker)) return;
    if (!marker.match(/^MIDDAY_/)) return;

    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (!mainSymbols.has(symbol)) return;

    const rowNum = startRow + index;
    deleteRows.push(rowNum);

    if (samples.length < 5) {
      samples.push({
        row: rowNum,
        symbol: symbol,
        marker: marker
      });
    }
  });

  if (deleteRows.length > 0) {
    deleteRowsByRowNums_(sheet, deleteRows);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();

    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV掃除", "置き換え済みMIDDAY行を分割削除しました。", {
      startRow: startRow,
      endRow: endRow,
      deletedRows: deleteRows.length,
      samples: samples
    });
  }

  return {
    nextCursor: startRow - 1,
    deletedRows: deleteRows.length
  };
}

function completeOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanup_(summary) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  const newCount = props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_NEW_COUNT_KEY) || "0";
  const refreshId = props.getProperty(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_REFRESH_ID_KEY) || "";

  clearOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanupState_();

  deleteTriggersByHandler_("quickRepairTrigger");
  ScriptApp.newTrigger("quickRepairTrigger")
    .timeBased()
    .after(5 * 1000)
    .create();

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV掃除", "OHLCV掃除チェーンが完了しました。続いてギャップ修復を5秒後に起動します。", {
    handlerName: "quickRepairTrigger",
    newCount: newCount,
    refreshId: refreshId,
    summary: summary || {}
  });

  debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.post_cleanup.complete", {
    newCount: newCount,
    refreshId: refreshId,
    summary: summary || {}
  });
}

function scheduleSingleOhlcvPostCleanupTrigger_(delayMs) {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_HANDLER);

  ScriptApp.newTrigger(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_HANDLER)
    .timeBased()
    .after(delayMs)
    .create();
}

function clearOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanupState_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  [
    OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_STEP_KEY,
    OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_CURSOR_KEY,
    OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_START_ROW_KEY,
    OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_LAST_ROW_KEY,
    OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_TODAY_KEY,
    OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_MAIN_SYMBOLS_KEY,
    OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_NEW_COUNT_KEY,
    OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_REFRESH_ID_KEY
  ].forEach(function(key) {
    props.deleteProperty(key);
  });

  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_HANDLER);
}

function resetOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanupNow() {
  clearOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanupState_();
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV掃除", "OHLCV掃除チェーンの状態とトリガーをリセットしました。", {});
  return { ok: true };
}

function startOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanupNow() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const newCount = props.getProperty(DAILY_MAINT_NEW_COUNT_KEY) || "0";
  const refreshId = props.getProperty(DAILY_MAINT_REFRESH_ID_KEY) || "manual_post_cleanup";

  scheduleOhlcvPostMaintenanceCleanup_(newCount, refreshId);

  return {
    ok: true,
    scheduled: true,
    handlerName: OHLCV_POST_CLEANUP_HANDLER
  };
}

// =====================================
// Post quick-repair OHLCV cleanup chain
// =====================================

function scheduleOhlcvPostRepairCleanup_(quickRepairSummary) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const app = getAppConfig_();
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(app.spreadsheetId);
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  if (!sh) throw new Error("Missing sheet: " + CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);

  clearOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_();

  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  const startRow = 2;
  const businessDate = getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_();
  const state = {
    version: OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_VERSION,
    step: "LOCK_AM",  // TS ステップはスキップ（appendOhlcvRowsToSheetDetailed_ で書き込み時に正規化済みのため不要）
    businessDate: businessDate,
    scanStartRow: startRow,
    lastRow: lastRow,
    cursor: lastRow,
    targetDates: [],
    dateIndex: 0,
    quickRepairSummary: quickRepairSummary || {},
    totals: {
      convertedRows: 0,
      protectedRows: 0,
      duplicateDeletedRows: 0,
      duplicateDatesProcessed: 0,
      sortAttempted: false,
      sortSkipped: false
    },
    // 降順レンジ削除（bulkRangedDeleteOhlcvRows_）のセッション状態。
    // 関数が必要時に lazy に設定するが、起動時に明示的に null 化して旧 compact* 残骸を防ぐ。
    rangedDeleteActive: false,
    deleteRangeIndex: null,
    actuallyDeleted: null,
    expectedDeletedRows: null,
    startedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
  scheduleSingleOhlcvPostRepairCleanupTrigger_(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_RESUME_AFTER_MS);

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Scheduled post-repair cleanup without running full-sheet dedupe/sort in quickRepair.", {
    handlerName: OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_HANDLER,
    businessDate: businessDate,
    startRow: startRow,
    lastRow: lastRow,
    scanRows: Math.max(0, lastRow - startRow + 1),
    quickRepairSummary: quickRepairSummary || {}
  });

  return {
    scheduled: true,
    handlerName: OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_HANDLER,
    startRow: startRow,
    lastRow: lastRow
  };
}

function resumeOhlcvPostRepairCleanup() {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_HANDLER);
  return runOhlcvPostRepairCleanup_();
}

function runOhlcvPostRepairCleanup_() {
  let lock = null;
  const startedAt = Date.now();

  // ★ ハードタイムアウト対策: 先にリトライトリガーをセット（600秒後）
  // 正常完了時は completeOhlcvPostRepairCleanup_ 内で削除。GASに強制終了されても自動再開できる。
  scheduleSingleOhlcvPostRepairCleanupTrigger_(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_SAFETY_AFTER_MS);
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", `ポストリペアクリーンアップの再開トリガーを ${Math.round(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_SAFETY_AFTER_MS / 1000)} 秒後に設定しました。`, {
    handlerName: OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_HANDLER,
    delayMs: OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_SAFETY_AFTER_MS
  });

  try {
    lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
    lock.waitLock(30000);

    const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    const app = getAppConfig_();
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(app.spreadsheetId);
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
    if (!sh) throw new Error("Missing sheet: " + CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);

    let state = loadOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_();
    if (!state) {
      const scheduled = scheduleOhlcvPostRepairCleanup_({ manual: true, reason: "missing_state" });
      return { ok: true, scheduled: true, postRepairCleanup: scheduled };
    }

    while (Date.now() - startedAt < OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_LIMIT_MS) {
      const isScanStep = state.step === "TS" || state.step === "LOCK_AM" || state.step === "COLLECT_DATES";
      if (isScanStep) state.cursor = Math.min(Number(state.cursor || sh.getLastRow()), sh.getLastRow());
      if (isScanStep && state.cursor < Number(state.scanStartRow || 2)) {
        state = advanceOhlcvPostRepairCleanupStep_(state, sh);
        saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
        if (state.step === "DONE") {
          return completeOhlcvPostRepairCleanup_(state);
        }
        continue;
      }

      if (state.step === "TS") {
        // TS ステップはスキップ:
        // - appendOhlcvRowsToSheetDetailed_ が書き込み時に正規化済み
        // - normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_ が HH:mm / HH:mm:ss / Date / シリアル値をすべて Date に変換
        // - sheet.sort(1, true) は Date オブジェクトを正しく昇順ソートする
        // - 全行スキャン（238,000行超）で 40分以上消費するため除去
        state = advanceOhlcvPostRepairCleanupStep_(state, sh);
        state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
        writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "TS ステップをスキップして次ステップへ進みます。", { nextStep: state.step });
        if (state.step === "DONE") {
          return completeOhlcvPostRepairCleanup_(state);
        }
        continue;
      }

      if (state.step === "LOCK_AM") {
        const rawSh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.RAW_SHEET);
        const r = processOhlcvPostRepairProtectMiddayAmChunk_(sh, rawSh, state.scanStartRow, state.cursor, state.businessDate, startedAt + OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_LIMIT_MS);
        state.cursor = r.nextCursor;
        state.totals.protectedRows += r.protectedRows || 0;
        state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
        continue;
      }

      if (state.step === "COLLECT_DATES") {
        const r = processOhlcvPostRepairCollectDatesChunk_(sh, state);
        state.cursor = r.nextCursor;
        state.targetDates = r.targetDates;
        state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
        continue;
      }

      if (state.step === "DEDUP_DATES") {
        // 旧 state との互換性: dateIndex が未設定/NaN の場合は 0 にフォールバック
        if (!Number.isFinite(Number(state.dateIndex))) state.dateIndex = 0;
        else state.dateIndex = Number(state.dateIndex);
        if (state.dateIndex >= state.targetDates.length) {
          state.step = "EARLY_DEDUP";
          state.earlyDedupComplete = false;
          state.cursor = Number(state.scanStartRow || 2) - 1;
          saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
          continue;
        }

        // DELETE phase: 降順レンジ削除（setValues を使わない安全な実装）
        if (hasPendingOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_() || state.rangedDeleteActive) {
          const deadlineMs = startedAt + OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_LIMIT_MS;
          writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Entering ranged delete (DEDUP_DATES batch).", {
            dateIndex: state.dateIndex,
            batchSize: OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_DATE_BATCH,
            deleteRangeIndex: state.deleteRangeIndex || null,
            actuallyDeleted: state.actuallyDeleted || 0
          });
          const r = bulkRangedDeleteOhlcvRows_(sh, state, deadlineMs);
          if (!r.done) {
            state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
            saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
            break;
          }
          state.totals.duplicateDeletedRows += r.deletedRows || 0;
          // 1バッチ分の delete 完了 → dateIndex を進める
          const advanced = Math.min(state.targetDates.length, state.dateIndex + OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_DATE_BATCH);
          state.totals.duplicateDatesProcessed = (state.totals.duplicateDatesProcessed || 0) + (advanced - state.dateIndex);
          state.dateIndex = advanced;
          state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
          saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
          continue;
        }

        // SCAN phase: 1回だけシートを読んで全削除対象行をPropertiesServiceにキャッシュ
        if (OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_LIMIT_MS - (Date.now() - startedAt) < OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_MIN_DEDUP_REMAINING_MS) break;

        // 1ショットあたりの作業量を予測可能にするため targetDates をバッチ分割
        const batchEnd = Math.min(state.targetDates.length, state.dateIndex + OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_DATE_BATCH);
        const batchDates = state.targetDates.slice(state.dateIndex, batchEnd);
        const dedupDeadlineMs = startedAt + OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_LIMIT_MS;
        writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Multi-date dedup scan starting.", {
          totalDates: state.targetDates.length,
          dateIndex: state.dateIndex,
          batchEnd: batchEnd,
          batchSize: batchDates.length,
          lastRow: sh.getLastRow(),
          readRowsLimit: OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_READ_ROWS,
          remainingMs: dedupDeadlineMs - Date.now(),
          sampleDates: batchDates.slice(0, 5)
        });
        const r = dedupeOhlcvAllTargetDates_(sh, batchDates, dedupDeadlineMs);
        if (r.timedOut) {
          state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
          saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
          break;
        }
        if ((r.duplicateRows || 0) > 0) {
          saveOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_(r.allDeleteRows);
          // 削除は次の iteration の DELETE phase で実施。ここでは dateIndex を進めない
        } else {
          // このバッチに重複なし → そのまま次のバッチへ
          const advanced2 = batchEnd;
          state.totals.duplicateDatesProcessed = (state.totals.duplicateDatesProcessed || 0) + (advanced2 - state.dateIndex);
          state.dateIndex = advanced2;
        }
        state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
        continue;
      }

      if (state.step === "EARLY_DEDUP") {
        // DELETE phase: 降順レンジ削除（setValues を使わない安全な実装）
        if (hasPendingOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_() || state.rangedDeleteActive) {
          const deadlineMs = startedAt + OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_LIMIT_MS;
          writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Entering ranged delete (EARLY_DEDUP).", {
            deleteRangeIndex: state.deleteRangeIndex || null,
            actuallyDeleted: state.actuallyDeleted || 0
          });
          const r = bulkRangedDeleteOhlcvRows_(sh, state, deadlineMs);
          if (!r.done) {
            state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
            saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
            break;
          }
          state.totals.duplicateDeletedRows += r.deletedRows || 0;
          state.earlyDedupComplete = true;
          state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
          saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
          continue;
        }

        // スキャン済みで削除対象なし（または削除完了）の場合はSORTへ
        if (state.earlyDedupComplete) {
          state.step = "SORT";
          state.cursor = Number(state.scanStartRow || 2) - 1;
          saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
          continue;
        }

        // SCAN phase: tail key set をメモリ内に構築し early ゾーンをスキャン。
        // PS クォータ問題を回避するため tail key set は PS に保存せず毎回再構築する。
        // 削除行は dedupeOhlcvEarlyRows_ 内で直接 PS に保存するため、戻り値 allDeleteRows は空。
        // hasPendingOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_() が true なら次の while iteration で DELETE phase に入る。
        // EARLY_DEDUP専用の長いタイムリミット（tail再構築2〜4分 + earlyスキャン）を使用する。
        if (OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_EARLY_DEDUP_LIMIT_MS - (Date.now() - startedAt) < OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_MIN_DEDUP_REMAINING_MS) break;

        const earlyDeadlineMs = startedAt + OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_EARLY_DEDUP_LIMIT_MS;
        writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Early-dedup scan starting.", {
          targetDates: state.targetDates.length,
          lastRow: sh.getLastRow(),
          remainingMs: earlyDeadlineMs - Date.now(),
          earlyCursor: state.earlyDedupEarlyCursor || null
        });
        const er = dedupeOhlcvEarlyRows_(sh, state.targetDates, state, earlyDeadlineMs);
        if (er.timedOut) {
          // state は dedupeOhlcvEarlyRows_ 内で既に saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_ 済み
          break;
        }
        // 削除行は dedupeOhlcvEarlyRows_ が直接 saveOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_ 済み。
        // hasPendingOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_() が false なら重複なしで完了。
        if (!hasPendingOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_()) {
          state.earlyDedupComplete = true;
        }
        state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
        continue;
      }

      if (state.step === "SORT") {
        const r = tryOhlcvPostRepairSortOnce_(sh);
        state.totals.sortAttempted = !!r.attempted;
        state.totals.sortSkipped = !!r.skipped;
        state.totals.sortResult = r;
        state.step = "DONE";
        state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
        return completeOhlcvPostRepairCleanup_(state);
      }

      throw new Error("Unknown post-repair cleanup step: " + state.step);
    }

    scheduleSingleOhlcvPostRepairCleanupTrigger_(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Post-repair cleanup paused and will resume.", {
      step: state.step,
      cursor: state.cursor,
      scanStartRow: state.scanStartRow,
      dateIndex: state.dateIndex,
      targetDates: state.targetDates ? state.targetDates.length : 0,
      totals: state.totals
    });
    return { ok: true, continuation: true, step: state.step, cursor: state.cursor };

  } catch (err) {
    debugLogToSheet_("ohlcv.post_repair_cleanup.error", {
      message: err && err.message ? err.message : String(err),
      stack: err && err.stack ? String(err.stack) : ""
    });
    scheduleSingleOhlcvPostRepairCleanupTrigger_(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_RESUME_AFTER_MS);
    return { ok: false, continuation: true, error: err && err.message ? err.message : String(err) };
  } finally {
    if (lock && lock.hasLock()) lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function advanceOhlcvPostRepairCleanupStep_(state, sheet) {
  if (state.step === "TS") {
    state.step = "LOCK_AM";
    state.cursor = Math.min(Number(state.lastRow || sheet.getLastRow()), sheet.getLastRow());
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Timestamp normalization finished. Protecting raw-volume AM rows next.", state.totals);
    return state;
  }
  if (state.step === "LOCK_AM") {
    state.step = "COLLECT_DATES";
    state.cursor = Math.min(Number(state.lastRow || sheet.getLastRow()), sheet.getLastRow());
    state.targetDates = [];
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "AM protection finished. Collecting duplicate target dates next.", state.totals);
    return state;
  }
  if (state.step === "COLLECT_DATES") {
    state.step = "DEDUP_DATES";
    state.cursor = Number(state.scanStartRow || 2) - 1;
    state.targetDates = normalizePostRepairTargetDates_(state.targetDates, state.businessDate);
    state.dateIndex = 0;
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Duplicate target date collection finished.", {
      targetDates: state.targetDates.length,
      samples: state.targetDates.slice(0, 20)
    });
    return state;
  }
  if (state.step === "DEDUP_DATES") {
    state.step = "SORT";
    state.cursor = Number(state.scanStartRow || 2) - 1;
    return state;
  }
  if (state.step === "SORT") {
    state.step = "DONE";
    return state;
  }
  return state;
}

function processOhlcvPostRepairTimestampChunk_(sheet, scanStartRow, cursor) {
  const endRow = Math.min(cursor, sheet.getLastRow());
  if (endRow < scanStartRow) return { nextCursor: scanStartRow - 1, convertedRows: 0 };

  const startRow = Math.max(scanStartRow, endRow - OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_CHUNK_ROWS + 1);
  const numRows = endRow - startRow + 1;
  const values = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, 3).getValues();
  const timestampValues = values.map(row => [row[0]]);
  let convertedRows = 0;
  const samples = [];

  values.forEach((row, index) => {
    const before = row[0];
    if (before instanceof Date && isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(before)) return;
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(before);
    if (!ts || !isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts)) return;
    timestampValues[index][0] = ts;
    convertedRows++;
    if (samples.length < 5) {
      samples.push({ row: startRow + index, before: String(before), after: Utilities.formatDate(ts, CONFIG.TIMEZONE, "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm"), symbol: String(row[2] || "") });
    }
  });

  if (convertedRows > 0) {
    sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, 1).setNumberFormat("yyyy/mm/dd hh:mm").setValues(timestampValues);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Timestamp を日時形式に変換しました。", {
      startRow: startRow,
      endRow: endRow,
      convertedRows: convertedRows,
      samples: samples
    });
  }

  return { nextCursor: startRow - 1, convertedRows: convertedRows };
}

function processOhlcvPostRepairProtectMiddayAmChunk_(sheet, rawSheet, scanStartRow, cursor, businessDate, deadlineMs) {
  return processOhlcvPostRepairProtectMiddayAmChunkOptimized_(sheet, rawSheet, scanStartRow, cursor, businessDate, deadlineMs);
  // 一括読み込み方式:
  // 当日 MIDDAY_ 行はシート末尾付近にしか存在しない（当日データ = ソート済みシートの最末尾）。
  // 旧来の 1,200 行チャンク × 全行スキャンから、末尾 OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_READ_ROWS 行の
  // 単発読み込みに変更することで、238,000 行を 53 秒かけてスキャンする処理を数秒に圧縮する。
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  const safeScanStart = Math.max(2, Number(scanStartRow || 2));
  if (lastRow < safeScanStart) return { nextCursor: safeScanStart - 1, protectedRows: 0 };

  const dateKey = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(businessDate);
  const middayMarker = makeMiddayRefreshId_(dateKey);
  const protectedMarker = makeProtectedMiddayRefreshId_(dateKey);

  const readStart = Math.max(safeScanStart, lastRow - OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_READ_ROWS + 1);
  const numRows = lastRow - readStart + 1;
  const values = sheet.getRange(readStart, 1, numRows, 8).getValues();
  const markerValues = values.map(row => [row[1]]);
  let protectedRows = 0;
  const samples = [];

  values.forEach((row, index) => {
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]);
    if (!ts || formatYmd_(ts) !== dateKey || getOhlcvSessionName_(ts) !== "AM") return;
    const marker = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    if (marker !== middayMarker) return;
    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (!symbol) return;
    const rawVolume = rawAlertVolumeMap ? rawAlertVolumeMap[makeRawAlertVolumeKey_(ts, symbol)] : undefined;
    if (rawVolume === undefined || !Number.isFinite(Number(rawVolume))) return;
    if (Math.round(toSafeVolumeNumber_(row[7])) !== Math.round(Number(rawVolume))) return;
    markerValues[index][0] = protectedMarker;
    protectedRows++;
    if (samples.length < 5) samples.push({ row: readStart + index, symbol: symbol, volume: row[7] });
  });

  if (protectedRows > 0) {
    sheet.getRange(readStart, 2, numRows, 1).setValues(markerValues);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Protected raw-volume AM rows from later dedupe/delete.", {
      businessDate: dateKey,
      readStart: readStart,
      lastRow: lastRow,
      protectedRows: protectedRows,
      samples: samples
    });
  }

  // nextCursor = safeScanStart - 1 を返すことで、呼び出し元の状態機械が即座に次のステップへ進む
  return { nextCursor: safeScanStart - 1, protectedRows: protectedRows };
}

function processOhlcvPostRepairProtectMiddayAmChunkOptimized_(sheet, rawSheet, scanStartRow, cursor, businessDate, deadlineMs) {
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  const safeScanStart = Math.max(2, Number(scanStartRow || 2));
  if (lastRow < safeScanStart) return { nextCursor: safeScanStart - 1, protectedRows: 0 };

  const dateKey = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(businessDate);
  const middayMarker = makeMiddayRefreshId_(dateKey);
  const protectedMarker = makeProtectedMiddayRefreshId_(dateKey);
  const readStart = Math.max(safeScanStart, lastRow - OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_READ_ROWS + 1);
  const numRows = lastRow - readStart + 1;

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "LOCK_AM tail scan starting.", {
    businessDate: dateKey,
    readStart: readStart,
    lastRow: lastRow,
    numRows: numRows
  });

  const keyRows = sheet.getRange(readStart, 1, numRows, 3).getValues();
  const volumeRows = sheet.getRange(readStart, 8, numRows, 1).getValues();
  const markerValues = keyRows.map(row => [row[1]]);
  const candidates = [];
  const expectedKeys = [];

  keyRows.forEach((row, index) => {
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]);
    if (!ts || formatYmd_(ts) !== dateKey || getOhlcvSessionName_(ts) !== "AM") return;
    const marker = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    if (marker !== middayMarker) return;
    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (!symbol) return;
    const volume = toSafeVolumeNumber_(volumeRows[index][0]);
    if (!Number.isFinite(volume)) return;
    const key = makeRawAlertVolumeKey_(ts, symbol);
    candidates.push({ index: index, row: readStart + index, key: key, symbol: symbol, volume: volume });
    expectedKeys.push(key);
  });

  if (candidates.length === 0) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "LOCK_AM tail scan finished with no MIDDAY AM candidates.", {
      businessDate: dateKey,
      readStart: readStart,
      lastRow: lastRow,
      numRows: numRows
    });
    return { nextCursor: safeScanStart - 1, protectedRows: 0 };
  }

  const rawAlertVolumeMapData = buildRawAlertVolumeMapForBusinessDate_(rawSheet, dateKey, {
    expectedKeys: Array.from(new Set(expectedKeys)),
    maxScanRows: OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_RAW_VOLUME_SCAN_ROWS,
    chunkRows: OHLCV_RAW_ALERT_VOLUME_MAP_CHUNK_ROWS,
    stopAtOlderDateBoundary: true,
    deadlineMs: deadlineMs
  });
  const rawAlertVolumeMap = rawAlertVolumeMapData.volumeMap;
  let protectedRows = 0;
  const samples = [];

  candidates.forEach(candidate => {
    const rawVolume = rawAlertVolumeMap[candidate.key];
    if (rawVolume === undefined || !Number.isFinite(Number(rawVolume))) return;
    if (Math.round(candidate.volume) !== Math.round(Number(rawVolume))) return;
    markerValues[candidate.index][0] = protectedMarker;
    protectedRows++;
    if (samples.length < 5) samples.push({ row: candidate.row, symbol: candidate.symbol, volume: candidate.volume });
  });

  if (protectedRows > 0) {
    sheet.getRange(readStart, 2, numRows, 1).setValues(markerValues);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  }

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "LOCK_AM tail scan finished.", {
    businessDate: dateKey,
    readStart: readStart,
    lastRow: lastRow,
    candidates: candidates.length,
    protectedRows: protectedRows,
    rawMapStats: rawAlertVolumeMapData.stats,
    samples: samples
  });

  return { nextCursor: safeScanStart - 1, protectedRows: protectedRows };
}

function processOhlcvPostRepairCollectDatesChunk_(sheet, state) {
  // 一括読み込み方式:
  // GAP_REPAIR / MIDDAY_ マーカーは quickRepair の修復範囲（直近 daysBack 日）にしか存在しない。
  // quickRepair は末尾 QUICK_REPAIR_SCAN_MAX_ROWS(=90,000) 行から対象を探して末尾に追記するため、
  // DEDUP と同じスキャン範囲（末尾 OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_READ_ROWS 行）を単発一括読み込みすれば十分。
  // 旧来の 1,200 行チャンク × 全行スキャン（238,000 行・130 分超）から単発 API 1 回（数秒）に圧縮する。
  const scanStartRow = Number(state.scanStartRow || 2);
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < scanStartRow) {
    return { nextCursor: scanStartRow - 1, targetDates: normalizePostRepairTargetDates_(state.targetDates, state.businessDate) };
  }

  const readStart = Math.max(scanStartRow, lastRow - OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_READ_ROWS + 1);
  const numRows = lastRow - readStart + 1;
  const values = sheet.getRange(readStart, 1, numRows, 3).getValues();

  const dateSet = {};
  (state.targetDates || []).forEach(dateKey => { dateSet[dateKey] = true; });

  values.forEach(row => {
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]);
    if (!ts) return;
    const marker = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    if (!isPostRepairDuplicateTargetMarker_(marker)) return;
    dateSet[formatYmd_(ts)] = true;
  });

  // nextCursor = scanStartRow - 1 を返すことで、呼び出し元の状態機械が即座に次のステップへ進む
  return {
    nextCursor: scanStartRow - 1,
    targetDates: normalizePostRepairTargetDates_(Object.keys(dateSet), state.businessDate)
  };
}

function isPostRepairDuplicateTargetMarker_(marker) {
  const value = String(marker || "").trim();
  if (!value) return false;
  if (value === "GAP_REPAIR" || value === "GAP_FAILED") return true;
  if (value.match(/^MIDDAY_/)) return true;
  if (value.match(/^(REPAIR_|AM_REPAIR_|TEMP_|TEST_)/)) return true;
  return false;
}

function normalizePostRepairTargetDates_(dates, businessDate) {
  const set = {};
  (dates || []).forEach(dateKey => {
    try {
      set[normalizeBusinessDateKey_(dateKey)] = true;
    } catch (e) {}
  });
  try {
    const today = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(businessDate || getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_());
    set[today] = true;
  } catch (e) {}
  return Object.keys(set).sort().reverse().slice(0, OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_MAX_DUP_DATES);
}

function dedupeOhlcvDateBucket_(sheet, dateKey, maxDeleteRows) {
  const normalizedDate = normalizeBusinessDateKey_(dateKey);
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  const bestByKey = {};
  const items = [];
  let scannedRows = 0;

  for (let row = 2; row <= lastRow; row += OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DUP_DATE_CHUNK_ROWS) {
    const numRows = Math.min(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DUP_DATE_CHUNK_ROWS, lastRow - row + 1);
    const values = sheet.getRange(row, 1, numRows, 8).getValues();
    scannedRows += numRows;

    values.forEach((valueRow, index) => {
      const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(valueRow[0]);
      if (!ts || formatYmd_(ts) !== normalizedDate) return;
      const symbol = String(valueRow[2] || "").trim();
      if (!symbol) return;
      const sheetRow = row + index;
      const key = formatOhlcvTimestampTextForSheet_(ts) + "|" + symbol;
      const item = { key: key, row: valueRow, sheetRow: sheetRow };
      items.push(item);

      const current = bestByKey[key];
      if (!current) {
        bestByKey[key] = item;
        return;
      }

      const cmp = compareOhlcvDuplicatePriority_(valueRow, current.row);
      if (cmp < 0 || (cmp === 0 && sheetRow > current.sheetRow)) {
        bestByKey[key] = item;
      }
    });
  }

  const deleteRows = [];
  const samples = [];
  items.forEach(item => {
    if (isProtectedMiddayOhlcvMarker_(item.row[1])) return;
    if (isPmLockedMarker_(item.row[1])) return;
    const best = bestByKey[item.key];
    if (!best || item.sheetRow === best.sheetRow) return;
    deleteRows.push(item.sheetRow);
    if (samples.length < 10) samples.push({ row: item.sheetRow, key: item.key, keptRow: best.sheetRow });
  });

  const maxRows = Math.max(1, Number(maxDeleteRows) || OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_MAX_DELETE_ROWS);
  const limitedDeleteRows = deleteRows.slice(0, maxRows);
  if (limitedDeleteRows.length > 0) {
    deleteRowsByRowNums_(sheet, limitedDeleteRows);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  }

  const remaining = Math.max(0, deleteRows.length - limitedDeleteRows.length);
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Date-bucket duplicate cleanup finished one pass.", {
    dateKey: normalizedDate,
    scannedRows: scannedRows,
    duplicateRows: deleteRows.length,
    deletedRows: limitedDeleteRows.length,
    remainingDuplicateRows: remaining,
    samples: samples
  });

  return {
    dateKey: normalizedDate,
    scannedRows: scannedRows,
    duplicateRows: deleteRows.length,
    deletedRows: limitedDeleteRows.length,
    remainingDuplicateRows: remaining
  };
}

// 削除対象行番号をPropertiesServiceに降順チャンク保存
// 降順で保存することで「先頭から削除」→「後続の行番号が不変」を保証
function saveOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_(rowNums) {
  if (!rowNums || !rowNums.length) return;
  const sorted = rowNums.slice().sort((a, b) => b - a);
  const propsToSet = {};
  let numChunks = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length; i += OHLCV_DEDUP_CHUNK_ITEMS) {
    propsToSet[OHLCV_DEDUP_ROWS_KEY_PREFIX + numChunks] = sorted.slice(i, i + OHLCV_DEDUP_CHUNK_ITEMS).join(",");
    numChunks++;
  }
  propsToSet[OHLCV_DEDUP_ROWS_META_KEY] = JSON.stringify({ chunks: numChunks, startChunk: 0, total: sorted.length });
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperties(propsToSet);
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Dedup delete row list cached.", { total: sorted.length, chunks: numChunks });
}

function hasPendingOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_() {
  const metaStr = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(OHLCV_DEDUP_ROWS_META_KEY);
  if (!metaStr) return false;
  try { return (JSON.parse(metaStr).total || 0) > 0; } catch (e) { return false; }
}

// 先頭チャンクから batchSize 件取り出して返す。取り出した分はストレージから削除
function loadAndDequeueOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_(batchSize) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const metaStr = props.getProperty(OHLCV_DEDUP_ROWS_META_KEY);
  if (!metaStr) return null;
  let meta;
  try { meta = JSON.parse(metaStr); } catch (e) { return null; }
  if (!meta || !meta.chunks || meta.chunks === 0 || (meta.total || 0) === 0) {
    clearOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_();
    return { rowsToDelete: [], remainingTotal: 0 };
  }
  const chunkKey = OHLCV_DEDUP_ROWS_KEY_PREFIX + (meta.startChunk || 0);
  const chunk = (props.getProperty(chunkKey) || "").split(",").map(Number).filter(n => !isNaN(n) && n > 0);
  const safe = Math.max(1, Number(batchSize) || OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_MAX_DELETE_ROWS);
  const toDelete = chunk.slice(0, safe);
  const remaining = chunk.slice(safe);
  meta.total = Math.max(0, (meta.total || 0) - toDelete.length);
  const propsToSet = {};
  if (remaining.length > 0) {
    propsToSet[chunkKey] = remaining.join(",");
  } else {
    props.deleteProperty(chunkKey);
    meta.startChunk = (meta.startChunk || 0) + 1;
    meta.chunks = Math.max(0, (meta.chunks || 1) - 1);
  }
  propsToSet[OHLCV_DEDUP_ROWS_META_KEY] = JSON.stringify(meta);
  props.setProperties(propsToSet);
  return { rowsToDelete: toDelete, remainingTotal: meta.total };
}

function clearOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const metaStr = props.getProperty(OHLCV_DEDUP_ROWS_META_KEY);
  try {
    if (metaStr) {
      const meta = JSON.parse(metaStr);
      const start = meta.startChunk || 0;
      const count = Math.max(meta.chunks || 0, 20);
      for (let i = start; i < start + count; i++) props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_DEDUP_ROWS_KEY_PREFIX + i);
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_DEDUP_ROWS_META_KEY);
}

// ── EARLY_DEDUP tail key set ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
// EARLY_DEDUPのtail rebuild結果を PropertiesService にチャンク保存する。
// tail read（40k行×3列）がGASスロットリングで350秒超ブロックするため、10k行ずつ読んで
// 途中でタイムアウトしても次回再開時に続きから継続できるよう設計している。

// tail から収集したキー（"timestamp|symbol" 形式）をPSに追記保存する
function appendEarlyDedupTailKeys_(keys) {
  if (!keys || keys.length === 0) return;
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const metaKey = OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_META_KEY;
  let meta = { chunks: 0 };
  try { meta = JSON.parse(props.getProperty(metaKey) || "{}"); } catch (e) {}
  if (!Number.isFinite(meta.chunks)) meta.chunks = 0;

  const CHUNK = OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_KEY_CHUNK;
  const propsToSet = {};
  let i = 0;
  while (i < keys.length) {
    propsToSet[OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_KEY_PREFIX + meta.chunks] = keys.slice(i, i + CHUNK).join(",");
    meta.chunks++;
    i += CHUNK;
  }
  propsToSet[metaKey] = JSON.stringify(meta);
  props.setProperties(propsToSet);
}

// PSに保存した tail key set を Set として読み込む
function loadEarlyDedupTailKeySet_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  let meta = { chunks: 0 };
  try { meta = JSON.parse(props.getProperty(OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_META_KEY) || "{}"); } catch (e) {}
  const keySet = new Set();
  for (let i = 0; i < (meta.chunks || 0); i++) {
    const raw = props.getProperty(OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_KEY_PREFIX + i) || "";
    raw.split(",").forEach(k => { if (k) keySet.add(k); });
  }
  return keySet;
}

// tail key set をPSから全削除する
function clearEarlyDedupTailKeys_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  let meta = { chunks: 0 };
  try { meta = JSON.parse(props.getProperty(OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_META_KEY) || "{}"); } catch (e) {}
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(meta.chunks || 0, 30); i++) {
    props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_KEY_PREFIX + i);
  }
  props.deleteProperty(OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_META_KEY);
}

// EARLY_DEDUP tail key set を CacheService にチャンク保存する（resume 間で再利用するため）。
// 戻り値: 保存できれば true。CacheService が利用不能・例外時は false。
function saveEarlyDedupTailToCache_(tailKeySet, lastRow, readFromRow) {
  if (!tailKeySet || tailKeySet.size === 0) return false;
  let cache;
  try { cache = CacheService.getScriptCache(); } catch (e) { return false; }
  if (!cache) return false;
  const keys = Array.from(tailKeySet);
  const perChunk = OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_CACHE_KEYS_PER_CHUNK;
  const chunks = Math.ceil(keys.length / perChunk);
  const toSet = {};
  for (let i = 0; i < chunks; i++) {
    toSet[OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_CACHE_PREFIX + i] = keys.slice(i * perChunk, (i + 1) * perChunk).join("\n");
  }
  toSet[OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_CACHE_META_KEY] = JSON.stringify({
    chunks: chunks, keysCount: keys.length, lastRow: lastRow, readFromRow: readFromRow, ts: Date.now()
  });
  try {
    cache.putAll(toSet, OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_CACHE_TTL_SEC);
    return true;
  } catch (e) {
    return false;
  }
}

// CacheService から tail key set を Set として読み込む。
// expectedLastRow / expectedReadFromRow と一致しなければ無効（シート末尾が変わった）として null を返す。
function loadEarlyDedupTailFromCache_(expectedLastRow, expectedReadFromRow) {
  let cache;
  try { cache = CacheService.getScriptCache(); } catch (e) { return null; }
  if (!cache) return null;
  let metaStr;
  try { metaStr = cache.get(OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_CACHE_META_KEY); } catch (e) { return null; }
  if (!metaStr) return null;
  let meta;
  try { meta = JSON.parse(metaStr); } catch (e) { return null; }
  if (!meta || !Number.isFinite(meta.chunks) || meta.chunks <= 0) return null;
  if (Number(meta.lastRow) !== Number(expectedLastRow)) return null;
  if (Number(meta.readFromRow) !== Number(expectedReadFromRow)) return null;
  const chunkKeys = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < meta.chunks; i++) chunkKeys.push(OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_CACHE_PREFIX + i);
  let got;
  try { got = cache.getAll(chunkKeys); } catch (e) { return null; }
  if (!got || Object.keys(got).length !== meta.chunks) return null;
  const set = new Set();
  for (let i = 0; i < meta.chunks; i++) {
    const v = got[OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_CACHE_PREFIX + i];
    if (typeof v !== "string") return null;
    const arr = v.split("\n");
    for (let j = 0; j < arr.length; j++) if (arr[j]) set.add(arr[j]);
  }
  return set;
}

// CacheService に保存した tail key set をすべて削除する
function clearEarlyDedupTailCache_() {
  let cache;
  try { cache = CacheService.getScriptCache(); } catch (e) { return; }
  if (!cache) return;
  let metaStr;
  try { metaStr = cache.get(OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_CACHE_META_KEY); } catch (e) { return; }
  const toRemove = [OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_CACHE_META_KEY];
  if (metaStr) {
    let meta;
    try { meta = JSON.parse(metaStr); } catch (e) {}
    const chunks = (meta && Number.isFinite(meta.chunks)) ? meta.chunks : 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < chunks; i++) toRemove.push(OHLCV_EARLY_DEDUP_TAIL_CACHE_PREFIX + i);
  }
  try { cache.removeAll(toRemove); } catch (e) {}
}

// PSに保存済みの削除対象行番号を全チャンクから Set として読み込む（デキューしない）
function loadAllOhlcvDedupDeleteRowSet_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const metaStr = props.getProperty(OHLCV_DEDUP_ROWS_META_KEY);
  if (!metaStr) return { set: new Set(), minRow: 0, maxRow: 0 };
  let meta;
  try { meta = JSON.parse(metaStr); } catch (e) { return { set: new Set(), minRow: 0, maxRow: 0 }; }
  const set = new Set();
  let minRow = Infinity, maxRow = -Infinity;
  const startChunk = meta.startChunk || 0;
  const numChunks = meta.chunks || 0;
  for (let i = startChunk; i < startChunk + numChunks; i++) {
    const raw = props.getProperty(OHLCV_DEDUP_ROWS_KEY_PREFIX + i) || "";
    raw.split(",").forEach(s => {
      const v = Number(s);
      if (v > 0) { set.add(v); if (v < minRow) minRow = v; if (v > maxRow) maxRow = v; }
    });
  }
  return { set, minRow: isFinite(minRow) ? minRow : 0, maxRow: isFinite(maxRow) ? maxRow : 0 };
}

// 【DEPRECATED 2026/05/15】bulkRangedDeleteOhlcvRows_ に置き換え済み。本関数は呼び出されない。
// 旧コンパクト書き戻し方式 (setValues + 末尾deleteRows) で削除対象行を除去する。
// インシデント記録: 2026/05/15、本関数の末尾 sheet.deleteRows() が事実上 no-op になり、
// setValues でシフトアップしたデータと未削除の末尾データが二重に残ってシート破損を引き起こした。
// 後継: bulkRangedDeleteOhlcvRows_（setValues を使わず降順 deleteRows のみ + 直後 getLastRow 検証）。
function bulkCompactDeleteOhlcvRows_(sheet, state, deadlineMs) {
  // 初回: 削除セット読み込み＆コンパクト範囲初期化
  if (!state.compactReadRow) {
    const { set, minRow } = loadAllOhlcvDedupDeleteRowSet_();
    if (set.size === 0) { clearOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_(); return { done: true, deletedRows: 0 }; }
    state.compactReadRow = minRow;
    state.compactWriteRow = minRow;
    state.compactRangeEnd = sheet.getLastRow();
    saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Compact delete initialized.", {
      minRow: minRow, rangeEnd: state.compactRangeEnd, deleteSetSize: set.size
    });
  }

  const { set: deleteSet } = loadAllOhlcvDedupDeleteRowSet_();
  const CHUNK = OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_COMPACT_CHUNK_ROWS;
  let totalDeleted = 0;

  while (state.compactReadRow <= state.compactRangeEnd) {
    // OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_COMPACT_MARGIN_MS 余裕を持って中断
    if (deadlineMs && Date.now() > deadlineMs - OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_COMPACT_MARGIN_MS) break;

    const count = Math.min(CHUNK, state.compactRangeEnd - state.compactReadRow + 1);
    const values = sheet.getRange(state.compactReadRow, 1, count, 8).getValues();
    const keepRows = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      if (!deleteSet.has(state.compactReadRow + i)) keepRows.push(values[i]);
      else totalDeleted++;
    }
    if (keepRows.length > 0) {
      sheet.getRange(state.compactWriteRow, 1, keepRows.length, 8).setValues(keepRows);
    }
    // 【安全順序】flush を先に行い、シートへの書き込みをコミットしてから状態を保存する。
    // 逆順（saveState → flush）では、GASハードkill時に状態だけ進んでシート書き込みが
    // 未コミットのまま再開されると、削除対象行がコンパクト済み領域に漏れる恐れがある。
    state.compactWriteRow += keepRows.length;
    state.compactReadRow += count;
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
  }

  // コンパクト未完了 → 次回へ
  if (state.compactReadRow <= state.compactRangeEnd) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Compact delete paused.", {
      compactReadRow: state.compactReadRow, compactWriteRow: state.compactWriteRow, compactRangeEnd: state.compactRangeEnd
    });
    return { done: false };
  }

  // 完了: 末尾を1回の deleteRows で削除
  // 【安全】state.compactRangeEnd はコンパクト開始時のスナップショット値なので、
  // GASハードkill後の再開でシートが既に縮小されている場合に deleteRows が範囲外で
  // 例外を投げてエラーループになる。sheet.getLastRow() で実際の末尾を参照する。
  const tailStart = state.compactWriteRow;
  const currentLastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (tailStart <= currentLastRow) {
    sheet.deleteRows(tailStart, currentLastRow - tailStart + 1);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  }
  clearOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_();
  state.compactReadRow = null;
  state.compactWriteRow = null;
  state.compactRangeEnd = null;
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Compact delete completed.", { deletedRows: totalDeleted });
  return { done: true, deletedRows: totalDeleted };
}

// 降順ソート済み行番号配列を「連続レンジ」に圧縮する。
// 入力: [200160, 197017, 197016, 197015]（降順）
// 出力: [{start:200160,len:1}, {start:197015,len:3}]（各レンジは降順、start は最小行）
function compressConsecutiveDescending_(sortedDesc) {
  const ranges = [];
  if (!sortedDesc || sortedDesc.length === 0) return ranges;
  let curEnd = sortedDesc[0];     // レンジ末尾（最大値）
  let curStart = sortedDesc[0];   // レンジ先頭（最小値、=start）
  for (let i = 1; i < sortedDesc.length; i++) {
    const r = sortedDesc[i];
    if (r === curStart - 1) {
      curStart = r;
    } else {
      ranges.push({ start: curStart, len: curEnd - curStart + 1 });
      curEnd = r;
      curStart = r;
    }
  }
  ranges.push({ start: curStart, len: curEnd - curStart + 1 });
  return ranges;
}

// PSの削除行集合を「降順レンジ削除」で除去する。bulkCompactDeleteOhlcvRows_ の後継。
//
// 設計の根拠（2026/05/15 シート破損インシデント）:
//   旧 compact 方式は setValues + 末尾 deleteRows の2段階。末尾 deleteRows が稀に no-op
//   になる事象が観測され、setValues でシフトアップしたデータと未削除の末尾データが二重に
//   残ってシート破損を起こした。本関数は setValues を一切使わず、deleteRows のみで降順に
//   1レンジずつ削除し、各 deleteRows の直前/直後の getLastRow 差分を検証する。
//
// 降順削除の利点:
//   - 削除しても上流の行番号は不変（deleteSet の row 番号は実行中ずっと有効）
//   - 連続行は1回の deleteRows でまとめて削除（API 呼び出し数 = レンジ数）
//   - state.deleteRangeIndex のみで再開可能（compactReadRow/WriteRow が不要）
//
// レンジ情報の保存戦略:
//   レンジ配列は PS の state に保存しない（最悪 15KB+ になり 9KB/property 上限を超える）。
//   PS の削除行集合（OHLCV_DEDUP_ROWS_*）は処理完了まで不変なので、毎回 set → sort →
//   compress を再計算しても結果は同一。state には deleteRangeIndex（int）と進捗カウンタの
//   みを保存し、PS state サイズを最小化する。再計算コストは set 22KB ロード + ソート ~50ms。
//
// クラッシュセーフ性（pre-advance 方式）:
//   各レンジ削除の前に state.deleteRangeIndex をインクリメントして saveState する。
//   ハードkill が deleteRows と次の saveState の間で発生した場合:
//     - 通常の post-advance: 同じ index を再実行 → 既にシフトした行を「誤った行」として
//       削除する致命的バグになる（前回の compact-delete 破損と同型）
//     - pre-advance: 同じ index は再実行されない → 削除漏れの重複行が残るだけで破損しない
//   削除漏れは次回の post-repair cleanup で再検出・修復される。
//
// 安全弁:
//   - 削除直後の getLastRow 差分が期待値と一致しない場合は即 throw
//   - 破損が連鎖する前に処理停止 → スプシをバージョン履歴から復元できる
function bulkRangedDeleteOhlcvRows_(sheet, state, deadlineMs) {
  const { set } = loadAllOhlcvDedupDeleteRowSet_();
  if (set.size === 0) {
    clearOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_();
    const totalDeletedEmpty = state.actuallyDeleted || 0;
    state.rangedDeleteActive = false;
    state.deleteRangeIndex = null;
    state.actuallyDeleted = null;
    state.expectedDeletedRows = null;
    return { done: true, deletedRows: totalDeletedEmpty };
  }

  // 旧コンパクト方式の残骸（古い state から再開した場合）をクリア
  if (state.compactReadRow != null || state.compactWriteRow != null || state.compactRangeEnd != null) {
    state.compactReadRow = null;
    state.compactWriteRow = null;
    state.compactRangeEnd = null;
  }

  const sortedDesc = [...set].sort((a, b) => b - a);
  const ranges = compressConsecutiveDescending_(sortedDesc);

  if (!state.rangedDeleteActive) {
    state.rangedDeleteActive = true;
    state.deleteRangeIndex = 0;
    state.expectedDeletedRows = set.size;
    state.actuallyDeleted = 0;
    saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Ranged delete initialized.", {
      totalDeleteRows: set.size,
      rangeCount: ranges.length,
      minRow: ranges[ranges.length - 1].start,
      maxRow: ranges[0].start + ranges[0].len - 1
    });
  }

  while (state.deleteRangeIndex < ranges.length) {
    if (deadlineMs && Date.now() > deadlineMs - OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_RANGED_DELETE_MARGIN_MS) break;
    const range = ranges[state.deleteRangeIndex];

    // 【pre-advance】先に index を進めて saveState する。ハードkill 時に重複削除を防ぐ
    // （詳細は関数ヘッダコメント参照）
    state.deleteRangeIndex++;
    saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);

    const beforeLast = sheet.getLastRow();
    sheet.deleteRows(range.start, range.len);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    const afterLast = sheet.getLastRow();
    const observedDelta = beforeLast - afterLast;
    // 安全弁: deleteRows が機能しなかったら破損が広がる前に即停止
    if (observedDelta !== range.len) {
      throw new Error(
        "deleteRows verification failed. before=" + beforeLast +
        " after=" + afterLast + " expectedDelta=" + range.len +
        " observedDelta=" + observedDelta +
        " rangeStart=" + range.start +
        " rangeIndex=" + (state.deleteRangeIndex - 1) +
        " totalRanges=" + ranges.length
      );
    }
    state.actuallyDeleted = (state.actuallyDeleted || 0) + range.len;
    saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
  }

  if (state.deleteRangeIndex < ranges.length) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Ranged delete paused.", {
      deleteRangeIndex: state.deleteRangeIndex,
      totalRanges: ranges.length,
      actuallyDeleted: state.actuallyDeleted || 0,
      expectedDeletedRows: state.expectedDeletedRows || 0
    });
    return { done: false };
  }

  const totalDeleted = state.actuallyDeleted || 0;
  const expected = state.expectedDeletedRows || 0;
  clearOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_();
  state.rangedDeleteActive = false;
  state.deleteRangeIndex = null;
  state.actuallyDeleted = null;
  state.expectedDeletedRows = null;
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Ranged delete completed.", {
    deletedRows: totalDeleted,
    expectedDeletedRows: expected
  });
  return { done: true, deletedRows: totalDeleted };
}

// スキャンのみ実行（削除なし）。削除対象行番号の全リストを返す
function dedupeOhlcvAllTargetDates_(sheet, targetDates, deadlineMs) {
  const empty = { timedOut: false, allDeleteRows: [], duplicateRows: 0, scannedRows: 0, totalRows: 0 };
  const dateSet = {};
  (targetDates || []).forEach(d => { try { dateSet[normalizeBusinessDateKey_(d)] = true; } catch (e) {} });

  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return empty;

  // 時間不足なら即中断
  if (deadlineMs && deadlineMs - Date.now() < OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_MIN_DEDUP_REMAINING_MS) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Multi-date dedup skipped: insufficient time budget.", { remaining: deadlineMs - Date.now() });
    return { timedOut: true, allDeleteRows: [], duplicateRows: 0, scannedRows: 0, totalRows: 0 };
  }

  // 末尾OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_READ_ROWS行だけ読む。全行=1.78Mセルで6分超タイムアウト
  // getValues()は1回にまとめて A〜H（8列）を一括取得し API ラウンドトリップを削減
  const numDataRows = lastRow - 1;
  const readRows = Math.min(numDataRows, OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_READ_ROWS);
  const readFromRow = lastRow - readRows + 1;

  const t0 = Date.now();
  const keyRows = sheet.getRange(readFromRow, 1, readRows, 3).getValues();
  const volumeRows = sheet.getRange(readFromRow, 8, readRows, 1).getValues();
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Multi-date dedup values read.", {
    readRows: readRows,
    readFromRow: readFromRow,
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0
  });
  if (deadlineMs && deadlineMs - Date.now() < OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_TAIL_MARGIN_MS) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Multi-date dedup scan timed out after getValues().", {
      readRows: readRows, elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0
    });
    return { timedOut: true, allDeleteRows: [], duplicateRows: 0, scannedRows: 0, totalRows: numDataRows };
  }

  const bestByKey = {};
  const items = [];
  let scanned = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < readRows; i++) {
    if ((i % OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_TIMECHECK_EVERY) === 0 && i > 0) {
      if (deadlineMs && deadlineMs - Date.now() < OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_TAIL_MARGIN_MS) {
        writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Multi-date dedup scan timed out mid-loop.", {
          processedRows: i, totalReadRows: readRows, elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0
        });
        return { timedOut: true, allDeleteRows: [], duplicateRows: 0, scannedRows: i, totalRows: numDataRows };
      }
    }
    scanned = i + 1;

    // 高速プリフィルタ: テキスト形式 "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm" の先頭10文字で dateSet に照合し、
    // マッチしない行は normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_（regex+Date parse）を呼ばずにスキップ
    const rawTs = keyRows[i][0];
    let quickDate = null;
    if (rawTs instanceof Date) {
      const jstMs = rawTs.getTime() + 9 * 3600 * 1000;
      const d = new Date(jstMs);
      quickDate = d.getUTCFullYear() + "-" + String(d.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0");
    } else if (typeof rawTs === "string") {
      const prefix = rawTs.substring(0, 10).replace(/\//g, "-");
      if (prefix.length === 10 && prefix.charCodeAt(4) === 45) quickDate = prefix;
    }
    if (!quickDate || !dateSet[quickDate]) continue;

    // プリフィルタ通過行のみ正規解析
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(rawTs);
    if (!ts) continue;
    const dateStr = formatYmd_(ts);
    if (!dateSet[dateStr]) continue;
    const symbol = String(keyRows[i][2] || "").trim();
    if (!symbol) continue;
    const sheetRow = readFromRow + i;
    const key = formatOhlcvTimestampTextForSheet_(ts) + "|" + symbol;
    // compareOhlcvDuplicatePriority_ は row[1](alert_id) と row[7](volume) だけ使う
    const valueRow = [keyRows[i][0], keyRows[i][1], keyRows[i][2], null, null, null, null, volumeRows[i][0]];
    const item = { key: key, row: valueRow, sheetRow: sheetRow };
    items.push(item);
    const current = bestByKey[key];
    if (!current) { bestByKey[key] = item; continue; }
    const cmp = compareOhlcvDuplicatePriority_(valueRow, current.row);
    if (cmp < 0 || (cmp === 0 && sheetRow > current.sheetRow)) {
      bestByKey[key] = item;
    }
  }

  const allDeleteRows = [];
  const samples = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
    const item = items[i];
    if (isProtectedMiddayOhlcvMarker_(item.row[1])) continue;
    if (isPmLockedMarker_(item.row[1])) continue;
    const best = bestByKey[item.key];
    if (!best || item.sheetRow === best.sheetRow) continue;
    allDeleteRows.push(item.sheetRow);
    if (samples.length < 10) samples.push({ row: item.sheetRow, key: item.key, keptRow: best.sheetRow });
  }

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Multi-date dedup scan completed.", {
    targetDates: (targetDates || []).length,
    totalRows: numDataRows,
    scannedRows: readRows,
    readFromRow: readFromRow,
    matchedRows: items.length,
    duplicateRows: allDeleteRows.length,
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0,
    samples: samples
  });

  return { timedOut: false, totalRows: numDataRows, scannedRows: readRows, duplicateRows: allDeleteRows.length, allDeleteRows: allDeleteRows };
}

// EARLY_DEDUPステップ専用。DEDUP_DATESの削除完了後に呼ぶ。
// EARLY_DEDUPステップ専用。DEDUP_DATESの削除完了後に呼ぶ。
// 2フェーズで実装（PS クォータ問題対策: tail key set は PS に保存せず毎回メモリ内に構築）:
//   Phase 1 (TAIL_BUILD): tail を OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_EARLY_TAIL_CHUNK_ROWS 行ずつ読み、
//     key set をメモリ内の Set に構築する（PS への累積保存は行わない）。
//     デッドラインに達したら timedOut=true で返り、次回再実行で tail を最初から再構築する。
//   Phase 2 (EARLY_SCAN): メモリ内 key set で early ゾーンを 30k 行ずつスキャン。
//     タイムアウト時は部分結果を saveOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_ で PS に追記し cursor を保存。
//     次回再実行時は tail を再構築したうえで earlyDedupEarlyCursor の続きから再開。
// 呼び出し側は戻り値の allDeleteRows を使わず hasPendingOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_() で判断する。
//
// 【設計メモ】旧実装は tail key set を PS に累積保存していたが、82 対象日 × 40k tail 行 では
// 収集キー数が ~40k 件（~1MB）となり PS の 500KB 総クォータを超えてクラッシュした。
// 新実装では PS 保存を廃止し、tail 再読み込みコストと引き換えにクォータ問題を根本解決する。
//
// 【Codex レビュー回答】OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_READ_ROWS < QUICK_REPAIR_SCAN_MAX_ROWS の
// 点: quickRepair が GAP_REPAIR 行を "追記" する仕様上、これらは常にシート末尾に存在するため
// tailウィンドウのスキャンで確実に捕捉できる。90k は quickRepair が "スキャンする" 行数であり
// GAP_REPAIR 行が存在する行数ではない。
function dedupeOhlcvEarlyRows_(sheet, targetDates, state, deadlineMs) {
  const numDataRows = Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);
  if (numDataRows === 0) return { timedOut: false, totalRows: 0, duplicateRows: 0, allDeleteRows: [] };

  const readRows = Math.min(numDataRows, OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_DEDUP_READ_ROWS);
  const readFromRow = numDataRows - readRows + 2;
  if (readFromRow <= 2) {
    // シート全体が tail ウィンドウに収まる → early ゾーン不要
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Early-dedup skipped: entire sheet fits within tail scan window.", { numDataRows, readRows });
    clearEarlyDedupTailKeys_();  // 旧実装の残存データをクリア
    clearEarlyDedupTailCache_();
    return { timedOut: false, totalRows: numDataRows, duplicateRows: 0, allDeleteRows: [] };
  }

  const dateSet = {};
  for (const d of (targetDates || [])) dateSet[d] = true;

  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  const TAIL_CHUNK = OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_EARLY_TAIL_CHUNK_ROWS;

  // ── Phase 1a: 前回 resume で構築済みなら CacheService から再利用 ─────────────────
  // tail 再構築は 40k 行 × 3 列の getValues() を 4 回行うため通常 165〜230s、
  // GAS API スロットリング時は 335s 超ブロックして 6 分上限を消し尽くす。
  // 結果として early scan の cursor が一切進まないタイムアウトループに陥っていた。
  // 一度構築した tail key set は resume 間（lastRow が変わらない間）はそのまま使い回せる。
  let tailKeySet = loadEarlyDedupTailFromCache_(lastRow, readFromRow);
  if (tailKeySet && tailKeySet.size > 0) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Early-dedup tail key set reused from cache.", {
      cachedKeys: tailKeySet.size, readFromRow, lastRow
    });
  } else {
    // ── Phase 1b: tail key set をメモリ内に構築し、CacheService に保存 ─────────────
    // 旧 PS 残存データを起動時にクリアしておく（クォータ圧迫防止）
    clearEarlyDedupTailKeys_();
    clearEarlyDedupTailCache_();

    const t0 = Date.now();
    tailKeySet = new Set();

    console.log("[OHLCV_POST_REPAIR] Building early-dedup tail key set (in-memory, will cache for resume runs).", {
      readFromRow, lastRow, chunkSize: TAIL_CHUNK
    });

    let tailCursor = readFromRow;
    while (tailCursor <= lastRow) {
      // チャンク読み込み前のデッドラインチェック: early scan のために EARLY_SCAN_MIN_REMAINING_MS 確保
      if (deadlineMs && deadlineMs - Date.now() < OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_EARLY_SCAN_MIN_REMAINING_MS) {
        writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Early-dedup tail build timed out (pre-read). Will retry from scratch next run.", {
          tailCursor, lastRow, builtKeys: tailKeySet.size, elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0
        });
        // state.earlyDedupEarlyCursor はリセットしない（次回再実行で再構築 + 続きから再開）
        return { timedOut: true };
      }
      const cnt = Math.min(TAIL_CHUNK, lastRow - tailCursor + 1);
      const chunk = sheet.getRange(tailCursor, 1, cnt, 3).getValues();
      // getValues() 後のデッドラインチェック（API スロットリングで遅延した場合）
      if (deadlineMs && deadlineMs - Date.now() < OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_EARLY_SCAN_MIN_REMAINING_MS) {
        writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Early-dedup tail build timed out (post-read). Will retry from scratch next run.", {
          tailCursor, lastRow, builtKeys: tailKeySet.size, elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0
        });
        return { timedOut: true };
      }
      for (let i = 0; i < cnt; i++) {
        const rawTs = chunk[i][0];
        let qd = null;
        if (rawTs instanceof Date) {
          const d = new Date(rawTs.getTime() + 9 * 3600 * 1000);
          qd = d.getUTCFullYear() + "-" + String(d.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0");
        } else if (typeof rawTs === "string") {
          const p = rawTs.substring(0, 10).replace(/\//g, "-");
          if (p.length === 10 && p.charCodeAt(4) === 45) qd = p;
        }
        if (!qd || !dateSet[qd]) continue;
        const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(rawTs);
        if (!ts || !dateSet[formatYmd_(ts)]) continue;
        const symbol = String(chunk[i][2] || "").trim();
        if (!symbol) continue;
        tailKeySet.add(formatOhlcvTimestampTextForSheet_(ts) + "|" + symbol);
      }
      tailCursor += cnt;
    }

    const cached = saveEarlyDedupTailToCache_(tailKeySet, lastRow, readFromRow);
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Early-dedup tail key set built in memory.", {
      builtKeys: tailKeySet.size, elapsedMs: Date.now() - t0, cached: cached
    });

    if (tailKeySet.size === 0) {
      // tail に対象キーが存在しない → cross-boundary 重複なし
      clearEarlyDedupTailCache_();
      state.earlyDedupTailBuilt = false;
      state.earlyDedupTailCursor = null;
      state.earlyDedupEarlyCursor = null;
      return { timedOut: false, totalRows: numDataRows, duplicateRows: 0, allDeleteRows: [] };
    }
  }

  // ── Phase 2: early ゾーンをチャンクスキャン（30k 行・再開可能・部分結果を PS 累積保存）──
  if (deadlineMs && deadlineMs - Date.now() < 25000) {
    return { timedOut: true };
  }

  const earlyEnd = readFromRow - 1;
  const earlyRowsTotal = earlyEnd - 1; // rows 2..earlyEnd
  const CHUNK = OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_EARLY_SCAN_CHUNK_ROWS;
  const t1 = Date.now();

  if (!Number.isFinite(Number(state.earlyDedupEarlyCursor)) || state.earlyDedupEarlyCursor < 2) {
    state.earlyDedupEarlyCursor = 2;
  }
  console.log("[OHLCV_POST_REPAIR] Scanning early rows for cross-boundary duplicates.", {
    earlyRowsTotal, readFromRow, chunkSize: CHUNK, cursor: state.earlyDedupEarlyCursor, tailKeySetSize: tailKeySet.size
  });

  const allDeleteRows = [];
  const samples = [];
  let cursor = state.earlyDedupEarlyCursor;
  let scannedTotal = 0;

  while (cursor <= earlyEnd) {
    if (deadlineMs && deadlineMs - Date.now() < 25000) {
      // 部分結果をPSの既存セットに追記して、次回再開時に引き継ぐ
      if (allDeleteRows.length > 0) {
        const { set: existingSet } = loadAllOhlcvDedupDeleteRowSet_();
        saveOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_([...existingSet, ...allDeleteRows]);
      }
      state.earlyDedupEarlyCursor = cursor;
      // tail key set は次回再構築（PS 保存なし）
      state.earlyDedupTailBuilt = false;
      state.earlyDedupTailCursor = null;
      saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state);
      writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Early-dedup scan timed out between chunks. Cursor saved; tail will be rebuilt next run.", {
        scannedTotal, earlyRowsTotal, cursor, partialDuplicateRows: allDeleteRows.length, elapsedMs: Date.now() - t1
      });
      return { timedOut: true };
    }
    const count = Math.min(CHUNK, earlyEnd - cursor + 1);
    const chunkData = sheet.getRange(cursor, 1, count, 3).getValues();
    for (let j = 0; j < count; j++) {
      // 高速プリフィルタ
      const rawTsE = chunkData[j][0];
      let qdE = null;
      if (rawTsE instanceof Date) {
        const dE = new Date(rawTsE.getTime() + 9 * 3600 * 1000);
        qdE = dE.getUTCFullYear() + "-" + String(dE.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(dE.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0");
      } else if (typeof rawTsE === "string") {
        const pE = rawTsE.substring(0, 10).replace(/\//g, "-");
        if (pE.length === 10 && pE.charCodeAt(4) === 45) qdE = pE;
      }
      if (!qdE || !dateSet[qdE]) continue;
      const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(rawTsE);
      if (!ts || !dateSet[formatYmd_(ts)]) continue;
      const symbol = String(chunkData[j][2] || "").trim();
      if (!symbol) continue;
      const key = formatOhlcvTimestampTextForSheet_(ts) + "|" + symbol;
      if (!tailKeySet.has(key)) continue;
      const marker = String(chunkData[j][1] || "").trim();
      if (isProtectedMiddayOhlcvMarker_(marker)) continue;
      if (isPmLockedMarker_(marker)) continue;
      allDeleteRows.push(cursor + j);
      if (samples.length < 10) samples.push({ row: cursor + j, key });
    }
    scannedTotal += count;
    cursor += count;
  }

  // Phase 2 完了: 今回発見分を既存PSセットにマージして保存
  const { set: existingFinal } = loadAllOhlcvDedupDeleteRowSet_();
  const totalDeleteRows = allDeleteRows.length + existingFinal.size;
  if (allDeleteRows.length > 0 || existingFinal.size > 0) {
    saveOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_([...existingFinal, ...allDeleteRows]);
  }

  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Early-dedup scan completed.", {
    targetDates: (targetDates || []).length,
    totalRows: numDataRows,
    earlyRows: earlyRowsTotal,
    readFromRow,
    duplicateRows: totalDeleteRows,
    tailKeySetSize: tailKeySet.size,
    elapsedMs: Date.now() - t1,
    samples
  });

  // PSに削除行を保存済みなので呼び出し側は hasPendingOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_() で判断
  state.earlyDedupTailBuilt = false;
  state.earlyDedupTailCursor = null;
  state.earlyDedupEarlyCursor = null;
  // early scan が完走したのでキャッシュは破棄（次回の cleanup 起動時は別シート状態のため）
  clearEarlyDedupTailCache_();
  return { timedOut: false, totalRows: numDataRows, earlyRows: earlyRowsTotal, duplicateRows: totalDeleteRows, allDeleteRows: [] };
}

function tryOhlcvPostRepairSortOnce_(sheet) {
  const dataRows = Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);
  if (dataRows <= 1) return { attempted: false, skipped: true, reason: "not_enough_rows", dataRows: dataRows };
  if (dataRows > OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_SORT_MAX_ROWS) {
    writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Skipped full sort after repair because the sheet is too large; unsorted-safe scans remain enabled.", {
      dataRows: dataRows,
      maxRows: OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_SORT_MAX_ROWS
    });
    return { attempted: false, skipped: true, reason: "too_many_rows", dataRows: dataRows };
  }

  const result = sortOhlcvSheetByTimestampSafe_(sheet);
  result.attempted = true;
  return result;
}

function completeOhlcvPostRepairCleanup_(state) {
  const summary = {
    ok: true,
    complete: true,
    quickRepair: state.quickRepairSummary || {},
    cleanup: state.totals || {},
    businessDate: state.businessDate,
    targetDates: state.targetDates ? state.targetDates.length : 0
  };

  clearOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_();
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeQuickRepair");
  clearEarlyDedupTailCache_();
  clearManualOhlcvBusinessDate_();

summary.githubOptimize = triggerGitHubActionsOptimize_();

// post-repair cleanup完了後に、保留していたDiscord通知を送信する
summary.discordNotice = sendPendingOhlcvCompletionNoticeAfterQuickRepair_(summary);

writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Post-repair cleanup completed. GitHub Actions triggered. Discord notice sent after post-repair cleanup.", summary);
return summary;
}

function loadOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_() {
  try {
    const raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const state = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!state || Number(state.version) !== OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_VERSION) return null;
    if (!state.totals) state.totals = {};
    if (!Array.isArray(state.targetDates)) state.targetDates = [];
    return state;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

function saveOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_(state) {
  state.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
}

function scheduleSingleOhlcvPostRepairCleanupTrigger_(delayMs) {
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_HANDLER);
  ScriptApp.newTrigger(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_HANDLER)
    .timeBased()
    .after(delayMs)
    .create();
}

function clearOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_() {
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_(OHLCV_POST_REPAIR_CLEANUP_HANDLER);
}

function finalizeQuickRepairWithPostCleanup_(props, state, completeSummary) {
  props.deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_STATE_KEY);
  props.deleteProperty(QUICK_REPAIR_CLEANUP_STATE_KEY);
  deleteTriggersByHandler_("resumeQuickRepair");

  const scheduled = scheduleOhlcvPostRepairCleanup_(completeSummary);
  return {
    ok: true,
    incomplete: true,
    reason: "post_repair_cleanup_scheduled",
    quickRepairComplete: true,
    quickRepair: completeSummary,
    postRepairCleanup: scheduled
  };
}

function previewOhlcvPostRepairCleanup() {
  const app = getAppConfig_();
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(app.spreadsheetId);
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);
  const rawSh = ss.getSheetByName(CONFIG.RAW_SHEET);
  if (!sh) throw new Error("Missing sheet: " + CONFIG.OHLCV_SHEET);

  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  const startRow = 2;
  const businessDate = getOhlcvBusinessDateKey_();
  const rawMap = buildRawAlertVolumeMapForBusinessDate_(rawSh, businessDate).volumeMap;
  const values = startRow <= lastRow ? sh.getRange(startRow, 1, lastRow - startRow + 1, 8).getValues() : [];
  const duplicateMap = {};
  const targetDateSet = {};
  const out = {
    ok: true,
    dryRun: true,
    businessDate: businessDate,
    scannedRows: values.length,
    blankTimestampRows: 0,
    invalidTimestampRows: 0,
    normalizedTimestampCandidates: 0,
    protectedAmCandidates: 0,
    duplicateKeysInTail: 0,
    duplicateExtraRowsInTail: 0,
    targetDates: [],
    staleIntradaySymbols: Object.keys(readIntradayStaleSymbolMap_(businessDate)),
    samples: []
  };

  values.forEach((row, index) => {
    const rowNum = startRow + index;
    const ts = normalizeOhlcvTimestamp_(row[0]);
    const marker = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    const symbol = String(row[2] || "").trim();
    if (!row[0]) out.blankTimestampRows++;
    if (!ts || !isOhlcvSessionTimestamp_(ts)) {
      out.invalidTimestampRows++;
      if (out.samples.length < 10) out.samples.push({ row: rowNum, timestamp: String(row[0] || ""), symbol: symbol });
      return;
    }
    if (String(row[0] || "") !== formatOhlcvTimestampTextForSheet_(ts)) out.normalizedTimestampCandidates++;
    if (isPostRepairDuplicateTargetMarker_(marker)) targetDateSet[formatYmd_(ts)] = true;

    if (formatYmd_(ts) === businessDate && getOhlcvSessionName_(ts) === "AM" && marker === makeMiddayRefreshId_(businessDate)) {
      const rawVolume = rawMap[makeRawAlertVolumeKey_(ts, symbol)];
      if (rawVolume !== undefined && Math.round(toSafeVolumeNumber_(row[7])) === Math.round(Number(rawVolume))) {
        out.protectedAmCandidates++;
      }
    }

    if (symbol) {
      const key = formatOhlcvTimestampTextForSheet_(ts) + "|" + symbol;
      duplicateMap[key] = (duplicateMap[key] || 0) + 1;
    }
  });

  Object.keys(duplicateMap).forEach(key => {
    if (duplicateMap[key] <= 1) return;
    out.duplicateKeysInTail++;
    out.duplicateExtraRowsInTail += duplicateMap[key] - 1;
  });
  out.targetDates = normalizePostRepairTargetDates_(Object.keys(targetDateSet), businessDate);
  return out;
}

function startOhlcvPostRepairCleanupNow() {
  const scheduled = scheduleOhlcvPostRepairCleanup_({ manual: true, reason: "manual_start" });
  return { ok: true, scheduled: true, postRepairCleanup: scheduled };
}

function resetOhlcvPostRepairCleanupNow() {
  clearOhlcvPostRepairCleanupState_();
  clearOhlcvDedupDeleteRows_();
  clearEarlyDedupTailCache_();
  writeProcessLog_("OHLCV_POST_REPAIR", "Post-repair cleanup state and trigger were reset.", {});
  return { ok: true };
}
